# GATE RUSH — Implementation Contract (ARCHITECTURE.md)

Status: FINAL. This is the only communication between the engineers who implement the modules in parallel. `docs/GDD.md` holds every tuning number; this document holds every interface. If a module needs something that is not here, the owner of that module adds it to `js/data.js` or to its own module and documents it in a PR note; no module ever reaches into another module's internals. Three.js is `three@0.170.0` from cdn.jsdelivr.net via the import map in both shells; addons are imported from `three/addons/...`.

Reading order for an engineer: §1 (your files) → §2 (conventions) → §3 (ctx) → §4 (orders) → §5 (events) → your module's section in §6 → §7 (hit tests) → §8 (data shapes) → §14 (coding rules, definition of done).

---

Module dependency rule (acyclic, enforced by review): `util` ← `data` ← `levelgen` ← everything else. Gameplay modules import only `three`, `util.js`, `data.js` and their own file; they talk to other modules only through `ctx` (read) and `ctx.events` (write). `render.js` and `models.js` are imported by gameplay modules for their factories only. `ui.js`, `audio.js`, `fx.js` are consumers of events and never imported by gameplay modules.

---

## 2. Conventions

### 2.1 World

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Units | 1 unit = 1 m, y up, right-handed |
| Forward | −Z. The squad faces −Z. Everything on the road moves toward +Z at `ctx.world.roadSpeed` plus its own speed |
| Road | 8.0 m wide, x ∈ [−4, 4]; lane centres x = −2 / +2 (pair), 0 (single), −2.7 / 0 / +2.7 (triple); lane width 4.0 (pair), 8.0 (single), 2.6 (triple) |
| Squad | front row at z = 0, formation extends toward +Z; `ctx.world.squadX ∈ [−2.1, 2.1]`; formation half-width ≤ 1.8 m |
| Spawn / despawn | objects spawn at z = `WORLD.spawnZ = −70`; recycled when z > `WORLD.despawnZ = +14` |
| Gate window | armed when gate z ≥ −36; stops accepting hits at z > −1.5; pass when z ≥ 0 |
| Camera | owned by `render.js`; preset = portrait when `aspect < WORLD.camera.portraitBelow` (0.9), else landscape (tweened 300 ms). Portrait: pos (0.4·sx, 9.5, 11), look (0.6·sx, 1, −14); landscape: pos (0.4·sx, 10.5, 13), look (0.6·sx, 1, −16); near 0.5 far 160. The vertical FOV is derived so the horizontal FOV stays constant per preset: portrait hFov 27.05° (= 55° vertical on the 390×844 reference) → `fov = clamp(2·atan(tan(13.525°) / aspect), 45, 75)`; landscape hFov 68.6° (= 42° vertical at 16:9) → `fov = clamp(2·atan(tan(34.3°) / aspect), 36, 55)` (`WORLD.camera.hFovDeg`, `fovClamp`) |
| Road ring | 6 segments × 30 m; segment k covers z ∈ [−160 + 30k + scroll, …]; recycled by `render.js` |
| Layers | 0 default (everything), 1 = bloom-only helpers (unused by default), 2 = shadow casters tag (see §9.3) |

### 2.2 Time

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| `STEP` | 1/60 s fixed simulation step (`WORLD.step`) |
| Frame dt | `unscaledDt = min(clamp, (now − last)/1000)` with `clamp = clamp(WORLD.dtFollow (1.25) · EMA, WORLD.dtClamp (0.05), WORLD.dtClampMax (0.2))` s (round 7, perf-render:0); EMA = `engine.sustainedFrameMs`, the exponential mean (`WORLD.dtEmaRate` 0.1 per frame) of the rAF intervals that follow a frame whose step H drew (a paused or frozen-menu frame never pulls it down), never fed by an interval ≥ `WORLD.dtEmaCapMs` (1000); the clamp uses the EMA of the frames BEFORE the current one, so one 400 ms hitch on a 60 fps device still gives 50 ms (measured), while 5–20 fps play in real time (60 / 30 fps 0.05, 15 fps 0.083, 10 fps 0.125, 5 fps 0.2 s; below 5 fps a deliberate slow-down); `dt = unscaledDt · time.scale` |
| Accumulator | `acc += dt; while (acc ≥ STEP && n < WORLD.maxSubSteps (12)) { step(STEP); acc −= STEP; n++ }`; if n hits 12, `acc = 0` (drop time, never spiral: the loop could only diverge if one step cost ≥ 16.7 ms; measured 0.02–0.3 ms) (round 7: 12 = dtClampMax / STEP; was 5, i.e. game time = real time × fps / 20 below 20 fps) |
| `alpha` | `acc / STEP` after stepping; render code may extrapolate `z + vz·alpha·STEP` for scrolling objects (optional, recommended for zombies, gates, bullets) |
| Time scale | `time.scale` ∈ [`WORLD.timeScaleMin` = 0.25, 1]; set by `fx.slowmo(scale, seconds)` → `engine.setTimeScale`, which calls `ctx.audio.setTimeScale(scale)` on every change including the return to 1.0 (audio rate `MUSIC.rate.base + MUSIC.rate.perScale·scale` = 0.6 + 0.4·scale) |
| Clocks | `time.now` (ms, performance.now), `time.elapsed` (s, scaled, since boot), `time.levelTime` (s, scaled, since level start; reset by `engine.resetLevelClock()`), `time.frame` (integer), `time.refreshMs` (display refresh interval, §6.2), `time.theatre` (ms, real time that holds while paused — see Theatre timers); all valid from `Engine.init()` |
| Pausing | `state === 'paused'`: no steps; the rAF loop keeps running on every tier (§4.2): `fx.update(0, unscaledDt)` and `ui.update(unscaledDt)` run every frame (flashes, vignette, coin flights keep animating), `audio.update` runs every frame (music keeps playing, ducked −12 dB); the scene is redrawn at ≤ 30 fps on High — round 7: only when the run held ≥ 15 fps (`sustainedFrameMs` at `pause()` ≤ 2 × the 33 ms gate) and never on software GL — and only on `render.dirty` otherwise |
| Theatre timers | timers that span a slow-mo (boss death → `finish(true)`, game over → `finish(false)`, hint display, name card, boss intro choreography, the dolly / game-over camera) are real-time deadlines on `ctx.time.theatre` (round 2, CORE-7): `beginFrame` advances it by the clamped real interval (`unscaledDt · 1000`; round 7: real time down to 5 fps, so a 2.4 s boss intro no longer lasts 13–24 s on a 2–4 fps device) every frame except while `state === 'paused'` or the WebGL context is lost, so theatre spans slow-mo but not a pause; owners fall back to `time.now` while `time.theatre` is undefined. The slow-mo countdown (`time.slowmoLeft`) also holds while paused, and `engine.freezeQuality` deadlines are on `time.theatre`. Gameplay timers use the scaled step |

Every `step(dt)` receives the scaled fixed step; every `render(alpha)`/`update(dt)` receives the scaled frame dt unless the signature says `unscaledDt`.

---

## 3. The shared context `ctx`

Created by `main.js` as a plain object; every system is attached by main in boot order. Fields are never deleted or re-created after boot except where noted ("per run"). Since round-2 (cross-group) the `ctx` literal declares EVERY member below up front (`null` until its owner attaches it), so the systems attached later never add properties to it: the old one-by-one adds (the last nine through computed keys `ctx[name] = …`) pushed `ctx` past V8's fast-property limit for keyed stores into dictionary mode, which turned every `ctx.<system>` read into a hash lookup. Shared plain state objects (`ctx.time`, `ctx.input`, `ctx.world`, `ctx.run`, `ctx.debug.jsMs`) create their fraction-holding fields from a fraction and then set the start value (§14 rule 11).

| Field | Type | Created by | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ctx.version` | string | main | `'1.3.0'` (round-7 release; = `package.json` `version`; the title's `#version` shows it as "v1.3.0"; `sw.js` `VERSION` `'v8'`; 1.2.0 shipped with `'v4'`, its round-5b build with `'v5'`, 1.2.1 with `'v6'`, 1.2.2 with `'v7'`) |
| `ctx.config` | `{ debug:boolean, level:number\|null, seed:number\|null, quality:'low'\|'med'\|'high'\|null, nomusic:boolean, endless:boolean, reset:boolean, isArtifact:boolean }` | main | parsed from URL params; `isArtifact = document.getElementById('app')?.dataset.shell === 'artifact'` (both shells put `data-shell` on `#app`; the artifact shell has no `<html>` of its own, so nothing ever reads `documentElement.dataset`) |
| `ctx.app` | HTMLElement | main (`#app`) | root element: input listeners (§6.2), size source (§9.5), `tabindex="-1"` focus target |
| `ctx.canvas` | HTMLCanvasElement | main (`#gl-canvas`) | render creates the WebGL 2 context on it |
| `ctx.events` | `EventBus` | main | §5 |
| `ctx.save` | `SaveStore` | main | §6.15, schema §12 |
| `ctx.profile` | `{ startSquad:number, fireRate:number, damage:number, coinMult:number, bossDamage:number, ranks:{squad,fireRate,damage,coinMult,bossDamage} }` | save (`save.profile()`) | written by save (`load`, `setUpgrade`, `reset` reassign `ctx.profile = this.profile()`) and by main at boot; read-only for others |
| `ctx.ui` | `UI` | main | §6.14, DOM contract §10 |
| `ctx.render` | `RenderSystem` | main | `.renderer .scene .camera .composer .textures .materials .env .uniforms.uTime .size {w,h,dpr,portrait} .dirty` |
| `ctx.models` | `ModelLibrary` | main | geometry factories and caches |
| `ctx.audio` | `AudioSystem` | main | §6.13, cue contract §11 |
| `ctx.engine` | `Engine` | main | loop; owns `ctx.time`, `ctx.state`, `ctx.input`; owns `ctx.quality` after render created it |
| `ctx.time` | `{ now, unscaledDt, dt, step:1/60, alpha, scale, elapsed, levelTime, frame, slowmoLeft, theatre, refreshMs }` | engine | numbers; `step` constant; every field initialised in `Engine.init()` (`now = theatre = performance.now()`, `frame = 0`, `scale = 1`, the rest 0; `refreshMs` 16.67 until measured) — valid before the first frame; `theatre` (ms, CORE-7) advances by the clamped real frame interval (round 7: real time down to 5 fps) except while paused / context-lost (§2.2) |
| `ctx.state` | `StateMachine` | engine | states: `boot`, `title`, `levels`, `shop`, `playing`, `paused`, `complete`, `gameover`; `settings` is a UI overlay, not a state; state → screen mapping in §6.2 |
| `ctx.input` | `{ steerDx:number, targetX:number, pointerDown:boolean, keyLeft:boolean, keyRight:boolean, gamepadX:number, hasSteered:boolean, locked:boolean }` | engine | `steerDx` = CSS px, sum of `clientX` deltas of coalesced pointer events since the last `beginFrame`, positive = right, zeroed after integration; `targetX` (m, ∈ ±`WORLD.steerRange`) is integrated ONCE per frame by `engine.beginFrame` (§6.2) and only read by squad; `locked = true` during the boss intro; `hasSteered` is set by an accepted drag delta and — round 7 — by keyboard or gamepad steering while playing and unlocked |
| `ctx.quality` | `{ tier:'low'\|'med'\|'high', initialTier, maxAutoTier, auto:boolean, params:QUALITY_TIERS[tier], dpr:number, frozenUntil:number, renderScale:number, software:boolean }` | render (`init()`, via `detectTier` / `isSoftwareGpu`), owned/written afterwards by engine | `params` is the live tier object; modules read it on `quality:change` and at init; every pool is allocated at the High capacity regardless of tier (§14 rule 3). Round 7: `software` = software GL (§9.4); `renderScale` = the Auto render-scale rung (1 = off, §9.5; r7 final fix: software GL only, always 1 on a hardware GPU), written by engine, applied by `render.setRenderScale`; `dpr` = the applied backing ratio (tier DPR × renderScale, may be < 1) |
| `ctx.rng` | `Rng` | main (boot, `new Rng(1)`); replaced per run by level | gameplay RNG seeded with the run seed; ALL gameplay randomness uses it; the boot instance serves the title idle scene |
| `ctx.rngFx` | `Rng` | main (boot, `ctx.rng.fork(7)`); replaced per run by level | cosmetic RNG (particles, prop jitter) seeded `seed + 7`, so cosmetics never perturb gameplay |
| `ctx.world` | `{ roadSpeed:number, roadSpeedBase:number, distance:number, squadX:number, squadHalfWidth:number, squadDepth:number, squadCount:number, invulnerable:boolean, bossActive:boolean, bossZ:number, bossX:number }` | level (`roadSpeed`, `roadSpeedBase`, `distance`), squad (`squadX`, `squadHalfWidth`, `squadDepth`, `squadCount`, `invulnerable`), boss (`bossActive`, `bossZ`, `bossX`, mirrored every `boss.step()`; cleared by `boss.reset()`) | the hot shared state, written only by the named owner, fields overwritten in place (never re-created). `bossActive = true` from `boss:spawn` until `boss:dead`, intro included: weapons re-aim and fire at the boss during the intro too |
| `ctx.run` | `RunStats` object (§6.16) | level (per run) | coins, kills, histograms, stars |
| `ctx.fx` | `Fx` | main | §6.12 |
| `ctx.squad` `ctx.zombies` `ctx.boss` `ctx.gates` `ctx.pickups` `ctx.weapons` `ctx.troops` `ctx.level` | systems | main | §6 |
| `ctx.debug` | `{ enabled:boolean, calls:number, jsMs:number, dropped:Record<string, number>, histograms:{...}, shotsPerSec:number, laserBeams:number, levelGenMs:number, levelGenHits:number, levelGenMisses:number }` | engine (round-3 polish: every field declared in `Engine.init()`, `levelGenMs` created from a fraction, §14 rule 11) | filled only when `config.debug`, except the overlay counters weapons (`shotsPerSec`: −1 until the first 1 s window — the overlay prints "-" —, `laserBeams`) and level (`levelGen*`, LVL-18) write in place; `dropped` stays a by-name counter record |

Access rule: a module may READ any `ctx` field; it may WRITE only the fields this table assigns to it. Cross-module commands go through method calls on the owning system (e.g. `ctx.squad.addSoldiers(n, origin)`), never by poking state.

---

## 4. Boot order and frame order

### 4.1 Boot (`js/main.js`, async)

```
 0. install window.onerror + unhandledrejection → showFatal(err)   (top of main.js, before any await; replaces the shells' inline classic handler, §15)
 1. ctx = { version, config: parseParams(location.search), app: document.getElementById('app'), canvas: document.getElementById('gl-canvas'),
            rng: null, rngFx: null, world: null, …, level: null }   // every §3 member declared up front (round-2 cross-group: no dictionary-mode ctx)
    ctx.config.isArtifact = ctx.app.dataset.shell === 'artifact'   (read after #app is resolved)
    ctx.rng = new Rng(1);  ctx.rngFx = ctx.rng.fork(7)              (boot RNGs for the title idle scene; level replaces both per run)
 2. ctx.events  = new EventBus()
 3. ctx.save    = new SaveStore(ctx);  ctx.save.load();  ctx.profile = ctx.save.profile()
 4. ctx.ui      = new UI(ctx);         ctx.ui.init()            // caches DOM, shows #scr-boot, applies settings to <html> classes, ui.refit() writes --ui
 5. ctx.render  = new RenderSystem(ctx); await ctx.render.init() // (a) probes canvas.getContext('webgl2', { failIfMajorPerformanceCaveat: true }), then on a fresh canvas with false (round 7: only the second works → software GL, §9.4) → null throws new Error(STRINGS.noWebGL2) which showFatal renders with RELOAD;
                                                                 // (b) creates the WebGLRenderer (antialias decided once from the initial tier, §9.4; round 7: never on software GL); (c) reads WEBGL_debug_renderer_info guarded;
                                                                 // (d) ctx.quality = { tier, initialTier: tier, maxAutoTier: maxAutoTierFor(gpuString, tier), auto: ctx.config.quality == null, params: QUALITY_TIERS[tier], dpr: 1, frozenUntil: 0, renderScale, software } with tier = detectTier(gpuString, deviceMemory, config.quality) (§6.2); round 7: software GL without ?quality= → tier / maxAutoTier 'low', renderScale QUALITY_TIERS.renderScale.softwareStart (0.7), else 1;
                                                                 // (e) only then camera + lights → TextureFactory.build (awaits fonts ≤ RENDER.textures.fontWaitMs, 0.5 s since round 7) → MaterialLibrary → applyQuality(params) (composer, shadows, fog, anisotropy) → Environment.build; reports progress via ctx.ui.setBootProgress(0..1)
                                                                 // round-3 boot polish: init() paints a frame (rAF → macrotask) after the context creation, after the textures, after materials + passes + applyQuality and after Environment.build; TextureFactory.build paints between its steps once they ran > 16 ms since the last paint
 6. ctx.models  = new ModelLibrary(ctx); ctx.models.init()      // all geometries, limb-swing materials; asserts the vertex-attribute budget (§6.4); then a yielded frame (round 3)
 7. ctx.audio   = new AudioSystem(ctx); ctx.audio.init()        // builds the AudioContext + the whole graph ('suspended' until a gesture; round-2 AUD-8 — up to ~1 s on some Windows Chrome builds), registers no listener; the unlock gesture is owned by engine (§6.2) and unlock() only resumes
 8. ctx.engine  = new Engine(ctx);     ctx.engine.init()        // time fields, state (boot), input + unlock listeners on #app, adopts the existing ctx.quality object (never re-creates it; round 7: a manual tier — saved or ?quality= — sets renderScale 1; r7 fix: on software GL with no ?quality=, the first boot of this build turns a saved 'low' into 'auto' once — save.setSetting('quality', 'auto'), save hint 'quality.swAuto' marked on that boot whatever the setting, a diag line — so a Low chosen afterwards stays), starts the auto-scaler, installs the diagnostics log (window.__gateRushDiag, §13), emits quality:change {reason:'init'}, resize → ctx.render.resize()
 9. ctx.fx = new Fx(ctx).init();  ctx.squad = new Squad(ctx).init();  ctx.zombies = new Zombies(ctx).init();
    ctx.boss = new BossSystem(ctx).init();  ctx.gates = new Gates(ctx).init();  ctx.pickups = new Pickups(ctx).init();
    ctx.weapons = new Weapons(ctx).init();  ctx.troops = new Troops(ctx).init();  ctx.level = new LevelRunner(ctx).init()
    // round-3 verify: after a system's init(), while it reports `initSteps > 0` main yields a painted frame and calls `initStep()` (one slice of deferred init
    // work per frame, at most the count read after init; the bar advances by the slice's share of the system's weight): the boss rigs one per frame, then the
    // level generator's JIT warm-up
 9b. await warmRender(ctx.render)                                // render.warmup(onProgress) (§6.3), mapped onto the bar's warm-up range with a bounded creep between reports; capped at WARMUP_TIMEOUT_MS; then a yielded frame (round 3: the warm-up's last draw and the prewarm's first surface never share a frame)
 9c. await ctx.ui.prewarm(onProgress)                            // round-2 UI-19 / CORE-9: first layout + raster of every first-use DOM surface behind #scr-boot — round 3: one surface per frame, progress mapped onto the bar; capped at 5 s (ui holds #scr-boot until it ends anyway)
10. window.__game = ctx;  first scene: if (ctx.config.endless) ctx.level.startEndless(ctx.config.seed) else if (ctx.config.level) ctx.level.start(ctx.config.level, ctx.config.seed) else ctx.level.quitToTitle()   // quitToTitle = the one entry into the title idle scene (resets systems, state 'title', idle camera, menu music); round 3: still under #scr-boot, in a task of its own, then setBootProgress(1) and a yielded frame
11. ctx.ui.hideBoot()                                           // the boot screen leaves in the first loop frame (ui applies the first state screen); hideBoot also waits for (or runs) the prewarm when main did not await it
12. ctx.engine.start()                                          // requestAnimationFrame loop
13. ctx.engine.armBackGuard()                                   // round 6 (guarded): popstate back guard, §6.2 "Back guard"; pushes nothing on the title
```

Every `init()` returns `this`. `init()` may create THREE objects and add them to `ctx.render.scene`; it must not depend on any system initialised after it. Steps 5–10 call `ctx.ui.setBootProgress(fraction)` with the weights of `main.js` `PROGRESS` — round-3 boot polish: each step's measured share of the boot (first bar → title) at 4× CPU throttling, 390 × 844 @ 2.75 (`docs/build/r3-boot-polish.md`): page 0–4 %, render.init 5–22 %, models 22–25.5 %, audio 25.5–29 %, engine 29–30 %, systems 30–42 % (each system advances the bar by its measured init cost, `SYSTEMS`), warm-up 42–70 %, UI prewarm 70–96 %, first scene 96–100 % — and main calls `ctx.ui.setBootCaption(i)` (guarded) before each real step (`STRINGS.boot.steps`: page, textures, models, audio, systems, shaders, and the UI prewarm; UI-29). Every boot step yields a painted frame (rAF → macrotask) before the next one; step 9 yields before each init once the inits since the last paint exceed 16 ms (`SYSTEMS_SLICE_MS`) and always before an init measured ≥ 100 ms at 4× (`SYSTEMS_ALONE_MS`), and runs each system's deferred init slices one per painted frame (round-3 verify: the boss rigs, ≈ 45–60 ms each at 4×, were one 222–299 ms frozen frame; the level generator's JIT warm-up). Acceptance (VIS-17 / CORE-9): no painted boot gap > 300 ms under 4× CPU throttling. Every pool is allocated in `init()` at its High-tier capacity (§14 rule 3).

### 4.2 Frame (`Engine.frame(now)`), in this exact order

```
A. engine.beginFrame(now)        // renderer.info.reset(); time.unscaledDt (round 7: clamp follows the sustained frame time, §2.2), time.dt, slow-mo countdown; steering integration ONCE per frame:
                                 //   input.targetX = clamp(input.targetX + input.steerDx·getSteerK() + (keyboard|gamepad velocity)·unscaledDt, −WORLD.steerRange, +WORLD.steerRange); input.steerDx = 0
                                 //   (round 7 keyboard: ±SQUAD.steer.keyboardSpeed (12 = maxSpeed) with no ramp, a press edge first moves targetX to squadX ± maxSpeed/smoothRate (never backward), a release sets targetX = squadX — r7 fix: so does every other end of a held key (_kbRelease: pause, blur, a hidden tab, lockInput, any non-playing frame), which used to zero the direction with the target still KB_LEAD ahead (a 0.9 m slide after the resume) — last pressed wins, a tap latched since the last simulation step keeps its direction until one step has run — r7 fix round 2: every press steers ≥ 1 step (0.2 m), step B clears the latch; it lasted one rendered frame, so a frame that ran no step (above 60 Hz, slow-mo) lost the tap; gamepad: gamepadX·SQUAD.steer.gamepadSpeed (9); integration runs only while state.is('playing') and !input.locked — in every other state steerDx is discarded and targetX stays 0)
B. if (state.is('playing')) {
     acc += time.dt; n = 0
     while (acc >= STEP && n < WORLD.maxSubSteps) {   // 12 (round 7)
       level.step(STEP)          // distance, roadSpeed lerp, script events (spawns), run-up, boss hand-off, E(z)
       squad.step(STEP)          // steering, commit lock, count/formation targets, invulnerability, armor
       troops.step(STEP)         // troop timers and attacks (uses zombies queries)
       weapons.step(STEP)        // spawn bullets, move, swept hit tests (zombies → gates → crates), damage, gate hits, power-up timers
       zombies.step(STEP)        // scroll + AI, contact/bites, globs, deaths, z-bins rebuild
       boss.step(STEP)           // boss movement, attacks, phases, safety valve
       gates.step(STEP)          // scroll, arm, commit, pass → squad.applyGate
       pickups.step(STEP)        // scroll, open, collect, magnet
       acc -= STEP; n++
     }
     if (n === WORLD.maxSubSteps) acc = 0
     time.alpha = acc / STEP
     if (n > 0) engine._kbTap = 0  // r7 fix round 2: the keyboard tap latch (A) is cleared once a fixed step has used it
   }
C. fx.update(time.dt, time.unscaledDt)                 // particles time, floaters, shake, flashes, slow-mo
D. squad.render(alpha); zombies.render(alpha); boss.render(alpha); gates.render(alpha);
   pickups.render(alpha); weapons.render(alpha); troops.render(alpha)   // instance matrix writes only
E. render.update(time.dt)        // camera follow + shake + dolly + FOV kick, environment scroll, lights follow, uTime
F. ui.update(time.unscaledDt)    // HUD DOM only (bars/coins at 10 Hz, dirty bits set by listeners); the count label is NOT DOM (fx owns it)
G. audio.update(time.unscaledDt) // queued cues started with AudioContext scheduling, music scheduler, voice counters reset
H. render.draw()                 // composer.render() or renderer.render(); clears render.dirty
I. engine.endFrame()             // quality sampler, debug overlay (renderer.info.render.calls = shadow + main + post), frame++
```

In `title`, `levels`, `shop`, `complete`, `gameover` the loop runs A, C, D, E, F, G, H, I — except a lite menu (round 7, flow-coins:1): when `levels` / `shop` / `complete` / `gameover` is entered from a non-menu state on a slow device (decided once at that entry: `quality.software` or `sustainedFrameMs` > `QUALITY_TIERS.menuLiteFrameMs` (40)), D, E and H stop `QUALITY_TIERS.menuSettleMs` (1000) after the entry — the boss collapse, confetti and game-over dolly finish first — and from then on run only for a `render.dirty` request (r7 fix: the decision is taken after step B, so the frame whose step opened the results is already a menu frame; the settle is measured on the rAF clock, `now − _menuSince`, not the clamped theatre clock — 0.55 s software frames stretched it to 3.3 s; and it is 0 when `sustainedFrameMs` > `QUALITY_TIERS.menuSettleMaxFrameMs` (250): those few frames showed no motion and held the results' count and clicks), ONE frame after it (`_menuDrawDue`), so the DOM screen change paints before the slow WebGL frame; menu → menu moves keep the decision. Fast devices and `title` always draw (the title's idle scene animates). In `title` the engine calls `level.idleStep(STEP)` inside the same accumulator loop in place of B; `idleStep` itself calls `squad.step(STEP)` and `gates.step(STEP)` only, with `ctx.world.roadSpeed = WORLD.idleRoadSpeed` (6), `input.targetX` held at 0 (no steering integration outside `playing`), no `weapons.step` (no bullets), no `pickups.step` (no coins, so nothing is ever credited in the title); `gates.step` skips `lockLane`/`applyGate`/all events for decorative gates (§6.9). In `complete`/`gameover` D runs (boss collapse, coin fountain, dissolves keep rendering) but no step runs. In `paused` the loop keeps running on every tier: A, C (`fx.update(0, unscaledDt)`), F, G every frame; H only on High (≤ 30 fps via a 33 ms gate; round 7: only when the run held ≥ 15 fps and not on software GL, perf-render:9) or when `render.dirty` is set (by `resize`, `webglcontextrestored`, `quality:change`, `ui.showScreen`, `engine.requestRedraw()`), in which case H runs once and clears the flag. The music scheduler additionally runs from a `setInterval(25)` started when the AudioContext first reports 'running' (round-2 AUD-8; the context itself is built at boot step 7) so it never depends on rAF (covers backgrounded tabs where rAF is throttled).

---

## 5. Event bus

`js/engine.js` exports `class EventBus`:

```js
on(name, fn)            // → unsubscribe function; fn(payload) called synchronously in subscription order
once(name, fn)          // → unsubscribe function
off(name, fn)
emit(name, payload)     // synchronous; payload may be a reused scratch object: listeners MUST NOT retain it
```

Rules: no event is emitted from inside a listener of the same event (depth guard 8 throws in debug); emitters reuse module-level payload objects (zero garbage); listener errors are caught, reported once to `ui.showError` in debug and swallowed otherwise. `gate`, `zombie` and `boss` payloads expose read-only views (plain objects with `id`, positions and state), never the pool entries. Listeners that act over several frames copy the fields they need on receipt (`ui` copies `level:complete`, `level:failed`, `endless:end`, `boss:spawn` into its own fields; `fx` copies positions into its pools). Exception: the `data` object passed to `state.set('complete'|'gameover', data)` is a fresh object created by `level.finish()` (once per run, not per frame). Events are emitted synchronously from inside the fixed-step loop (up to 5 steps per frame): `ui` listeners only store values and set a dirty bit, all DOM writes happen in `ui.update()` (step F); `audio` listeners only queue cues, started in `audio.update()` (step G) with `AudioContext.currentTime` scheduling.

### 5.1 Complete event list (name → payload shape)

| Event | Emitter | Payload |
|---|---|---|
| `state:change` | engine | `{ from, to, data }` |
| `resize` | engine | `{ w, h, dpr, portrait }` (CSS px) |
| `quality:change` | engine | `{ tier, params, reason:'init'\|'auto'\|'manual' }` |
| `quality:scale` | engine | `{ scale, reason:'auto'\|'manual', meanInt }` (round 7; r7 final fix: the 'nogain' / 'busy' undo reasons went with the hardware-GPU gain check — the rungs run on software GL only) — the render scale changed (§9.5; `meanInt` = the scaler's mean frame interval in ms, 0 when not measured); reused payload; no game module listens (harnesses, diagnostics) |
| `pause` / `resume` | engine | `{}` |
| `audio:unlocked` | audio | `{}` |
| `settings:change` | ui | `{ key, value }` |
| `save:change` | save | `{ reason }` |
| `error` | any | `{ message, fatal }` |
| `level:start` | level | `{ level, seed, endless, script, district }` (round 5: `district` = the run's district id, docs/DISTRICTS.md) |
| `district:change` | level | `{ id, name, index, label, accent, seconds, reason:'start'\|'rotate'\|'title', banner, first, step, steps }` (round 5) — after `level:start` at a run start (seconds 0), on an endless rotation (seconds `DISTRICT_RULES.fadeSeconds`), at `quitToTitle` (banner false); ui shows the district banner when `banner` is true; reused payload. Round 7 (explain:4): `banner` is true at EVERY run start (retries included) and every rotation; `first` = the big "NEW DISTRICT n" card (a district's first level when the previous run this session was another level, an endless start, an endless rotation); `step` / `steps` = the campaign level's place in its district (k / `DISTRICT_RULES.levelsPerDistrict`; 0 in endless and at the title) — ui prints "NAME · k/5" or "NAME · FINALE" |
| `level:progress` | level (10 Hz) | `{ distance, length, fraction }` |
| `level:runup` | level | `{ secondsToBoss }` |
| `level:complete` | level | `{ level, stars, survivors, eBoss, coins, breakdown:{ kills, gates, piles, boss, bonus, bonusStars, bonusSurvivors } }` — round 7 (explain:7): `bonusStars` + `bonusSurvivors` = `bonus` (the level bonus's star and survivor parts, as credited); `piles` also holds duplicates and refunds |
| `level:failed` | level | `{ level, fraction, coinsKept }` (campaign only; an endless run ends with `endless:end`) |
| `endless:chunk` | level | `{ index, difficulty }` |
| `endless:end` | level | `{ distance, score, best, coins, chunks }` |
| `hint:show` | level | `{ id, text, hold, until, priority, event }` — round 7 (teaching:0 / :3): `hold` = theatre s the hint may not be cut by a waiting one (0 = none); `until` = release key (a gate event id, `'crate'`, or r7 fix round 3 a weapon id; `null` = none) — the hint stays its hold (at most `JUICE.ui.hintHoldMaxSeconds`) unless that key's `hint:release` comes first; `priority` = a boss attack's lesson (queued first; it cuts a normal hint on screen to `JUICE.ui.hintCutSeconds`, and the cut one comes back first unless it had already been read); `event` = the gate event whose gates pulse while it is up (−1 none); reused payload |
| `hint:release` (round 7) | level | `{ key }` — the subject of a held hint is resolved (a lane of its gate event passed or missed; a crate opened, an item was picked up or — r7 fix round 3 — no closed crate is left ahead, `pickups.hasClosedCrateAhead()` polled in `step` while the crate hint is out: `'crate'`; r7 fix round 3: `weapon:end` → the weapon's id, its name hint); ui takes that hint down, or drops it from the queue unshown (unmarked; one taken down before `JUICE.ui.hintMarkSeconds` on screen stays unmarked too) |
| `codex:new` (round 7) | level | `{ id }` — a FIELD GUIDE entry with a NEW card met for the first time ever (`CODEX.cards`: a purple item lane at its spawn — before the choice —, a crate's item at its pickup, the first MAX gate, the first grey ×1); ui queues the card (`#newcard`). Every entry met, card or not, is marked `'seen:<id>'` in `save.hints` by `level._codexSeen` |
| `gate:event` | gates | `{ eventId, layout, z, hint }` once per spawned event, before its per-lane `gate:spawn` emits |
| `gate:spawn` | gates | `{ gate, eventId, laneIndex, laneCount }` one emit per lane, in lane order, same step; gate view = `{ id, op:'+'\|'-'\|'x'\|'/'\|'item', value, base, mult, x, z, lane, layout, state:'far'\|'armed'\|'locked'\|'passed'\|'missed', maxed, flipped, item }` |
| `gate:hit` | gates | `{ gate, value, prev, weight, x, y, z }` |
| `gate:max` | gates | `{ gate }` |
| `gate:flip` | gates | `{ gate }` (−N crossed 0, or ÷ ladder reached x2) |
| `gate:step` | gates | `{ gate, mult }` |
| `gate:commit` | gates | `{ gate, lane }` |
| `gate:pass` | gates | `{ gate, op:'+'\|'-'\|'x'\|'/'\|'item', value, before, after, x, z, other:{ op, value, wouldBe }\|null }` — `other` describes the best lane NOT taken (`wouldBe` = the count it would have produced from `before`), `null` for singles |
| `gate:miss` | gates | `{ gate }` — also emitted for EVERY lane of an event that reaches the squad line after `squad:dead` (C = 0 or level phase `'lost'`), with no `gate:pass`, no gate coins and no item grant (round-2 GAT-6) |
| `squad:count` | squad | `{ count, prev, delta, reason:'start'\|'gate'\|'bite'\|'spit'\|'slam'\|'chew'\|'medic'\|'charge'\|'stomp'\|'car'\|'debug' }` |
| `squad:spawn` | squad | `{ count, x, z }` visual burst origin |
| `squad:loss` | squad | `{ count, x, z, cause }` one per soldier death; `cause` ∈ `squad:count.reason` minus `'start'`, `'gate'`, `'medic'`, `'debug'` (never emitted for gate losses) |
| `squad:dead` | squad | `{ distance, fraction }` |
| `squad:invuln` | squad | `{ on }` |
| `armor:change` | squad | `{ plates, delta }` |
| `shot` | weapons (per step, coalesced) | `{ count, x, z, weapon }` |
| `weapon:stored` (round 6) | weapons (`storeWeapon`, from `pickups.grantItem`) | `{ id, slots }` — a weapon pickup went into the FIFO weapon slot (`slots` = stored count after it, 1..`PICKUPS.weaponSlots`); emitted before that pickup's `item:pickup`. Not emitted when the slot is full (that pickup pays `coin:pickup { source:'refund' }` and reports `duplicate`), nor when the slot is emptied (FIRE → `weapon:start`; level end; `reset`) — ui reads `weapons.storedCount` |
| `weapon:start` / `weapon:end` | weapons | `{ id, duration }` / `{ id }` — round 6: `weapon:start` is the ACTIVATION (the player's FIRE started a stored weapon with its full `WEAPONS.*.duration`; fx J13 edge glow, audio sting, ui pill), never the pickup |
| `weapon:fire` | weapons / troops | `{ id, x, z }` (`'weapon.rocket'`, `'weapon.railgun'`, `'troop.sniper'` → cues `rocket` / `rail` / `crack`) |
| `gear:start` / `gear:end` | weapons | `{ id, duration }` / `{ id }`; `duration = Infinity` for `gear.armor` and `gear.magnet` (ui shows no ring/time for Infinity) |
| `explosion` | weapons / troops | `{ x, z, radius, kind:'rocket'\|'apc' }` |
| `zombie:spawn` | zombies | `{ id, type, x, z, hp }` |
| `zombie:hit` | zombies | `{ id, type, x, y, z, damage, hp, maxHp, killed }` |
| `zombie:die` | zombies | `{ id, type, x, z, cause:'bullet'\|'bite'\|'crush'\|'burn'\|'explosion'\|'cleanup' }` (no coin field: income travels only on `coin:pickup`) |
| `zombie:bite` | zombies | `{ id, type, x, z, kills }` |
| `zombie:telegraph` | zombies | `{ id, kind:'ring'\|'spit'\|'screech'\|'charge'\|'lunge', x, z, radius, duration }` |
| `zombie:stagger` | zombies | `{ id, x, z }` |
| `spit:impact` | zombies / boss | `{ x, z, radius, kills }` |
| `boss:spawn` | boss | `{ id, name, hp, maxHp, archetype:keyof BOSSES.archetypes, twins:boolean, mini:boolean }` (one emit for a twin pair; `hp`/`maxHp` are the pair's sums) |
| `boss:intro` | boss | `{ id, name, duration }` |
| `boss:fight` | boss | `{ id }` |
| `boss:hp` | boss | `{ hp, maxHp, fraction }` |
| `boss:phase` | boss | `{ phase, fraction }` |
| `boss:attack` | boss | `{ kind:'slam'\|'charge'\|'spit3'\|'rain'\|'stomp'\|'lane'\|'car'\|'chew', x, z, radius, delay, lane }` |
| `boss:hit` | boss | `{ damage, x, y, z, crit }` |
| `boss:shield` | boss | `{ hits, needed, up, x, z }` emitted on every change (hit, raise, drop) |
| `boss:dead` | boss | `{ id, x, z }` (once per pair for twins; no coin field) |
| `crate:open` | pickups | `{ item, x, z }` |
| `item:pickup` | pickups (`grantItem`, also when called by gates for an item lane) | `{ item, x, z, duplicate }` (`item` = data id `'weapon.shotgun'`, `'gear.armor'`, `'troop.medic'`, `'coins'`; `duplicate` = a 25 c payout happened instead of a new effect) |
| `coin:pickup` | zombies (`kill`), pickups (`pile`, `crate`, `duplicate`), gates (`gate`, `max`), boss (`boss`), weapons (`refund`), level (`bonus`) | `{ amount, x, y, z, source:'kill'\|'pile'\|'crate'\|'gate'\|'max'\|'boss'\|'duplicate'\|'refund'\|'bonus' }` — `amount` is the base value, pre-multiplier. THE ONLY income path: every credit of coins is exactly one `coin:pickup` emitted by the system that earned it; level is the only listener that accounts it (§6.16) |
| `coins:change` | level | `{ total, runTotal, delta, source }` — `total = ctx.save.data.coins + run.coins` (what the HUD shows), `delta` after multipliers |
| `troop:add` / `troop:remove` | troops | `{ id }` / `{ id, reason }` |
| `troop:fire` | troops | `{ id, x, y, z, tx, ty, tz }` — one per sniper shot (`'troop.sniper'`) and per drone shot (`'troop.drone'`); `x, y, z` = the muzzle in world space (`y` = muzzle height: the sniper's barrel on its crate, the drone's gun under its body), `tx, tz` = the target, `ty` = the hit height (sniper: `TROOPS.sniper.hitY` on a zombie, `BOSSES.coreY` on the boss; drone: `FIRE.bullet.y`) (round-2 CHR-14 / FX-13; fx draws the sniper beam from `y` to `ty`) |
| `ui:screen` | ui | `{ id, prev }` |
| `ui:button` | ui | `{ id }` |
| `shop:buy` | ui | `{ id, rank, cost }` |
| `debug:histogram` | any (debug only) | `{ key, value }` |

Who listens (contract): `fx` and `audio` subscribe to the gameplay events per the GDD J-table (plus `boss:shield` → fx ring gauge at the boss core, `item:pickup` → fx icon flight, `level:failed` → fx desaturate + `render.gameOverCamera(true)`, `settings:change` → fx `haptics`/`reducedMotion`); `ui` subscribes to `squad:count`, `coins:change`, `save:change`, `level:start` (hides boss bar, letterbox, hint, name card, overlays; builds progress ticks), `level:progress`, `level:runup`, `weapon:*`, `gear:*`, `armor:change`, `boss:spawn/intro/hp/phase/shield/dead` (boss bar, letterbox, name card are driven ONLY from these events), `hint:show`, `hint:release`, `codex:new` (round 7), `level:complete`, `level:failed`, `endless:end`, `state:change` (screen mapping §6.2), `quality:change`; `level` subscribes to `zombie:die`, `gate:hit`, `gate:max`, `gate:flip`, `gate:pass`, `coin:pickup`, `boss:fight`, `boss:dead`, `squad:loss`, `squad:dead`, `squad:count` (stats, coins, hints — `coin:pickup` is the ONLY coin-accounting input) and (round 7: hints, FIELD GUIDE) `gate:spawn`, `gate:miss`, `gate:step`, `boss:attack`, `boss:phase`, `zombie:spawn`, `zombie:telegraph`, `item:pickup`, `crate:open`, `weapon:stored`, `weapon:start`, `armor:change`, `gear:start`; `save` subscribes to `level:complete`, `level:failed`, `endless:end`, `shop:buy`, `settings:change`, `hint:show` ONLY to call `flush()`; it never mutates state from a listener (mutations are direct calls: `level.finish` → `save.addCoins`, `save.recordLevel`/`recordEndless`, `save.setLastIncome`, `save.addStats`; `ui` → `save.markHint(id)` once a hint has been on screen `JUICE.ui.hintMarkSeconds` (round 7, teaching:0 F — it was at display, round-2 LVL-24 / UI-25; `level.showHint` never marks), `'read:<id>'` when the FIELD GUIDE that showed an entry closes, `'bosscard.<archetype>'` at an archetype's first name card; `level` → `save.markHint('seen:<id>')` at a FIELD GUIDE entry's first encounter; `ui` → `save.spend`/`setUpgrade`/`setSetting`); `engine` subscribes to `settings:change` (key `quality` → `setQuality`); `audio` to `settings:change` (`sound`, `music`) and `level:start`, `level:runup`, `boss:fight`, `level:complete`, `level:failed`, `endless:end`, `state:change` (music modes); `render` to `settings:change` (`hcGates`, `reducedMotion`) and `quality:change`; `squad` to `gear:start` / `gear:end` (magnet id), `armor:change` and `level:start` ONLY to drive the soldier gear glows through `models.setGearGlow` (§6.5).

---

## 6. Module contracts

Common shape for every system (`Squad`, `Zombies`, `BossSystem`, `Gates`, `Pickups`, `Weapons`, `Troops`, `Fx`, `LevelRunner`): `constructor(ctx)` stores `ctx` only; `init()` builds GPU objects and subscribes to events, returns `this`; `reset()` returns the system to the level-start state without allocating (pools cleared, `count = 0`, timers zeroed, meshes hidden); `dispose()` removes listeners and disposes geometries/materials it created (used on `webglcontextlost` rebuild and in tests). `reset()` is called by `LevelRunner.start()` in the order fx, weapons, zombies, boss, gates, pickups, troops, squad (squad last so its `squad:count` start event sees an empty world). Every per-frame method is allocation-free.

### 6.1 `js/util.js`

```js
export class Rng { constructor(seed:number); next():number /*[0,1)*/; int(min,max):number /*inclusive*/; range(min,max):number;
                   pick(arr):any; chance(p):boolean; shuffle(arr):arr; fork(offset:number):Rng; get seed():number }
export const ease = { outCubic(t), outBack(t, s=1.70158), inOutCubic(t), outQuad(t), linear(t) };
export function clamp(v, a, b); export function lerp(a, b, t); export function lerpExp(cur, target, rate, dt) /* cur + (target-cur)*(1-exp(-rate*dt)) */;
export function smoothstep(a, b, x); export function round5(v); export function round10(v); export function roundDesign(v) /* 1 <30, 5 <100, 10 above */;
export function hashString(s):number; export function fmtCoins(n):string /* round 7: the full number with comma thousands separators, "1,130" (no Intl) */;
export function fmtCompact(n):string /* round 7: the former fmtCoins, "1.2K" above 999 — only the ENDLESS tile's best score */; export function noise1(t):number /* value noise, deterministic */;
export class Pool { constructor(capacity, factory); alloc():object|null; free(obj); forEach(fn); get count(); clear() }  // swap-remove, indexed
export const scratch = { v3a, v3b, v3c, q, m4, m4b };  // THREE scratch objects, module-level
export const isIOS:boolean, isTouch:boolean, hasHaptics:boolean;
export function vibrate(pattern) /* guarded navigator.vibrate */;
export const storage = { get(key):string|null, set(key, value):boolean, remove(key):boolean, volatile:boolean };
export function mulberry32(seed):()=>number;
export function warnOnce(key:string, msg:string):void;
```

`Rng` is mulberry32. Seeds: run seed = `script.seed` (L1: 1001; generated: 1000 + L; endless: `Date.now() & 0xffff` unless `?seed=`). `ctx.rngFx = ctx.rng.fork(7)`. `isIOS = /iP(hone|od|ad)/.test(navigator.platform) || (navigator.platform === 'MacIntel' && navigator.maxTouchPoints > 1)` (iPadOS reports `MacIntel`). `storage` resolves the backing store once at module load — `let ls = null; try { ls = window.localStorage; ls.setItem('__t', '1'); ls.removeItem('__t') } catch { ls = null }` (in a sandboxed iframe the `window.localStorage` getter itself throws) — every `get/set/remove` is additionally try/catch wrapped and falls back to a module-level `Map`; `storage.volatile = ls === null`.

### 6.2 `js/engine.js`

```js
export class EventBus { on, once, off, emit }                          // §5
export function detectTier(gpuString:string|null, deviceMemoryGB:number|null, configQuality:string|null):'low'|'med'|'high';
  // pure; configQuality wins; else the first QUALITY_TIERS.heuristics rule whose regex matches gpuString; no string → fallbackTier ('med');
  // string without a match → unknownGpuTier ('low'); deviceMemoryGB ≤ lowMemoryGB caps the result at 'low'. Called by render.init() (§4.1 step 5)
export function maxAutoTierFor(gpuString:string|null, tier):'low'|'med'|'high';   // the matched rule's maxAuto, else tier (unknown string → unknownMaxAuto)
export function isSoftwareGpu(gpuString:string|null):boolean;   // round 7: the matched heuristics rule has software: true (SwiftShader, llvmpipe / softpipe / lavapipe, WARP, OSMesa); pure, used by render.init
export class StateMachine { constructor(ctx); get current():string; get prev():string; get data():any;
                            set(name, data?):void /* emits state:change ONLY; ui maps it to a screen in its own state:change listener */; is(name):boolean }
export class Engine {
  constructor(ctx); init():this; start():void; stop():void;
  frame(now):void;                      // §4.2
  setTimeScale(scale, seconds):void;    // used by fx.slowmo: sets time.scale and slowmoLeft (unscaled countdown); calls ctx.audio.setTimeScale(scale) on every change incl. the return to 1.0
  pause():void; resume():void;          // state playing ⇄ paused, emits pause/resume; resume() re-arms the continuous redraw
  requestRedraw():void;                 // sets ctx.render.dirty so the next paused frame runs step H once
  resetLevelClock():void;               // time.levelTime = 0; called by LevelRunner.start()/startEndless()/restart()
  setQuality(tier:'low'|'med'|'high'|'auto'):void;   // manual tier disables auto, sets render scale 1; emits quality:change {reason:'manual'}. Round 7: 'auto' from a manual tier returns to quality.initialTier when the
                                        //   current tier is above it (reason 'auto') and restarts the render scale (RS.softwareStart on software GL, else 1); 'auto' while already auto is a no-op
  lastInputMs:number;                   // r7 final fix: performance.now() ms of the last trusted player input — window pointerdown (capture), a steering drag's pointermove,
                                        //   keydown, a gamepad stick off its dead zone or any pressed button (polled); −Infinity before the first
  inputIdleS():number;                  // r7 final fix: (now − lastInputMs) / 1000 on the same clock (Infinity before any input); ui / level gate once-ever lesson marks on it (JUICE.ui.hintIdleS)
  get sustainedFrameMs():number;        // round 7: EMA of the rAF intervals of drawing frames (§2.2; 0 before the first) — dt clamp, scaler hitch filter, paused redraw gate, lite menus
  diagText(log?:boolean):string;        // round 7: the diagnostics report (§13); log = true also prints it (window.__gateRushDiag() calls diagText(true))
  freezeQuality(seconds):void;          // boss intro / level start; deadline on time.theatre (a pause does not use it up, CORE-7)
  measureRefresh():void;                // (re)starts the draw-free refresh probe (CORE-6); called by init, visibility → visible, trailing resize, pause
  get frameTargetMs():number;           // auto-scaler frame target = max(time.refreshMs, QUALITY_TIERS.scaler.targetMinMs)
  lockInput(locked:boolean):void;       // input.locked; steering integration and hasSteered are suspended while locked
  resetSteering():void;                 // input.targetX = 0, input.steerDx = 0; called by LevelRunner.start()
  armBackGuard():void;                  // round 6: boot step 13 — history back guard (below); no-op in the artifact shells, framed pages or without a usable history
  getSteerK():number;                   // SQUAD.steer.k / (SQUAD.steer.kFraction · W_eff) · settings.sensitivity (m per CSS px); W_eff = ctx.render.size.w in portrait,
                                        //   min(w, SQUAD.steer.landscapeWidthFactor · h) when w/h ≥ WORLD.camera.portraitBelow (CORE-8)
}
```

State → screen mapping (implemented by `ui`'s `state:change` listener, nowhere else): `boot → 'boot'` (shown by `ui.init`), `title → 'title'`, `levels → 'levels'`, `shop → 'shop'`, `playing → null` (HUD only; `showScreen(null)` also closes every overlay), `paused → 'pause'`, `complete → data?.endless ? 'endless' : 'complete'`, `gameover → 'gameover'`.

Input. Engine owns the input listeners on `ctx.app` (`#app`, NOT the canvas: the UI layer is `pointer-events: none` except interactive controls, §10.2, so drags anywhere outside a control bubble to `#app`). `#app` has `tabindex="-1"` and `contextmenu` is `preventDefault`ed on it. Pointer: `pointerdown` — if `event.target.closest('button, input, .seg, .card, [data-ui]')` is non-null the event is ignored for steering (UI controls); on iOS a `pointerdown` with `clientX < SQUAD.steer.iosEdgePx` (12) is ignored (edge swipe); if `_pointerId !== -1` ignore; else `_pointerId = e.pointerId; _lastX = e.clientX; _gestureAbs = 0; input.pointerDown = true; try { app.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId) } catch {}; app.focus({ preventScroll: true })`. `pointermove` (for `_pointerId` only): for each `ev` of `e.getCoalescedEvents?.() ?? [e]`: `dx = ev.clientX − _lastX; _lastX = ev.clientX; _gestureAbs += |dx|; if (_gestureAbs > SQUAD.steer.deadZonePx) input.steerDx += dx` (the first 2 px of accumulated |dx| per gesture are discarded); `input.hasSteered = true` on the first accepted delta (round 7: also on any keyboard / gamepad steering frame while playing and unlocked, so the steer hint yields for keyboard players). On `pointerup`, `pointercancel`, `lostpointercapture` whose `e.pointerId === _pointerId` (a second finger's events, including its own `lostpointercapture` bubbling from the canvas, never release the steer — CORE-5), window `blur`, `visibilitychange` hidden: `_pointerId = -1; input.pointerDown = false`. `movementX` is never used. Keyboard: `keydown/keyup` (A/D/←/→ → `keyLeft/keyRight`; round 7: a non-repeat keydown also records the direction (`_kbLastDir`, last pressed wins) and latches a tap bit (`_kbTap`) that beginFrame reads and frame() step B clears once a fixed step has run (r7 fix round 2: every press steers for at least one 1/60 s step), see §4.2 A / B; P/Esc pause, M mute); the `keydown` handler calls `preventDefault()` for ArrowLeft/ArrowRight/ArrowUp/ArrowDown/Space/KeyA/KeyD/KeyP/Escape when `state.is('playing') || state.is('paused')`. Gamepad: in `init()` run `try { navigator.getGamepads(); this._gamepadOk = true } catch { this._gamepadOk = false }` (a cross-origin iframe without the `gamepad` permissions policy throws `SecurityError`); poll (left stick x → `gamepadX`, dead zone 0.15, Start pauses) only when `_gamepadOk` AND after a `gamepadconnected` event has been seen; the per-frame poll is itself wrapped in try/catch and sets `_gamepadOk = false` on the first throw. Steering integration happens ONCE per frame in `beginFrame` (§4.2 A) into `input.targetX`; `level.start()` asks engine to zero it via `engine.resetSteering()` (sets `targetX = 0`, `steerDx = 0`, `hasSteered` untouched).

Audio unlock gesture (owned by engine, not audio): `init()` installs `pointerdown`, `pointerup`, `touchend`, `click`, `keydown` capture-phase listeners on `#app` whose first action is the synchronous `ctx.audio.unlock()`; they are removed once `audio:unlocked` fires and re-installed when audio reports a `statechange` to `suspended`/`interrupted` (`audio.needsGesture` true, §6.13). `touchend`/`click` matter because a touch `pointerdown` is not an activation-triggering event on iOS Safari.

Back guard (round 6, docs/build/r6-backguard.md): in an installed PWA (standalone, no history of its own) the Android back gesture — an edge swipe with gesture navigation — or button closes the app, and a page cannot block it. `armBackGuard()` (boot step 13) listens for `popstate` and keeps AT MOST ONE history entry, `history.pushState({ gateRush: 'back-guard' }, '')`, above the page's own entry whenever the player is anywhere but the title (state ≠ `title`/`boot`, or the settings or FIELD GUIDE overlay is open; tracked from `ui:screen`). It is pushed on the first screen change away from the title — normally inside a tap's activation window, which Chrome's history-manipulation intervention requires for the entry not to be skipped — and dropped with `history.back()` on arriving at the title (that popstate is consumed; a 1 s resync re-reads `history.state` if it never arrives), so one Back on the title leaves the app as before. A `popstate` that pops the guard runs the screen's Back: settings overlay → its `[data-back]` button; the FIELD GUIDE overlay (round 7, `ui:screen` `guide`, over the title or the pause panel) → its `[data-back]` button; `playing` → `pause()` (pause screen); `paused` → stays; `levels` / `shop` → their `[data-back]`; `complete` / `gameover` (and the endless results) → their HOME icon (`level.quitToTitle()` when missing); `title` → nothing. Buttons are `click()`ed so ui runs its own flow (`ui:button` → tap cue, `_back`, `quitToTitle`); the guard is then re-armed (see "Re-arm" below) unless that Back landed on the title. A reload on the guard entry finds `history.state` already marked and pushes nothing. Not armed when `ctx.config.isArtifact`, when `window.top !== window.self` (pushState in a frame writes the host tab's joint history and a `history.back()` could navigate the host) or when `history.pushState` / `history.back` are missing or throw; every history call is in try/catch. Steering keeps the iOS-only `iosEdgePx` dead strip: on Android the OS claims a back swipe itself (the page gets `pointercancel`, which already releases the steer), so an Android dead strip would only shrink the steering area. Re-arm (round-6 verify, docs/build/r6-verify.md §2.2): after a Back pops the guard, engine does NOT push it again — it steps forward onto the entry the Back just left (`history.forward()` from `popstate`, `_rearmGuard`; that popstate is consumed like the drop's, the ui:screen syncs of the Back action are held until it arrives, and with no forward entry a `BACK_GUARD_FORWARD_MS` (600 ms) timeout falls back to the push). Reason, measured with a browser-initiated Back (`chrome.tabs.goBack` → `NavigationController::GoBack`, the path Android's back gesture takes; Chrome 155): Chrome's history-manipulation intervention honours one pushed entry per user activation, an activation from before a Back does not carry over it, and a push from `popstate` therefore marked the page's own entry skippable — the second Back in a row with no touch in between closed the app. A traversal adds no entry, so the history stays `[page, guard]` and every Back while in a run pauses (or steps back a screen). `history.back()` / DevTools history navigation are not subject to the intervention.

Other listeners: `resize` via a `ResizeObserver` on `#app` (guarded; fallback `window.resize`) plus `screen.orientation.addEventListener('change')` when available, both feeding `render.resize()` + the `resize` event on a leading + trailing edge (CORE-10): the first event of a burst resizes at once (no frame is drawn with a stale drawing buffer / camera aspect), later events re-arm a trailing resize `JUICE.ui.resizeDebounceMs` (120) after the last one, and an event that flips portrait ⇄ landscape against the applied size also resizes at once; `visibilitychange` (→ `pause()` when hidden and playing; `audio` handles its own suspend/resume). Round 7: a `matchMedia('(resolution: <dpr>dppx)')` change listener (re-armed for each new value) feeds the same resize path — a window moved to another monitor changes the DPR without a ResizeObserver event. Window `blur` (round 7) clears held input as before and pauses a run: `pause()` when the state is `playing`, the loop runs, the page had focus (`document.hasFocus()` at init, then window `focus` / `blur`) and the player gave a trusted input (pointerdown anywhere, keydown, gamepad stick / Start) since the last `title` state — so a `?level=` boot or a harness that never touched the page is never paused this way, and the click that started the run counts; never in the boot / title flow. A 1 s poll of `document.hasFocus()` catches a focus loss whose `blur` never arrived (a Chrome popup taking the focus fired none, measured), counting only a true → false change the poll itself saw. Reason: the tester's L1 finished, and its once-ever boss lessons were spent, while they were in another window. Context-loss give-up (round 7, flow-coins:4): `webglcontextlost` also arms a `QUALITY_TIERS.restoreGiveUpMs` (12 s) timer that counts visible time only (cleared when hidden, re-armed when visible while still lost, cleared by `webglcontextrestored`); when it fires the fatal box `ui.showError(STRINGS.errors.gpuGone, true)` with RELOAD (z 60) covers the 'Reloading graphics…' overlay (z 40), so the overlay is never a dead end — the save is committed before the results appear, a reload loses at most an unfinished run. `pause()` ducks audio via `audio.duck(MUSIC.duck.pause, 0)` and sets the ≤ 30 fps redraw gate on High (`params.blur`), redraw on demand elsewhere (§4.2).

Auto-scaler (GDD §16): `refreshMs` comes from the refresh probe (CORE-6) — `measureRefresh()` runs its own rAF chain that draws nothing and keeps an interval only when no `renderer.render()` ran in it or in the interval before it (`renderer.info.render.frame` unchanged) and it is < `hitchIgnoreMs` (250); with `refreshSamples` (90) samples, or at `refreshProbeMs` (5 s) with ≥ `refreshMinSamples` (60), the median snapped to the nearest of {8.33, 11.1, 16.67} becomes `ctx.time.refreshMs` (default 16.67; fewer samples keep the previous value). Started by `init()` (boot step 8: the boot screen before the warm-up), on `visibilitychange` → visible, after each trailing resize and on `pause()`; a call while a probe runs extends it. Title / gameplay frames never count. The scaler target is `frameTargetMs = max(refreshMs, scaler.targetMinMs (16.67))` — Medium is the 60 fps contract. Rolling window of `interval` (rAF delta) and `jsMs` (beginFrame → endFrame) — round 7 (perf-render:3): the last `windowSec` (1) s of frames, at least `windowMin` (8), at most `window` (60, the old window at 60 fps); an interval counts unless it is a hitch ≥ max(`hitchIgnoreMs` 250, min(`hitchMaxMs` 1000, `hitchFactor` 3 × `sustainedFrameMs`)) (a < 4 fps device used to be all hitches). Drop a tier when mean `interval` > `dropFactor` (1.4)·target for `dropAfter` (3) s. Raise when mean `interval` ≤ `raiseFactor` (1.05)·target AND mean `jsMs` < `raiseJsMs` (7.5) for `raiseAfter` (10)·`raiseBackoff` (2)^n s (n = auto drops from the tier being raised to this session; `setQuality('auto')` clears n) and `tier < ctx.quality.maxAutoTier`. At most one change per `hysteresis` (15) s — a DROP while the mean interval exceeds `emergencyFactor` (3) × target waits `emergencyHysteresis` (4) s (round 7); frozen while `time.theatre < quality.frozenUntil` (boss intro, first `freezeLevelStart` (2) s of a level); a manual tier disables auto. Render-scale rungs (round 7, perf-render:1, `QUALITY_TIERS.renderScale`): on the lowest tier of **software GL** (`quality.software`) `_sampleRenderScale` runs before the tier rules — one rung down the ladder (1, 0.85, 0.7, 0.6, 0.5) after `dropAfter` (2) s of the drop condition; one rung up after `raiseAfter` (5) s × `scaler.raiseBackoff`^(drops from that rung this session, `_rsDrops`; `setQuality('auto')` clears them) while the interval predicted at the next rung (mean × (next / cur)², fill-bound — a CPU rasteriser always is) is ≤ `raiseMargin` (0.85) × the drop line, or while the mean holds ≤ `raiseFactor` × target (vsync hides the headroom: it probes up); ≥ `hysteresis` (3) s between rung changes; tier changes only at scale 1. A hardware GPU never calls it: its `renderScale` is 1 from `render.init` to the end of the session (r7 final fix, lead decision 1 — rounds 0–3 ran the rungs there too behind a gain check, `gainMin` / `gainBusy` / `gainRechecks` / `gainRetryAfter` / `gainRetryGrowth` / `gainHitch` with the `_rsCheck*` / `_rsAcc*` / `_rsStrikes` / `_rsRetryAt` state and the 'nogain' / 'busy' undo reasons, all removed: a CPU-bound hardware GPU sank to 0.5 for whole sessions at unchanged fps, and after a transient load the four-rung climb back kept its tiers at Low ≈ 50 s longer than 1.2.2), so its tiers follow 1.2.2's raise timing. Each change sets `quality.renderScale`, calls `render.setRenderScale(s)` (applied inside the next draw), restarts the window and emits `quality:scale`. Debug overlay (`?debug=1`): `ui.debugPanel` text updated at 4 Hz with `renderer.info.render.calls` (= shadow + main + post, since `renderer.info.autoReset = false` and `beginFrame` resets it), triangles, rolling JS ms, tier, DPR, counts (soldiers, zombies, bullets, shots/s and live laser beams from `ctx.debug.shotsPerSec` / `laserBeams` (round-2 WPN-7), particles), distinct materials drawn (`render.callStats.materials`, VIS-13), dropped-pool counters, red flags at > 84 calls (50 main + 20 shadow + 14 post) or > 7.5 ms; `render.draw()` samples `info.render.calls` after the RenderPass so the overlay prints main/shadow/post separately — r7 fix: only for a frame that drew (`_drewFrame`); a frozen lite menu or a draw-free paused frame prints "calls 0 (no draw)", not the last draw's split.

### 6.3 `js/render.js`

```js
export class RenderSystem {
  constructor(ctx); async init():Promise<this>;   // order: WebGL 2 probe → WebGLRenderer → GPU string → ctx.quality via detectTier → camera + lights → TextureFactory.build (needs the renderer for anisotropy) → MaterialLibrary → applyQuality(params) (composer, shadows, fog) → Environment.build; round-3 boot polish: a painted frame (yieldPaint: rAF → macrotask, 100 ms fallback) after the context + scene setup, after the textures, after materials / passes / applyQuality and after Environment.build, and TextureFactory.build paints between its steps once they ran > 16 ms since the last paint, so no init step shares a frozen frame with the next
  renderer:THREE.WebGLRenderer; scene:THREE.Scene; camera:THREE.PerspectiveCamera; composer:EffectComposer|null;
  textures:TextureFactory; materials:MaterialLibrary; env:Environment;
  uniforms:{ uTime:{value:number} };               // the ONE shared time uniform, referenced by every custom material
  size:{ w:number, h:number, dpr:number, portrait:boolean };
  dirty:boolean;                                   // "draw one frame even while paused"; set by resize/webglcontextrestored/quality:change/ui.showScreen/engine.requestRedraw; cleared by draw()
  lights:{ sun:THREE.DirectionalLight, hemi:THREE.HemisphereLight, bossCore:THREE.PointLight, laser:THREE.PointLight };
  update(dt):void;         // camera follow (ctx.world.squadX), shake offset from ctx.fx.getShake(out), dolly, FOV kick, env.scroll(roadSpeed*dt), sun follow, uTime
  draw():void;             // (round 7: first applies a pending render scale) composer.render() or renderer.render(); samples renderer.info.render.calls after the RenderPass for the overlay; dirty = false, _sizeDirty = false
  callStats:{ main, shadow, post, total, materials };   // per-draw call split for the debug overlay; materials = distinct materials of the visible drawables, resampled every 30 draws (round-2 QA VIS-13; counted in an Int32Array indexed by Material.id — no property is ever added to a three material); the overlay pass's draws are scene draws and count as MAIN
  overlayLayer:number;     // 30 — round-2 FX-10 residual (cross-group): objects on this layer (fx count label + plate only on it; the HDR clamp and the floaters on layers 0 + 30) are drawn by overlayPass AFTER the bloom pass and before GradeOutputPass (no clear, no swap, camera sees only the layer, scene matrices not re-updated; the lights carry the layer too and the shadow map is not re-rendered by this pass — `shadowMap.autoUpdate` is off for its render — so it leaves the main pass's light setup in three's per-scene render state: r170 renders the shadow map before `setupLights()`, and an overlay render without lights made the next frame's shadow pass key its depth programs with no lights and link them mid-play, e.g. the skinned boss body at every first boss spawn — round-2 release verification); on Low (no composer) draw() enables the layer on the camera for the single scene render. The clamp draws in both passes (pre-bloom: no bloom seeding under / around the plate; post-bloom: caps the haze that still reached it); floaters bloom in the main pass and are drawn again above the plate
  overlayPass:RenderPass;  // the post-bloom overlay pass (composer chain: RenderPass → TierBloomPass → overlayPass → GradeOutputPass)
  warmup(onProgress?:(t:number)=>void):Promise<void>;   // VIS-2, chunked since round-2 QA VIS-17: compiles every program of the committed tier a few per frame (≈ 16 ms), draws every renderable once out of sight in ≈ 16 ms chunks, then the composer's first use and the first real frame in tasks of their own; onProgress(0..1) monotonic (boot bar, CORE-9); never throws; awaited by boot and after webglcontextrestored. Round-3 boot polish: the link poll that resolves each new program's uniform / attribute tables (a synchronous GPU round trip per program, plus the info logs under ?debug=1) is bounded by the same per-tick budget (at least one program per tick) — 8 per tick froze the boot bar ≈ 0.3 s at 4× CPU throttling
  resize():void;           // §9.5: size from #app.clientWidth/clientHeight, DPR cap + pixel cap × render scale, renderer.setPixelRatio/setSize ONLY when the size or DPR changed (round 7: then _sizeDirty = true until the next draw, and render.dirty), composer.setPixelRatio + setSize (in-place, no reallocation), camera aspect + preset tween, ui.refit(); the `resize` event is engine's
  setRenderScale(s:number):void;   // round 7 (perf-render:1): quality.renderScale = s, applied at the start of the next draw() (re-allocation + draw in one task: no blank frame); called by engine's auto-scaler / setQuality
  applyQuality(params):void;  // §9.3/§9.4: shadows (sun.castShadow, map size), bloom pass, MSAA samples, fog far (round 5: through env.applyQuality, params.fogFar × the district's farScale), prop density, dust, anisotropy; called on quality:change; the only place render targets are (re)created, with dispose() of the old ones
  dollyTo(pos:THREE.Vector3, look:THREE.Vector3, outSec, holdSec, backSec):void;  // boss intro; no-op under reduced motion
  cancelDolly():void;                   // snaps the camera back to the follow preset; called by LevelRunner.start()
  get dollying():boolean;               // round-3 FTUE: true while the boss-intro dolly owns the camera (fx fades the count label, ui hides #shield)
  setBloomStrength(v):void;             // round 5: non-finite v → bloomBase (the district's strength); finite v holds for this frame
  setDistrict(district, seconds?:number):void;   // round 5 (docs/DISTRICTS.md §6.1 / §8.2): lights, shadow box, fog / clear colour, exposure, bloom, grade, uRoadBg, eye glow; theatre-clock fade (0 = snap); forwards to env.setDistrict
  get district():District; get bloomBase():number;   // round 5
  get titleBlend():number;              // round-5 art review: eased title idle-rig weight (0 in gameplay, 1 on the title rig, tweened with the camera); environment's title-rig lamp clearance reads it
  setDesaturate(t01:number):void;       // GradeOutputPass uDesat (no-op on Low); called by fx
  gameOverCamera(on:boolean):void;      // GDD J18: drop 1 m + slow 10° orbit over 1.2 s (RENDER.gameOver); static under reduced motion; off = instant return
  worldToScreen(v3:THREE.Vector3, out:{x,y}):{x,y};   // CSS px
  idleCamera(on:boolean):void;          // title rig: WORLD.camera.title (portrait) / titleLandscape (aspect ≥ portraitBelow), tweened; while on, the title light (RENDER.title: hemisphere fill, sun swung toward the camera) eases in — uniforms only (round-2 QA VIS-15 / VIS-20)
  blobShadowSlot(i:number, x:number, z:number, scale:number):void;   // Low tier only: positions blob-shadow disc i under a character; no-op on other tiers. Slot ranges: squad 0–299, zombies 300–559, troops 560–564, boss 565–566; capacity 567
  rebuild():void;                       // after webglcontextrestored (§9.5): re-applies renderer state; render's own lost-context GL objects (composer + bloom targets, warm target, shadow map) are DROPPED without dispose() and the composer is rebuilt for the committed tier; the canvas is resized only when size / DPR changed (round-2 QA VIS-21: disposing lost-context objects logged INVALID_OPERATION warnings); round 6: `textures.restoreAll()` re-draws every procedural canvas from code before the re-upload (§9.5)
  dispose():void;
}
export class TextureFactory {
  constructor(ctx); async build(progressCb):Promise<void>;   // runs after the renderer exists; all canvases drawn (each recipe kept for re-draws, §9.6); waits document.fonts.load('100px "Lilita One"') ≤ RENDER.textures.fontWaitMs (round 7: 0.5 s, was 1.5 s — the late-font re-draw below made the long wait pointless), redraws the atlas once if the font arrives late; installs the resume / font listeners (§9.5)
  restoreAll():void;             // round 6: re-draw EVERY canvas from its recipe + needsUpdate (RenderSystem.rebuild after webglcontextrestored); font re-checked first
  verify():boolean;              // round 6: 1×1 probe pixel per canvas; any blank → restoreAll-style re-draw of all; atlas drawn with the fallback + font now loaded → atlas re-draw; true when it re-drew. Run on visibilitychange → visible, pageshow, 'resume'
  onRepaint:(() => void)|null;   // set by RenderSystem: render.dirty = true after any re-draw (a paused game draws on demand)
  repaints:number; lastRepaint:string; lastRepaintMs:number;   // debug / harness: re-draw count, last cause ('<canvas>:webgl|2d|blank:<canvas>|font|aniso', 'all:…'), its cost
  road:THREE.CanvasTexture;      // 512×1024, RepeatWrapping; anisotropy = min(params.anisotropy, renderer.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy())
  digitAtlas:THREE.CanvasTexture; // 1024×256: 2 rows × 10 cells, cell 102×128 px, glyph drawn at 100 px Lilita One centred with a RENDER.atlas.strokePx (18 px since round-2 QA VIS-12) #0B0F1A stroke and 8 px padding on all sides; row 0 = 0–9, row 1 = + − × ÷ M A X . ★ ⚡ (÷ drawn as shapes, VIS-5; ★ / ⚡ drawn as shapes since the round-3 verify — their system-font fallback lookup cost 70–250 ms at 4× CPU at boot, the intermittent atlas-step stall); generateMipmaps true, minFilter LinearMipmapLinearFilter, magFilter LinearFilter, anisotropy 4, colorSpace SRGBColorSpace, premultiplyAlpha true (canvases are premultiplied)
  glyphUV(ch:string, out:{u0,v0,u1,v1,advance}):void;  // cell rect inset by 2 px; advance in em: digits 0.62, + 0.55, − 0.5, × 0.6, ÷ 0.6, M 0.7, A 0.62, X 0.62, . 0.3
  glow:THREE.CanvasTexture;      // 128×128 radial
  smoke:THREE.CanvasTexture;     // 256×256 noise
  windows:THREE.CanvasTexture;   // 256×256 emissive map
  particle:THREE.CanvasTexture;  // 64×64 soft disc
  muzzle:THREE.CanvasTexture;    // 128×64, 2 frames
  dust:THREE.CanvasTexture;      // 32×32
  icons:THREE.CanvasTexture;     // 512×64, 8 item icons (shotgun, minigun, rocket, rail, laser, armor, chip, booster) + 8 (magnet, sniper, medic, flame, drone, apc, coins, star); used by fx.iconFlight sprites and DOM pills; round 4: one bold silhouette per item (guns = tilted side views), mid-to-light fills under a dark outline, legible at 18–36 css px on the pill's light disc, the dark HUD and the item-gate panel
  iconUV(id, out):void;
  iconDataURL(id, px):string;    // for DOM pills (cached)
}
export class MaterialLibrary {
  constructor(ctx);  // created after textures
  standard(hex, opts?):THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;    // cached by key `${hex}|${roughness}|${metalness}|${vertexColors}`; every data.js hex is sRGB and goes through Color.setHex
  hdrBasic(r,g,b, opts?):THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;     // toneMapped:false; colour set with material.color.setRGB(r, g, b, LinearSRGBColorSpace) (HDR triplets are already linear, never double-converted); cached
  gatePanel():THREE.ShaderMaterial;                   // ONE instance for the instanced panel mesh: transparent, DoubleSide, depthWrite false; per-instance iPanel/iScale attributes carry tint, stripes, flash, ring (§6.9); opacity uniform uOpacity = GATE_RULES.panel.opacity | opacityHC; round-5 art review: the ring-gauge fill is capped under the bloom threshold (RING_LUM_CAP) and item lanes back their icon with a near-opaque, near-black plate (ITEM_PLATE_*); every glyph plate is at least 60 % opaque (PLATE_ALPHA)
  gateFrame():THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;             // onBeforeCompile: colour/emissive/stripes from iFrame (§6.9); emissive RENDER.gateFrameEmissive (+ lowTierEmissiveBoost on Low)
  glyph():THREE.ShaderMaterial;                       // digit atlas quads with iGlyph/iPopTime/iColor
  particle():THREE.ShaderMaterial;                    // GPU-simulated particles (two variants: additive, normal)
  decal():THREE.ShaderMaterial;                       // instanced decals, kind/progress/size/colour from iDecal (§6.12)
  hpBar():THREE.ShaderMaterial;
  sky():THREE.ShaderMaterial;
  tracer():THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;                   // instanceColor HDR (values written straight into the Float32Array)
  blobShadow():THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;
  depthFor(o:{ instanced?:boolean, instanceColor?:boolean, map?:boolean }):THREE.MeshDepthMaterial;   // round-2 QA VIS-14: cached MeshDepthMaterial({ depthPacking: RGBADepthPacking }) per combination, allocated once; assign as a caster's customDepthMaterial. three's WebGLShadowMap draws every caster WITHOUT one with a single shared depth material and copies map / alphaTest onto it, so instanced, instanced-with-colour, mapped and plain casters alternating in one shadow pass re-resolve its program every frame. Rule: every InstancedMesh caster and every mapped caster sets customDepthMaterial = materials.depthFor({ instanced, instanceColor: !!mesh.instanceColor, map: !!mesh.material.map }) (Environment does for all env casters; zombies' globs and pickups' crates use it or do not cast); plain non-instanced, unmapped casters (boss parts, troops) stay on three's default, which then only ever sees that one combination
  markAllNeedsUpdate():void;                          // iterates every cache (depthFor materials included) and sets needsUpdate = true (shadow type change, §9.3)
  uTime:{value:number};                               // === ctx.render.uniforms.uTime
}
export class Environment {
  constructor(ctx); build():void;
  scroll(dz):void;   // moves road ring, curbs, props, smoke by +dz; recycles segments (seeded prop re-placement, seed = levelSeed*1000 + segmentIndex)
  setLevel(seed, propDensity):void;  // re-seeds props at level start (uses the given seed, never ctx.rng)
  setFog(near, far, lerpSeconds):void;
  sky:THREE.Mesh; ground:THREE.Mesh; segments:THREE.Mesh[6]; props:Map<string, THREE.InstancedMesh>; dust:THREE.Points;
}
```

Round 5, visual districts (contract and shader / uniform list: docs/DISTRICTS.md §6, §8.2–8.3):
- `RenderSystem.setDistrict(district, seconds = 0)` lerps lights (sun colour / intensity / offset vector, hemisphere), the shadow camera's bottom bound (`RENDER.sun.frustum.b × max(1, yz / yz₁)`, so shadows reach z ≈ −23 on the road in every district), fog colour, clear colour, exposure, bloom strength and radius (the threshold is fixed), the grade, the gate panel `uRoadBg` and `models.setEyeGlow` on the theatre clock (`ctx.time.theatre`, ms; 0 = snap). Every consumer reads a preallocated current-state mirror (`_cur`): `_updateTitleLight` lerps `_cur.hemi.intensity → _cur.hemi.title` and swings `_cur.sun.titleAzimuthDeg`; `_setSunAzimuth` uses `_cur.sun.offset` and also runs on fade frames. It forwards to `env.setDistrict`.
- `get district`; `get bloomBase` (a non-finite `setBloomStrength()` restores it; a finite one holds the strength for the frame, so fx's boss-death pulse wins over a running fade); `warmup()` also draws `env.districtWarmList()` once; `rebuild()` re-applies `_cur` (no `setDistrict`, no environment layout). Round-5 integration (docs/DISTRICTS.md §8.7): fx's boss-death pulse rides the base — it peaks at `bloomBase + (RENDER.bloom.deathPeak − RENDER.bloom.strength)` and ends (and `fx.reset()` cancels it) with `setBloomStrength()`, so a Night / Toxic / Ash / Frozen pulse is centred on that district's strength (District 1: 0.6 → 1.6 → 0.6, unchanged).
- Implementation (round 5, render core; docs/build/r5-render-core.md): `_cur` is one `Float64Array(40)` (linear colours from `Color.setHex`, scalars, the offset vector); every DISTRICTS entry is packed once at construction, so a fade frame is index arithmetic (the offset is a normalized lerp: direction nlerped, length lerped; k = 1 copies the target exactly) followed by the consumer writes. `setDistrict` also accepts a 0-based index or an id (like `env.setDistrict`) and any object with the district key tree (packed into a preallocated spare). A snap always resets the bloom strength to the base (a level start / title reset). `setBloomStrength`'s hold stamp is `ctx.time.frame`; a finite value holds for that frame only: on a fade frame and on any later frame without a hold, `update` writes the base back (so fx's pulse, which ends on `RENDER.bloom.strength`, cannot leave a non-D1 district at the District 1 strength). `applyQuality` no longer calls `env.setFog(near, params.fogFar, 0)`: the environment's own `applyQuality` writes `params.fogFar × fog.farScale` (into a running lerp's target), so a tier change no longer cancels a run-up or district fog lerp mid-way; render writes `fog.far` itself only before the environment exists. A new bloom pass (High ⇄ Medium, Low → composer) is created with `_cur`'s radius and the current strength. The texel size for shadow snapping reads the shadow camera's live bounds. Verified: `renderer.info.programs` flat across all six districts, a 4 s cross-fade, an interrupted fade, Low / High / Medium switches and a context restore.
- `Environment.setDistrict(district, seconds = 0)` covers the sky (new `uDisc`, `uCloudCover`, `uStars`, `uSkyLitTh`, `uSkyDip`), ground, road (new `gr:road` program), building (`uGrWin`, `uGrSnow`), lamp / slot-1 (split uniforms, per-instance upright / lit bits re-applied at the fade's 0.5 point), smoke (anchors) and weather uniforms (swap dip 0.45–0.55 for weather and skyline columns), and owns fog near / far (`params.fogFar × fog.farScale`, also in `applyQuality`; a `setFog` during a fade takes them over). Prop slots swap geometry immediately when seconds is 0, otherwise drain (placers run with writes suppressed) → swap → refill as segments recycle. `applyQuality` sets the dust drawRange to `params.dust × weather.density`; `_sync` wraps the dust scroll by the active weather box.
- `Environment.districtWarmList(out)`; `get district`.
- Implementation (round 5, env art; docs/build/r5-env-art.md): everything a fade lerps is resolved once per district at module load into a flat `Float64Array` (linear colours, multipliers, scalars, the disc direction) plus per-slot entries (kind, merged layout, weight, tint); a fade frame lerps a "from" snapshot into the preallocated mirror and writes ≈ 70 uniforms (k = 1 copies the target exactly; the disc direction is nlerped mid-fade only). The district kinds are models.js geometries adopted as per-slot *view* geometries (models' BufferAttributes and index shared, never mutated, never disposed here; the emissive slots add their own shared `iEmit`); a kind with no geometry falls back to the slot's built-in kind. `env.warmDistricts()` (called by `ModelLibrary.init`, boot step 6) also parks one hidden `InstancedMesh` proxy per (slot, alternative kind) sharing the slot's material, depth material and instance attributes: hidden objects draw nothing, and every "draw each renderable once" warm pass (`render.warmup`, `_warmHiddenChunked` after a tier change) creates their VAOs, so no swap ever uploads or links. `env.setDistrict` takes a district object, a 0-based DISTRICTS index or an id (as `render.setDistrict`).
