Additions beyond DISTRICTS.md §6.2, all reducing to District 1 exactly: sky `uCloudOff` (cirrus position carried across a drift change, so the clouds never race during a fade), lamp / beacon `uGrUnlit` (an unlit emissive part shows its own dim hue instead of the clamped white; 0 in District 1), road `uGrWet` (puddle reflection of the sky horizon colour × 0.5 × Schlick fresnel; without it the smooth darkened puddles read as black holes away from the sun's mirror point), and container-style scatter kinds with `colors.paint` get one paint per instance (the cars rule). The blink *rate* switches at the fade's 0.5 point with `lamps.lit` (a lerped rate sweeps the phase `rate·uTime` through many cycles); the duty lerps.
- Round 5, art review (docs/build/r5-art.md; every item is a constant, a uniform or a shader edit compiled at boot — no program is added, `renderer.info.programs` stays 58 / 58 / 42; District 1 gameplay frames are pixel-identical for the environment / render edits):
  - **Gate panel** (materials.js): the ring-gauge fill (progress band and the full ring of a × gate at its cap or a ÷ flipped to ×2) is normalized to linear luminance `RING_LUM_CAP` = `RENDER.bloom.threshold − 0.05` (0.80) and a full ring pulses up to `RING_PULSE` (30 %) below it — the old fill (≈ 1.0–1.7 on a × gate) bloomed in from all four sides and lifted the glyph plate it frames (glyph : panel 1.9–2.9 : 1 at the armed line on Medium / High). Item lanes (pentagon top) mix their plate `ITEM_PLATE_DARKEN` 0.85 toward the outline colour at opacity ≥ `ITEM_PLATE_ALPHA` 0.9 with luminance ≤ `ITEM_PLATE_LUM_CAP` 0.02 (the mid-tone icon read 2.3–2.6 : 1 on Low on the translucent violet plate); every other type keeps `PLATE_*`, whose minimum plate opacity `PLATE_ALPHA` is 0.6 (was 0: at the 36 px point a 38 % panel let the road and the additive glow quad lift the plate, glyph : panel 2.2–2.8 : 1 on Low).
  - **Road puddles** (environment.js `gr:road`): the horizon-band reflection factor is at least `DISTRICT_RULES.puddleReflectFloor` (0.3) up to `puddleFloorFadeM[0]` (18 m) of view distance, fading out by `[1]` (35 m, before the 36 px gate); the roughness switches inside the puddle only (`smoothstep(0.7, 1, pud)`, was pud³); a new chunk after `<lights_fragment_end>` (`ROAD_FRAG_SPEC`) removes up to 90 % of the direct specular across the rim (pud 0.45 → 0.92), where the intermediate roughness caught the key light as a bright crescent; inside a Night City light pool a puddle adds pool radiance × `DISTRICT_RULES.poolPuddleGlint` (0.12). All inert where `road.puddles` is 0 (District 1, Ash). Read guarded with these values as fallbacks.
  - **Title-rig lamp clearance** (environment.js `_titleClear`, `LAMP_VERT_ROW`, `uGrLampRow` vec2 shared by the lamp material, the road and the ground): while `render.titleBlend` > 0, a lamp row within `LAMP_CLEAR_M` [1.8, 2.8] m of the camera's x is scaled to its feet (lamp material only — the beacon material shares `LAMP_VERT_PARS` but never clears), its light pools fade with it and the lamp slot casts no shadow (`castShadow` is an object flag, not a program key; `_swapSlot` keeps the hold). Only the landscape / desktop title rig (`WORLD.camera.titleLandscape`, x 4 — 0.75 m inside the right row) is affected: gameplay and the portrait title see (1, 1). Fixes r5-integrate §5 issue 2 (every right-shoulder lamp swept through the desktop title every 2.5 s).
- The 8 prop InstancedMeshes keep their materials. Slot capacity = max(`RENDER.env.props`, 6 × the largest `DISTRICT_PROPS[kind].per`) (tree 40 → 60). Instance stride, `count` and `castShadow` (a kind may set `castShadow: false`) follow the active kind. Non-builtin kinds follow the placer contract of docs/DISTRICTS.md §7.2 (per-placer defaults, `half` extents with the `DISTRICT_RULES.edgeClearX` clamp, `poseY`, `smokeY`). District 1 placement is unchanged byte for byte.

Tier pre-compile (VIS-1 `_compileChunked`, also used by `warmup`): every work item is compiled through ONE plain stand-in object (`COMPILE_STAND`) carrying exactly the fields three r170's `compile()` / `getParameters()` read from an object (`isMesh/isPoints/isLine/isSprite/isInstancedMesh/isBatchedMesh/isSkinnedMesh`, `material`, `geometry`, `instanceColor`, `morphTexture`, `_colorsTexture`, `traverse`/`traverseVisible`) — never through `Object.create(object)`, which made every scene mesh a V8 prototype object (round-2 cross-group; the program sets are identical, verified on Low → Medium → High). Re-check that field list when upgrading three.

Every material built with `onBeforeCompile` sets `material.customProgramCacheKey = () => 'gr:' + variantId` where `variantId` names the injected chunk set (`'char'`, `'char-recoil'`, `'gateframe'`, `'hpbar'` …); identical chunk sets intentionally share a program (three keys program caches by `onBeforeCompile.toString()` otherwise, so two variants from one factory would silently share a shader). Note on fonts: `TextureFactory.build` resolves after the atlas is drawn; if the font arrives later (within the 1.5 s wait), the atlas is redrawn once before resolve. After resolve the only texture uploads are the re-draws of §9.5 (context / canvas loss, a blank canvas on resume, the display font arriving after the wait — `document.fonts` 'loadingdone') and the road's anisotropy change.

### 6.4 `js/models.js`

```js
export class ModelLibrary {
  constructor(ctx); init():this;
  geo:{ soldier, sniper, flamer, medic, walker, runner, crawler, brute, spitter, gateFrame, gatePanel, crate, coin, rocket, glob, shard, tracer, muzzle, quad,
        barricade, sandbags, car, building, tree, rubble, smoke, lamppost, blobDisc, apc, drone }:Record<string, THREE.BufferGeometry>;
  // character geometries carry attributes: position, normal, color (vertexColors), aPart (float), aPivot (vec3); NO uv (geometry.deleteAttribute('uv') before merge)
  limbParamsFor(classId:'soldier'|'walker'|'runner'|'crawler'|'brute'|'spitter'):{ uFreq, uLegAmp, uArmAmp, uArmRest, uBobAmp, uRecoil };  // returns SQUAD.limb or ZOMBIES[classId].limb; no values live in models.js
  makeCharacterMaterial(classId):THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;   // vertexColors, onBeforeCompile injects the limb-swing chunk + hit-flash/tint/death fragment chunk, shares ctx.render.uniforms.uTime; customProgramCacheKey 'gr:char' | 'gr:char-recoil' (soldier)
  makeCharacterDepthMaterial(classId):THREE.MeshDepthMaterial; // same vertex chunk, RGBADepthPacking, for customDepthMaterial
  buildBossRig(archetype):THREE.Group;                         // unique CPU-animated rig with named children: torso, head, jaw, armL, armR, legL, legR, core, plates[4], shield (Colossus), chains[]
    // round-2 CHR-11: the seven animated joints (legL, legR, torso, armL, armR, head, jaw) are THREE.Bone objects (same names, origins,
    // rest transforms, children) of ONE SkinnedMesh 'skin' (body, rigidly weighted, castShadow true). Round 5b (docs/build/r5b-boss-draws.md):
    // core, plate0–3 and chain0–1 are Bones too, and the rig draws in THREE SkinnedMeshes bound to one Skeleton — 'skin' (body),
    // 'armour' (plate0–3 + chain0–1, metal material, no shadow) and 'glow' (eyes + Queen sacs + core, HDR basic, uGlowCol[3] per group);
    // only the Colossus 'shield' stays a plain Mesh. A plate is hidden by visible = false + scale 0; rigs of one archetype (the Twins)
    // share the merged geometries. rig.userData exposes rig.skin / rig.armour / rig.glow / rig.skeleton. Draws per rig: 3 main
    // (+1 while the Colossus shield is up), 1 shadow; Hulk Twins 6 main / 2 shadow (was Hulk / Juggernaut 7, Queen 8, Colossus 10).
    // boss.js picks shadow casters by name (SHADOW_PARTS includes 'skin'); per-joint castShadow toggles act on Bones and do nothing.
  buildTroopMesh(id):THREE.Mesh|THREE.Group;                   // sniper, medic, flamer (soldier material), drone, apc; named children troops.js animates: 'discs' (drone rotor-disc blur, replaces 'rotor'), 'wheels' (APC running gear, ONE merged mesh, replaces wheelFL…wheelRR), 'led', 'lens', 'cross', 'ring'
  setGearGlow(magnet01:number, armor01:number):void;            // CHR-2: magnet → purple helmet-ring pulse (1.5 Hz), armour → blue shoulder glint; uniform writes only, every soldier-class material
}
export class CharacterInstances {
  constructor(ctx, classId, capacity, { castShadow, receiveShadow });
  mesh:THREE.InstancedMesh;  // frustumCulled=false, DynamicDrawUsage, customDepthMaterial set, count=0
  attrs:{ iAnim:THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute /* vec4: phase, speed, hitTime, fireTime */, iState:THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute /* vec4: death01, tintR, tintG, tintB */ };  // 2 attribute slots
  alloc():number /* instance index or -1 */; free(i):void;      // swap-remove keeps [0,count) dense; caller is told the moved index via onMove(from,to) callback
  onMove:(from:number, to:number)=>void;
  writeMatrix(i, x, y, z, yaw, pitchTopple, scale):void;        // composes into instanceMatrix.array at i*16 using scratch
  setHit(i, nowSec):void /* iAnim.z */; setFire(i, nowSec):void /* iAnim.w */; setDeath(i, t01):void /* iState.x */; setSpeed(i, v):void /* iAnim.y */; setTint(i, r,g,b):void /* iState.yzw */; setPhase(i, p):void /* iAnim.x */;
  commit():void;  // once per frame: instanceMatrix.needsUpdate, attrs needsUpdate with addUpdateRange(0, count*stride)
}
```

Round 5, visual districts (docs/DISTRICTS.md §6.3, §7):
- `geo.barrier`, `cones`, `billboard`, `truck`, `neon`, `barrel`, `container`, `smokestack`, `pipe`, `puddle`, `burningWreck`, `dune`, `rock`, `snowbank`, `frozenCar` and `pine` use the environment-prop convention (`mergeProps`; position / normal / uv / color linear itemSize 3; feet at y = 0; colours from `DISTRICT_PROPS[kind].colors`; emissive parts on barricade- and lamppost-slot kinds as colour excess over 1.0).
- Implementation (round 5, env art): `buildDistrictGeometries` after `buildStaticGeometries` (≈ 3.7 k triangles for the 16 kinds; every part indexed — Polyhedron / Extrude parts get a sequential index — so the kind merges and keeps the slot's attribute layout). At the end of `init()` models calls `ctx.render.env.warmDistricts()` when present (the environment is built at step 5, before models). A `setEyeGlow` value set before a class material exists is used when it is created.
- `setEyeGlow(v)` writes `v` to `uEyeEmissive` of the five zombie classes (it drives every 0.5-tagged piece: eyes and spitter acid sacs); uniform writes only. Bosses are untouched: boss.js rewrites the per-rig glow colours every frame.

`aPart` = part id + a piece tag in its fraction (models.js `TAG`): .125 helmet dome, .25 helmet brim (magnet ring), .5 emissive (eyes, sacs), .75 shoulder (armour glint); shaders use `floor(aPart)` for the limb id and `fract(aPart)` for the tag.

Vertex attribute budget: `position`, `normal`, `color`, `aPart`, `aPivot` (5) + `instanceMatrix` (4) + `iAnim`, `iState` (2) = 11 of the 16 `MAX_VERTEX_ATTRIBS` guaranteed on Adreno 6xx / Mali-G5x–G7x / Apple GPUs (13 if a variant adds `instanceColor` + one more); `ModelLibrary.init()` asserts the count against `renderer.capabilities.maxAttributes` and throws at boot otherwise. Merging uses `mergeGeometries(parts, false)` from `three/addons/utils/BufferGeometryUtils.js` (`mergeBufferGeometries` no longer exists in r170); before merging, every part gets the same attribute set (`position`, `normal`, `color`, `aPart`, `aPivot`; `uv` deleted) via `ModelLibrary._tagPart(geo, partId, pivot, hex)`; the helper asserts attribute parity and throws at boot otherwise. Vertex shader chunk (replaces `begin_vertex` and `beginnormal_vertex`), per GDD §3.1/§6.1: limb rotation about `aPivot` by part using `limbParamsFor(classId)` uniforms, bob, recoil on part 6 (soldier variant only), sink on `iState.x`. Fragment chunk (after `color_fragment`): `diffuseColor.rgb *= iState.yzw; += max(0, 1 − (uTime − iAnim.z)·(1/ZOMBIES.common.hitFlash)); mix toward 0.12 by iState.x·0.6`. Soldier capacity 300 (geometry ~340 tris), walker 120, runner 60, crawler 40, brute 16, spitter 24.

### 6.5 `js/squad.js`

```js
export class Squad {
  constructor(ctx); init():this; reset():void; dispose():void; step(dt):void; render(alpha):void;
  get count():number;            // C (logical)
  get visible():number;          // V = min(C, Vmax)
  get x():number;                // squadX (also mirrored to ctx.world.squadX)
  get halfWidth():number; get depth():number; get centerZ():number /* depth/2; formation centre = (x, centerZ) */; get invulnerable():boolean; get armor():number;
  setCount(n, reason):void;                                  // absolute; emits squad:count
  addSoldiers(n, reason, originX = this.x, originZ = -2):void; // n > 0; spawn burst from origin; emits squad:spawn + squad:count; no-op once squad:dead fired (CHR-10)
  removeSoldiers(n, reason, x = this.x, z = 0):void;         // n > 0; plates absorb 1:1 for reasons 'bite' and 'spit' only (a brute swing is one call with n = zombies._bruteKills(C) ≤ 3, so ≤ 3 plates — round 7); for 'slam', 'charge', 'stomp', 'car', 'chew' plates are ignored and soldiers die directly; never called with 'gate' (see applyGate); emits squad:loss per soldier (cause = reason), squad:count, squad:dead at 0
  applyGate(op:'+'|'-'|'x'|'/', value:number, originX:number, originZ:number):{ before:number, after:number };
    // GDD §4.3 incl. "never below 1" — for a living squad only: after squad:dead (until reset) it returns { before: 0, after: 0 } with no events and no invulnerability (CHR-10; setCount(n, 'debug') still works and revives for QA); positive result: internally addSoldiers(after − before, 'gate', originX, originZ) (burst from the gate footprint);
    // negative / ÷: internally setCount(after, 'gate') with NO squad:loss events (the dissolve is rendered by squad rim-inward, SQUAD.dissolvePer100ms, triggered by the same call);
    // sets SQUAD.invulnOnPass (0.6 s) invulnerability; the single squad:count it emits has reason 'gate'
  setArmor(plates:number):void;                              // emits armor:change
  lockLane(laneX:number|null):void;                          // commit lock (called by gates); null releases
  setInvulnerable(seconds:number):void;                      // Infinity allowed (level end)
  shooterPosition(k:number, out:THREE.Vector3):THREE.Vector3; // muzzle of the k-th front-most visible soldier (k < min(V,40)); world coords
  slotWorld(i:number, out:THREE.Vector3):THREE.Vector3;       // world position of slot i (troops use centre/rear/front slots)
  nearestSoldierIndex(x, z):number;                          // outermost occupied slot nearest the attacker (for topple direction)
  intersectsCircle(x, z, r):boolean;                         // contact test against the formation AABB inflated by r
  bounds(out:{minX,maxX,minZ,maxZ}):typeof out;
  setFireVisual(k:number):void;                              // recoil (iAnim.w) for shooter k
  instances:CharacterInstances;                              // soldiers; shadows on
}
```

Owns: the soldier `CharacterInstances` (capacity 300), the slot table (`Float32Array` 300×2 + `frontOffset[301]`, built in `init()` per GDD §3.1), per-slot soldier state (`Float32Array` x, z, scale, spawnTime, deathTime; `Uint8Array` alive), the dissolve queue for gate losses. It does NOT own the count label (fx, §6.12) nor the lane decal (gates, §6.9). Reads: `ctx.input.targetX` ONLY (never `steerDx`, never `getSteerK`; engine integrates, §6.2), `ctx.quality.params.vmax`, `ctx.world.roadSpeed` (for lean), `ctx.profile.startSquad`. Writes: `ctx.world.squadX`, `squadHalfWidth`, `squadDepth`, `squadCount`, `invulnerable`. `step`: `target = input.targetX`; while `lockLane(laneX)` is active `target = clamp(target, laneX − 0.3, laneX + 0.3)` locally (never writes `ctx.input`); `squadX = lerpExp(squadX, target, SQUAD.steer.smoothRate (14), dt)` with `|Δ| ≤ SQUAD.steer.maxSpeed (12)·dt`; reflow `SQUAD.reflowDelay` after a death, spawn/death/dissolve animation timers, invulnerability countdown. `render`: writes V instance matrices (lerped slot positions, spawn scale, topple), `commit()`, then calls `ctx.fx.label.setWorldPos(x, SQUAD.label.y + SQUAD.label.yPerSqrt·√V, SQUAD.label.z)` — the only label call squad ever makes (never `tweenTo`/`snapTo`/`showMult`). `reset()`: count = `ctx.profile.startSquad` (emits `squad:count` reason `'start'`), all timers 0. Listens to `gear:start` / `gear:end` (magnet id only), `armor:change` and `level:start` ONLY to push `ctx.models.setGearGlow(magnet01, armor01)` (CHR-2; listeners store state, no gameplay mutation). Hot passes (round-2 CHR-13): the per-step scalars (clock, squadX, lean, invulnerability, lane x) live in one `Float64Array` and the per-soldier work runs in module functions over typed arrays (`stepCore`, `writeInstances`, JIT-warmed in `init()`), so step / render allocate nothing; a pending reconciliation runs at the end of `step` (after the record motion). Once `squad:dead` fired the count is final until `reset()`: `applyGate` and `addSoldiers` do nothing (CHR-10).

### 6.6 `js/troops.js`

```js
export class Troops {
  constructor(ctx); init():this; reset():void; dispose():void; step(dt):void; render(alpha):void;
  add(id:'troop.sniper'|'troop.medic'|'troop.flamer'|'troop.drone'|'troop.apc'):boolean;  // false when already present (caller pays 25 c)
  has(id):boolean; remove(id, reason):void; get list():string[];
  apcIntersects(x, z, r):boolean;      // true when the APC (2.4 × 3.4 m box 3.5 m ahead of the squad, round-2 CHR-9) overlaps the circle
  apcAbsorb(kills:number):number;      // consumes pool; returns kills absorbed (0 when no APC); explodes at 0 via zombies.damageInCircle + 'explosion' event
  get apcPool():number;
}
```

Owns ≤ 5 meshes from `models.buildTroopMesh`, their timers and the flame emitter (asks `fx.emit('flame', ...)` at 30/s). Reads `ctx.squad.slotWorld`, `ctx.zombies` queries (`highestHp`, `damageInCone`, `nearest`), `ctx.level.expectedSquad()` (medic cap), `ctx.level.waveHz()` (§6.16), `ctx.profile.damage`, `ctx.weapons.squadDps()/damageMult`, `ctx.world.bossActive`. Behaviours per GDD §8.3:

- Medic: every `TROOPS.medic.every` s, `ctx.squad.addSoldiers(max(min, ceil(pct·C)), 'medic')` while `C < level.expectedSquad()`, cap `capPerLevel` per level.
- Sniper is instant (NOT a bullet kind): every `every` s, `zombies.highestHp(out)` → `dmg = TROOPS.sniper.dmg · ctx.level.waveHz() · ctx.profile.damage`; `zombies.damage(out.id, dmg, out.x, 1.0, out.z, 'bullet')`; when no zombie qualifies and `ctx.world.bossActive`: `boss.damage(min(dmg · TROOPS.sniper.bossMult, TROOPS.sniper.bossCapFrac · boss.maxHp), 1, bx, 4.5, bz)` (round-3 verify: the 5 % cap — the wave hz carries endless's design multiple and pressure while the mini-boss HP does not, and one uncapped shot killed an endless mini-boss 0.5–2.4 s after it spawned, r3-balance-audit §5). Emits `troop:fire { id:'troop.sniper', x, y, z, tx, ty, tz }` (fx: red tracer line from the muzzle `y` to the hit height `ty` for one frame, crit damage number) and `weapon:fire { id:'troop.sniper' }` (audio: `crack`). Perch (round-2 CHR-9): the LEFT flank of the blob at mid-depth — x = squadX − (halfWidth + `TROOPS.anim.sniperSideMargin`), floored at `sniperMinX`, z = centerZ — on a 0.25 m crate, mesh scale 1.05.
- Flamethrower (round-2 CHR-12, support role): the cone runs in the shared zombie scan (`dps = TROOPS.flamer.dps · ctx.level.waveHz() · ctx.profile.damage`, `TROOPS.flamer.dps` 6) and is NON-LETHAL: it never takes a zombie below `TROOPS.anim.flamerHpFloor` (25 %) of `ZOMBIES[type].baseHp · hz`, and sets every zombie in reach burning (`setBurning(id, burnSeconds)`, +20 % bullet damage). Every `flamerVolleyEvery` (1.0) s a narrow jet damages ONE target (`flamerVolleyShare` × dps × every): lethal only against spitters / brutes holding at range, floored like the cone otherwise. Kills keep cause `'burn'`; bullets finishing a burning zombie count as `'bullet'`.
- Drone: `TROOPS.drone.rate` shots/s via `ctx.weapons.spawnBullet(x, y, z, dirX, dirZ, FIRE.bullet.speed, FIRE.bullet.life, weight, 'drone')` aimed at `zombies.nearest` (runners prioritised via `nearestOfType('runner')` first); `weight = TROOPS.drone.dpsFraction · weapons.squadDps() / (profile.damage · weapons.damageMult)` so each shot deals 5 % of squad DPS; drone bullets use the shared 256 pool, never hit gates or crates, and are excluded from the `FIRE.spawnRateCap` fold. Hover (round-2 CHR-9): `TROOPS.drone.hover` 5.2 m up, `TROOPS.anim.droneSideX` 1.2 m right of the blob centre (x ≤ `droneMaxX`), `droneAhead` 2.5 m ahead; each shot also emits `troop:fire { id:'troop.drone', … }` (fx ignores non-sniper ids today).
- APC: box `TROOPS.apc.width × length` at `ahead` (3.5) m in front of the squad; `apcIntersects`/`apcAbsorb` per §7; crushes crawlers each step (`damageInCircle(..., 9999, 'crush')` filtered by `typeOf === 'crawler'`); at pool 0 explodes: `zombies.damageInCircle(x, z, explodeRadius, squadDps()·explodeDpsSeconds, 'explosion')` + `explosion { kind:'apc' }` + `troop:remove`.

### 6.7 `js/zombies.js`

```js
export class Zombies {
  constructor(ctx); init():this; reset():void; dispose():void; step(dt):void; render(alpha):void;
  spawn(type:'walker'|'runner'|'crawler'|'brute'|'spitter', x:number, z:number, hp:number, opts?:{ guardEventId?:number, guardLane?:number, lunge?:boolean }):number; // stable id or -1 when the pool is full
  spawnWave(wave:{ pattern, units:Record<type, number>, spread?:number, x?:number, z?:number }, hpByType:Record<type, number>):number; // count spawned; pattern layouts per GDD §6.2; z default WORLD.spawnZ
  get alive():number; get aliveOfType(type):number;
  hitTestSegment(x0, z0, x1, z1, radius, out:{ id, x, y, z, t }):boolean;   // nearest alive zombie whose capsule intersects the swept segment; t ∈ [0,1]; z-bins (2 m) rebuilt each step
  hitTestSeg(seg:Float64Array /* [x0, z0, x1, z1] */, radius, out):boolean;   // round-2 QA ENE-16: same semantics / out shape, coordinates never cross the call as doubles; hitTestSegment forwards to it
  damage(id, amount, x, y, z, cause):boolean;        // returns killed; false for a stale/non-alive id (emits nothing); emits zombie:hit / zombie:die and, on a kill with cause ≠ 'bite'|'cleanup', coin:pickup { amount: ECONOMY.kill[type], source:'kill' }
  damageInCircle(x, z, r, amount, cause):number;     // AOE; returns kills
  damageInCone(x, z, dirX, dirZ, halfAngle, range, amount, cause):number;
  damageInLine(x, z0, halfWidth, amount, cause, maxTargets = Infinity, zMin = WORLD.spawnZ):number;  // zombies with |zx − x| ≤ halfWidth + radius and zMin ≤ zz ≤ z0, nearest first when maxTargets is finite; the crawler hittableZ rule applies; returns kills (railgun: Infinity; laser beam: Infinity from the beam origin)
  setBurning(id, seconds):void;                      // +TROOPS.flamer.burnBonus bullet damage (cause 'bullet' only), orange emissive
  highestHp(out:{ id, x, y, z, hp }):boolean;        // sniper target (any alive, z < 0)
  nearest(x, z, out:{ id, x, y, z }):boolean; nearestOfType(type, x, z, out):boolean;
  position(id, out:THREE.Vector3):THREE.Vector3; typeOf(id):string /* '' for a stale id */; hpOf(id):number;
  killAll(cause = 'cleanup'):void;                   // level end; no coins for 'cleanup'
  forEachAlive(fn:(id, type, x, z, hp) => void):void; // tools/debug only
}
```

Ids are stable handles, never raw instance indices: `slot` ∈ 0–259 is a pool slot with a per-type base (walker 0–119, runner 120–179, crawler 180–219, brute 220–235, spitter 236–259); `id = (generation << 12) | slot`, `generation` (per slot) incremented on each free, so a stale id never aliases a new zombie: every query validates the handle (`typeOf(id)` returns `''`, `damage(id)` returns `false`, `position` leaves `out` unchanged). The `CharacterInstances` index is separate: zombies keep `slotToInstance`/`instanceToSlot` `Int32Array`s per pool, updated in the `onMove(from, to)` callback of each `CharacterInstances`. `cause` ∈ `'bullet'|'bite'|'crush'|'burn'|'explosion'|'cleanup'`, exactly the `zombie:die.cause` vocabulary; rail, laser, drone and sniper use `'bullet'`, rockets and the APC blast `'explosion'`, the flamer `'burn'`, APC crush `'crush'`.

Per-zombie `state ∈ { alive, dying, free }`: only `alive` entries are inserted in the z-bins, returned by any query (`hitTestSegment`, `damageIn*`, `nearest*`, `highestHp`), counted by `alive`/`aliveOfType`, or tested for contact; `dying` entries only render (topple `ZOMBIES.common.death.topple` 0.55 s + sink 0.3 s, slot freed at `free` 1.4 s). `damage()` on a non-alive id returns `false` and emits nothing. Owns: five `CharacterInstances` (walker 120, runner 60, crawler 40, brute 16, spitter 24), SoA state per pool (`x, z, vx, vz, hp, maxHp, phase, state, timer, hitTime, deathTime, burnUntil, guardEventId, guardLane, generation`), the glob pool (32 `IcosahedronGeometry(0.25,0)` instances), the HP-bar `InstancedMesh` (capacity 260, `iHp` attribute, billboarded in the vertex shader), z-bins (`Int16Array` heads/next, 2 m bins from −80 to +20), telegraph decals (the brute's swipe + the spit arc from `fx.decal`; round 7, r7 brute look: the brute's telegraph is `fx.decal('swipe', x, z, 2·JUICE.telegraph.bruteReachM, Infinity, PALETTE.fx.brute, onTop = true)` — an amber frontal arc sized to its real reach, drawn over the formation — instead of the boss-red 3 m ring the tester called "Hulk slam"; `zombie:telegraph` kind 'ring' keeps its name (audio's roar) with radius = the reach; `ZOMBIES.brute.swing.ringR` is retired). Movement: `z += (roadSpeed + ownSpeed)·dt`; AI per GDD §6.1 (walker drift, runner zig-zag + re-target, crawler lunge, brute charge/stagger with `ZOMBIES.brute.stagger.freeze`, spitter hold + spit). Contact: when `squad.intersectsCircle(x, z, hitRadius)` and not `squad.invulnerable` → `troops.apcAbsorb` first, then `squad.removeSoldiers(bite, 'bite', x, z)`; regular zombies die (`cause 'bite'`, no coins); brutes swing on a `swing.cooldown` 1.0 s cooldown (one `removeSoldiers(k, 'bite')` call per swing, k = `_bruteKills(squad.count)` = clamp(round(`ZOMBIES.brute.swing.killsFrac` 0.06 · C), 1, bite 3) — round 7; the APC swing branch keeps `apcAbsorb(3)`) at the formation FRONT only (z ≤ `ZOMBIES.brute.maxContactZ`, −0.3 m): a flank contact first walks to the front at `contactHomingZ` (4 m/s, squad frame) and re-fills its ring there before its first swing, and after `maxSwings` (6) swings the brute staggers back `ZOMBIES.brute.backoffM` (2 m) and re-telegraphs instead of swinging (round-2 QA ENE-12); spit impacts call `squad.removeSoldiers(1|2, 'spit')` unless `|squad.x − x| ≥ 1.2 || |squad.centerZ − z| ≥ 1.2` (formation centre test, §7). Reads: `ctx.world`, `ctx.squad`, `ctx.troops`, `ctx.level.level` (spit kills from L10), `ctx.rng` (patterns, phases), `ctx.rngFx` (tints). Recycles any zombie with z > despawnZ (`cause 'cleanup'`).

### 6.8 `js/boss.js`

```js
export class BossSystem {
  constructor(ctx); init():this; reset():void; dispose():void; step(dt):void; render(alpha):void;
  get initSteps():number; initStep():number;   // round-3 verify (§4.1 step 9 slices): init() resolves the archetypes and QUEUES one rig per root (two for a twin root) instead of
                                               // building them (≈ 45–60 ms each at 4× CPU, the boot's longest frozen frame); initStep() builds the next one and returns how many are
                                               // left; spawn() first builds whatever is still queued (a host that never ran the slices).
                                               // Round 5 (r4-release §7 item 3): initSteps also counts one "dress rehearsal" per archetype after the rigs (_warmArchetype: a silent
                                               // spawn → intro steps → render → fight start → fight step → one hit → aimPoint → reset() on the hidden rig(s), every sink — events,
                                               // engine, squad, weapons, render, fx, zombies, troops, level — detached, ctx.world's boss mirror and the core light restored, no
                                               // random draw, nothing drawn or emitted), so the boss code paths are compiled and warm before the first arrival; reset() re-runs the
                                               // rehearsals one archetype per step() from 45 fixed steps after every level start (V8 may flush bytecode that has not run for a while)
  spawn(spec:{ id:string, archetype:keyof BOSSES.archetypes, hp:number /* total; twins: each twin gets hp·hpSplit, i.e. the pair holds 1.1× total */, phases?:Phase[], mini?:boolean, minionHp:Record<type, number>, x?:number, z?:number }):void;
    // name is taken from BOSSES.archetypes[archetype].name (the base's name for hulkTwins/colossusPrime); `base` is resolved recursively and `twins`, `hpSplit`, `shieldHits`, `hpMult` merged;
    // mini = endless mini-boss (no road slow-down, no letterbox, no dolly, no input lock); z default BOSSES.approachZ; runs the intro choreography unless mini
  get active():boolean; get hp():number; get maxHp():number; get phase():number; get x():number; get z():number; get inIntro():boolean;
  hitTestSegment(x0, z0, x1, z1, radius, out:{ x, y, z, t }):boolean;   // capsule r 1.6; twins: the nearest living twin; t ∈ [0,1]
  hitTestSeg(seg:Float64Array, radius, out):boolean;   // round-2 QA ENE-16 typed variant (hitTestSegment forwards to it). out.x / out.y are COSMETIC impact points (ENE-13):
                                                       // y = coreY · lerp(BOSSES.hitYRange) (0.45–0.95, rngFx), x = capsule-entry x jittered ±0.3·radius inside the capsule; out.z / out.t unchanged
  damage(amount:number, weight:number, x, y, z, crit = false):boolean;
    // weight = weighted hits for the shield gauge (bullets: w; rocket: WEAPONS.rocket.gateHits·w; rail: WEAPONS.railgun.gateHits·w; laser: WEAPONS.laser.gateHitsPerSec·w·dt — heavy weapons are weighted hits ×w, GDD §8.1, round-1 QA WPN-4; sniper: 1; drone: 0);
    // while the shield is up `amount` is ignored and `weight` accumulates (emits boss:shield on every change; the shield drops at shieldHits);
    // otherwise applies ctx.profile.bossDamage (and the juggernaut platesOff damageMult) to the twin nearest (x, z); emits boss:hit, boss:hp, boss:phase, boss:dead; returns dead
  aimPoint(out:THREE.Vector3):THREE.Vector3;   // core position of the nearest living twin (re-aim target for weapons/troops)
  intersectsSquad():boolean;
  skipIntro():void;                    // debug
}
```

Owns the rig `Group`(s) from `models.buildBossRig` (CPU-animated: limb swing by `uTime`, jaw hinge, plate detach, chain sway, core pulse), the boss point light (`render.lights.bossCore`), the shield gauge state (`hits`, `needed`, `up`, regen timer). Writes `ctx.world.bossActive/bossX/bossZ`: `step()` mirrors `active`, `x`, `z` every step (twins: the nearest living twin), `spawn()` sets `bossActive = true` immediately (intro included), `reset()` clears all three. Choreography (GDD §7, skipped when `mini`): on `spawn` → `engine.lockInput(true)`, `engine.freezeQuality(BOSSES.intro.dollyOut + hold + back)`, `squad.setInvulnerable(BOSSES.intro.invuln)` (2.6 s), `weapons.pauseTimers(true)` (weapon AND gear timers), `render.dollyTo(RENDER.dollyPos, aimPoint, dollyOut, hold, back)`, emits `boss:spawn` then `boss:intro` (ui: letterbox + name card; audio: roar), then `boss:fight` after `BOSSES.intro.fightAfter` (2.4 s) (`lockInput(false)`, `pauseTimers(false)`). All intro timers are theatre deadlines on `ctx.time.theatre` (fallback `ctx.time.now`; round-2 CORE-7 / ENE-17: a pause no longer burns them). Movement: approach from `approachZ` to hold `holdZ[phase]` (−12 / −10 / −8); the hold is `ownSpeed = −roadSpeed`. Attacks are data-driven from `phases[]` (§8.7 schema, `kills_total = kills + floor(L·killsPerLevel)`): each `attack` kind has a telegraph (`boss:attack` with `delay`) followed by the kill resolution against `squad`/`troops.apcAbsorb` per §7. Round 7 (r7 boss telegraph visibility): the telegraph decals that sit on the formation — the slam ring, the charge / lane stripe, the car band and the spit / rain acid marks — are `fx.decal(…, onTop = true)` (drawn without the depth test, so a big squad never hides the rim or the fill); the stomp ring at the boss stays depth-tested; audio sounds `warn` at every telegraph start (slam / charge / lane / car / stomp). Minion bursts (`BOSSES.minionBurst`, `lastHorde`, phase `spawn` actions) call `zombies.spawnWave({ pattern:'horde', units }, spec.minionHp)` — the boss never computes HP itself. Twins: one `boss:spawn`/`boss:hp`/`boss:dead` sequence for the pair; `hp`/`maxHp` are summed (HP bar shows the sum); phases are evaluated per twin; the two rigs stand at x ±2.2; `boss:dead` fires when the second twin dies. Colossus shield: `shieldPct·maxHp` raised by the phase-2 `shield` action, regenerates `shieldRegenPerSec·maxHp` per second while up (clamped to the shield size), drops at `shieldHits` weighted hits (round 7: 120; 160 for Prime — 3 s / 4 s at the 40 hits/s intake, never sooner than `shieldHits / 40` s); fx draws the gauge from `boss:shield`. Safety valve: after `valve.after` 45 s of fight the boss advances to z = `valve.z` (−3) and calls `squad.removeSoldiers(…, 'chew')` at `valve.perSec` accumulated per second. Death: `fx.slowmo(BOSSES.death.slowmo, slowmoTime)`, emits `boss:dead { id, x, z }` and `coin:pickup { amount: ECONOMY.bossCoins(L), source:'boss', x, y: 4.5, z }` (fx: 60-coin fountain), collapse `death.collapse` 1.8 s of theatre time (§2.2), then `level` finishes (§6.16). The core light (`render.lights.bossCore`, `BOSSES.anim.lightIntensity` 20) sits `BOSSES.anim.lightOffsetZ` (−1.0 m) along the rig facing, just inside the torso (round-2 QA ENE-18). Shadow casters are picked by mesh name (`SHADOW_PARTS`, which includes the skinned body `'skin'`, §6.4).

### 6.9 `js/gates.js`

```js
export class Gates {
  constructor(ctx); init():this; reset():void; dispose():void; step(dt):void; render(alpha):void;
  spawnEvent(ev:{ layout:'single'|'pair'|'triple', lanes:Lane[], z?:number, hint?:string, decorative?:boolean }):number;  // event id (stable handle); Lane per §8.7 with caps mandatory; z default WORLD.spawnZ
  hitTestSegment(x0, z0, x1, z1, out:{ gate:GateView, x, y, z, t }):boolean;
    // gates with state ∈ {'armed','locked'} and z < WORLD.gateStopZ only; XZ-only test against the panel footprint (lane width − 0.2) × depth GATE_RULES.panel.depth (0.4) centred at (gate.x, gate.z);
    // y is ignored (the panel is treated as a full-height wall for hit tests: bullets fly at y = 0.95, below the 1.0 m panel bottom); t ∈ [0,1] = entry point into the panel box along the segment; out.y = FIRE.bullet.y
  hitTestSeg(seg:Float64Array /* [x0, z0, x1, z1] */, out):boolean;   // round-2 QA GAT-9: same semantics / out; hitTestSegment stays as a wrapper
  applyHit(gateId:number, weight:number, x, y, z):void;     // GDD §4.2 incl. intake cap; ignored (returns) when state ∉ {'armed','locked'} or z ≥ WORLD.gateStopZ or the gate is decorative; emits gate:hit / gate:max / gate:flip / gate:step and coin:pickup {amount: GATE_RULES.plus.coinPerHit, source:'max'} per hit past MAX (cap GATE_RULES.plus.maxCoins per gate)
  get activeEvent():{ id, layout, z, lanes:GateView[] }|null;  // nearest unpassed event
  laneCenters(layout):Float32Array;                          // [−2, 2] / [0] / [−2.7, 0, 2.7]
  pulseEvent(eventId:number, seconds:number):boolean;        // round 7 (r7 teaching:0 E; contract C → D): while a held lesson hint points at an event, its
    // frames and glows pulse (emissive + GATE_RULES.fx.attnGain·(0.5 + 0.5·sin(attnRadPerS·t))) for `seconds` of theatre time (ctx.time.theatre);
    // seconds ≤ 0 stops it; it ends by itself at the pass / miss; eventId = the spawnEvent handle; false for a stale / resolved / decorative event
}
```

Ids: gate ids and event ids are stable handles like zombie ids: `id = (generation << 12) | slot`, slot 0–11 (gates) / 0–7 (events), generation incremented per free; `applyHit`/views with a stale id are ignored. Owns: pool of 12 gates (SoA: `op, value, base, cap, flipCap, stepCost, mult, hits, intakeBudget, state, x, z, lane, layout, eventId, item, maxed, flipped, popTime, ring, tintT, divStage, decorative, generation`), four instanced draws — `frames` InstancedMesh (12, `iFrame` vec4 = typeIdx, tintT, maxed, emissiveBoost), `panels` InstancedMesh (12, `PlaneGeometry(3.6, 2.4)`, `materials.gatePanel()` ShaderMaterial transparent/DoubleSide/depthWrite false, per-instance `iPanel` vec4 = tintIdx, stripeMix, flash, ring01 + 2·reach and `iScale` float = 1 | 2.4/3.6 for triples; the ring gauge and the MAX badge are drawn by the panel shader from `iPanel.w` and `iPanel.x`. Round 7 (r7 teaching:4): the gauge also runs on + gates (spawn value → MAX; 0 once maxed) and − gates (spawn value → the flip; a red panel shows its empty track from spawn); a ÷ gate's gauge is full on its ×1 rung (r7 explain:5: `DIV_TOP_FINAL` = `GATE_RULES.div.stepFraction.flipBlock ≥ 1`, one armed window never pays the ×1 → ×2 flip; also for a lane whose flip target is ×1); `reach` (+2 on w) = at its weighted-hits/s EMA (`GATE_RULES.reach.rateTauS`, above `minRate`, capped at the intake budget) the gate reaches its goal — red: the flip, +: MAX, ×: its next step, ÷: the ×1 rung — before `WORLD.gateStopZ`; the shader then colours the band the way the gate is heading (red → the + blue, + → gold, × / ÷ → white) and pulses its head; same program, no new variant), `glows` InstancedMesh (12, additive), `glyphs` InstancedMesh (60 = 12 × 5 quads, digit atlas, `iGlyph/iPopTime/iColor`; the "MAX" badge text uses 3 of a maxed gate's quads) — and the lane highlight decal id. Per-gate hit flash (`iPanel.z` = +1.0 decaying over 120 ms) and flip tint lerp (`tintT`) are per-instance values written by `gates.render()`, never shared uniforms. `step`: scroll `z += roadSpeed·dt`; state `far → armed` at z ≥ `WORLD.gateArmZ` (frame lights up); `armed → locked` at z ≥ `WORLD.gateCommitZ` (−4): `squad.lockLane(centre of the lane nearest the steering target)`, `gate:commit` — round 7 (r7 controls:1): the lane choice (this commit, the lane highlight and the unlocked pass fallback) reads `ctx.input.targetX` (intent; `squad.x` only without an input object), not the squad centre still on its way there, so a dodge counts from the first frame of the key press or drag and the squad slides into the committed lane inside the 4 m commit window; physical tests (zombie contact, boss attacks, crates, acid) stay on `squad.x`; hits are still accepted while `locked` until z ≥ `WORLD.gateStopZ` (−1.5), so the applied value equals the value the player saw; at z ≥ 0 — unless the squad is dead (C = 0 or `level.phase === 'lost'`), in which case every lane is missed (`gate:miss` per lane; no `applyGate`, no `gate:pass`, no gate coins, no item grant; round-2 QA GAT-6) — the committed lane **passes** (an event that never locked: the lane nearest the steering target): `squad.applyGate(op, value, gate.x, gate.z)` (item lanes: `pickups.grantItem(item, gate.x, 0)` + `squad.setInvulnerable(SQUAD.invulnOnPass)`), emits `gate:pass { op, value, before, after, x, z, other }` (item lanes: `op:'item', value:0, before:C, after:C`; `other` = the best un-passed lane's `{ op, value, wouldBe }`) and `coin:pickup { amount: maxed ? GATE_RULES.coins.max : GATE_RULES.coins.pass, source:'gate', x: gate.x, y: 2.2, z: gate.z }`; the other lanes emit `gate:miss`; `squad.lockLane(null)` after the pass. Lane highlight (single owner = gates): at z ≥ `WORLD.laneDecalZ` (−10) gates calls `id = fx.decal('lane', laneX, gate.z, 4, Infinity, laneColour)` for the lane whose centre is nearest the steering target (round 7: `input.targetX`), re-targets it with `fx.moveDecal(id, laneX, gate.z)` each step (moves with the player until commit, then fixed on the committed lane) and `fx.removeDecal(id)` on pass/miss. Decorative gates (title idle scene): never arm, never commit, never pass (`far → missed` at z ≥ 0), accept no hits, emit no events, show no decal. Round 5 (r4-release issue 5): once behind the title squad (z > `world.squadDepth + WORLD.idleGateSink.z0Pad`) every part of a decorative gate is lowered `idleGateSink.rate` m per metre of z (an instance-matrix y offset in `render()`; the opaque road hides what has sunk — every gate part is depth-tested), and the event is freed once its tallest part (the MAX pill top, panel top + `badgeLift` + `icon / 2`) is under the road: the gate never slides through the title camera's near field (the rig sits 1.7 m above the top bar). Value text is rebuilt into glyph quads only when the value changes (`markDirty`). Round-3 FTUE label yield: `render()` projects fx's count-label plate (`fx.label` x / y / z / width / plateH) and each gate's op + digit block into view-space tan extents with `render.camera.matrixWorld`; while they overlap (× 1.1) during playing / paused, that gate's text fades out over 0.1 s (glyph colour × fade³, the glyph shader's coverage knee does the rest) and back in once they part — so the lane the squad passes no longer prints its value through the count ("69" over "+15" read "+695"); the MAX badge and the other lanes are untouched; per-gate `_textFade` reset at spawn. Round 7 (r7 controls:2): before the commit only the pass lane (the lane nearest the steering target, or the locked lane) may yield — a wide landscape count label used to hide both lanes' values from 7–9 m, exactly while the other lane's (still changing) value decides a dodge; from the commit on any overlapping text yields as before. An item lane's pictogram yields by the same rule (r7 S5: the flamer's flame sat behind the count at the pass and read as a burn status; the icon shader fades coverage with `iColor`). Readability sizes per GDD §4.1. Reads `ctx.world.roadSpeed`, `ctx.input.targetX` (round 7), `ctx.squad.x`, `ctx.level.phase` (idle), `ctx.rngFx` (spark jitter), `ctx.time.theatre` (pulseEvent deadlines). Caps always come from the script (`gateDefaults` fills them at generation, §6.17); gates never computes caps and never reads `ctx.level.expectedSquad()`. Hints: `ev.hint` is forwarded to `level.showHint(id)` at spawn; `Lane.hintOnHit/hintOnPass/hintIfOtherBetter` are evaluated by `level` from `gate:hit`/`gate:pass` (§6.16) — gates only carries them in the view.

### 6.10 `js/pickups.js`

```js
export class Pickups {
  constructor(ctx); init():this; reset():void; dispose():void; step(dt):void; render(alpha):void;
  spawnCrate(item:string, x:number, z = WORLD.spawnZ):void;      // item: 'weapon.*' | 'gear.*' | 'troop.*' | 'coins'
  hasClosedCrateAhead():boolean;   // r7 fix round 2: a closed crate is on the road ahead of the squad (z < 0) — level's crate hint row (r7 fix round 3: and its release while the hint is out)
  spawnCoinLine(x:number, count = 8, z = WORLD.spawnZ):void;
  grantItem(item:string, x:number, z:number):void;               // routes to weapons.storeWeapon (round 6; a full slot → duplicate: true, weapons pays the refund) / weapons.startGear / troops.add; 'coins' → coin:pickup { amount: PICKUPS.crateCoins, source:'crate' }; a duplicate troop (troops.add returned false) → coin:pickup { amount: PICKUPS.duplicateCoins, source:'duplicate' }; always emits item:pickup { item, x, z, duplicate } (fx answers with iconFlight)
  hitTestSegment(x0, z0, x1, z1, out:{ id, x, y, z, t }):boolean; // closed crates only; XZ box PICKUPS.crate.size, y ignored; t ∈ [0,1]
  hitTestSeg(seg:Float64Array, out):boolean;                     // round-2 QA GAT-9 typed variant (hitTestSegment wraps it)
  hit(id):void;                                                  // PICKUPS.crate.hitsToOpen (5) hits open the crate → emits crate:open { item, x, z } and immediately calls grantItem(item, x, z); crates touched while closed open and grant the same way
  setMagnet(radius:number):void;                                 // GEAR.magnet.baseRadius (1.2) default, GEAR.magnet.radius (5.0) with magnet
}
```

Ids: crate ids are stable handles (`(generation << 12) | slot`, slot 0–7). Owns: crate pool 8 (`BoxGeometry(0.8)` + emissive seams, lid pop animation), coin `InstancedMesh` 64 (spinning, magnet pull at `PICKUPS.magnetPull` 12 m/s inside the radius). There is no dropped item icon: the item is granted at the moment the crate opens (GDD §8/J12 — the icon rising 1.5 m and flying to the HUD is `fx.iconFlight` from `item:pickup`). Collection: a coin within `squad.intersectsCircle(x, z, magnetRadius)` is pulled and collected at ≤ 0.8 m → `coin:pickup { amount: PICKUPS.coinLine.value, source:'pile', x, y, z }` (fx flies the coin to the HUD; level accounts it); a crate within `PICKUPS.collectRadius` (1.5) opens on contact. `step` is not called in the title idle scene. After `squad:dead` (round-2 QA GAT-6) pickups ignores contact, hits and `grantItem`, skips the magnet capture, and coins already flying to the squad vanish without paying. Crates own a shadow `customDepthMaterial` (`materials.depthFor({ instanced: true })`, round-2 QA GAT-8); a crate hit flashes the seam colour × `PICKUPS.crate.hitFlashGain`, re-triggered at most every `hitFlashRetrigger` s (GAT-10).

### 6.11 `js/weapons.js`

```js
export class Weapons {
  constructor(ctx); init():this; reset():void; dispose():void; step(dt):void; render(alpha):void;
  get weapon():string|null; get weaponTimeLeft():number; get gear():Record<string, number /*seconds left; Infinity for gear.armor and gear.magnet*/>;
  storeWeapon(id):boolean;                              // round 6: weapon pickups (pickups.grantItem) → FIFO slot of PICKUPS.weaponSlots (2); emits weapon:stored { id, slots }; slot full → coin:pickup { amount: PICKUPS.replaceRefund, source:'refund', x: squad.x, y: 1, z: 0 }, returns false
  requestFire():boolean;                                // round 6: the player's FIRE (ui: #btn-fire pointerdown, Space, gamepad A); false while a weapon is active or nothing is stored (the stored one waits); else the FIFO head starts via startWeapon at the top of the next step
  get storedCount():number; storedId(i):string|null; get canFire():boolean;   // round 6: slot model for ui / the smoke bot
    // stored weapons survive the boss intro / fight; when level.phase turns 'won' each unused one pays PICKUPS.replaceRefund ('refund', before the commit); 'lost' (squad dead — every endless run) clears them silently (QA LVL-20: no refunds once lost); reset() clears them
  startWeapon(id:'weapon.shotgun'|'weapon.minigun'|'weapon.rocket'|'weapon.railgun'|'weapon.laser'):void;  // replaces + refreshes; when replacing an active weapon emits coin:pickup { amount: PICKUPS.replaceRefund, source:'refund', x: squad.x, y: 1, z: 0 }; emits weapon:start
  startGear(id:'gear.armor'|'gear.magnet'|'gear.chip'|'gear.booster'):void;
    // armor → squad.setArmor(min(C, GEAR.armor.platesCap)); re-pickup refills plates, no refund; duration Infinity
    // magnet → pickups.setMagnet(GEAR.magnet.radius); re-pickup → coin:pickup { amount: PICKUPS.duplicateCoins, source:'duplicate' }; duration Infinity
    // chip / booster → GEAR.chip.duration / GEAR.booster.duration timers; re-pickup refreshes the timer, no refund
    // emits gear:start { id, duration } (Infinity for armor/magnet)
  pauseTimers(on:boolean):void;                         // pauses the weapon timer AND the chip/booster gear timers (boss intro)
  get fireRateMult():number; get damageMult():number;   // from gear (chip / booster); damageMult does not apply to gate hits
  squadDps():number;                                    // C · d · f · fireRateMult · damageMult · FIRE.accuracy
  bulletWeight():number;                                // max(1, C/FIRE.weightDiv) · targetRate/spawnRate
  spawnBullet(x, y, z, dirX, dirZ, speed, life, weight, kind:'rifle'|'shotgun'|'minigun'|'drone'):boolean;   // false when the 256 pool is full; the sniper is NOT a bullet (instant, §6.6)
  get liveBullets():number;
}
```

Owns: bullet SoA (`Float32Array` x, y, z, px, pz, dx, dz, speed, life, weight; `Uint8Array` kind; capacity `FIRE.bullet.pool` 256), per-shooter phase timers (40), tracer `InstancedMesh` (capacity 256 allocated once, `BoxGeometry(0.04, 0.04, 0.9)`, `instanceColor` HDR per kind, drawn `count` ≤ `params.tracers`), muzzle-flash pool (64 quads, 33 ms = GDD J1 "2 frames", `FIRE.muzzleFlash.ms`; round-1 QA WPN-6), rocket pool 8 (mesh + smoke emitter), rail bolt mesh, laser beams (≤ 8 cylinders), the power-up and gear timers, module-level scratch `out` objects per target type for the swept tests (§7). `step` (GDD §3.3 and §8.1): shooters = min(C, `FIRE.shooters`); for each shooter whose timer expires, spawn from `squad.shooterPosition(k)` + `FIRE.muzzleOffset`, direction −Z with ±`FIRE.bullet.spreadDeg` yaw (`rngFx`), or toward `boss.aimPoint` when `ctx.world.bossActive` (intro included) and the boss is within `FIRE.bossAimRange` — except in a step where `zombies.nearest(squad.x, −3)` lies ≤ `FIRE.bossAimYieldM` (6 m) ahead of the front row (−6 ≤ z ≤ +0.5) and within `world.squadHalfWidth` + 1 m of `squad.x`: then bullets, rockets and laser beams fly straight into that contact threat (round-2 QA WPN-7, GDD §3.3 "minions on the line still soak"); boss-aimed bullets get a cosmetic vertical velocity so the tracer climbs from `FIRE.bullet.y` to the middle of `BOSSES.hitYRange`·coreY at the capsule (round-2 QA WPN-9; hit tests stay XZ); `squad.setFireVisual(k)`; coalesced `shot` event per step. Hardening (WPN-7): a non-finite `boss.aimPoint` or a fire configuration whose per-shooter rate, spawn scale or weight is not finite and > 0 falls back to the base rifle configuration for that step (`warnOnce('weapons.fireConfig')`); `ctx.debug.shotsPerSec` (1 s windows) and `ctx.debug.laserBeams` feed the overlay, and under `?debug=1` `warnOnce('weapons.noShots')` fires when C > 0 in `playing` and nothing was fired for 1 s while ≥ 2 shots were due. Bullet integration: `px = x; pz = z; x += dx·speed·dt; z += dz·speed·dt; life −= dt`, then the swept test in priority order (§7). Rockets: on the first candidate hit or at `WEAPONS.rocket.maxRange`: `zombies.damageInCircle(x, z, splash, squadDps()·dpsSeconds, 'explosion')`, `boss.damage(squadDps()·dpsSeconds, WEAPONS.rocket.gateHits·w, x, 4.5, z)` if within splash + 1.6 m, `gates.applyHit(gate, WEAPONS.rocket.gateHits·w, …)` for an armed/locked panel within 1.8 m (x) and 1.0 m (z), emits `explosion { kind:'rocket' }` + `weapon:fire { id:'weapon.rocket' }`. Rail: `zombies.damageInLine(x, 0, halfWidth, squadDps()·dpsSeconds, 'bullet')`, `boss.damage(…, WEAPONS.railgun.gateHits·w, …)` if |bossX − x| ≤ 2.0, `applyHit(g, WEAPONS.railgun.gateHits·w, …)` on every armed/locked gate whose lane contains x, emits `weapon:fire { id:'weapon.railgun' }`. Laser: per beam each step `damageInLine(bx, bz, halfWidth, dps_beam·dt, 'bullet')`, `boss.damage(dps_beam·dt, WEAPONS.laser.gateHitsPerSec·w·dt, bx, 4.5, bz)` when the beam crosses the boss capsule, `applyHit(gate, gateHitsPerSec·w·dt, …)` for the panel the beam touches (w = max(1, C/`FIRE.weightDiv`) on every heavy-weapon gate/shield hit, GDD §8.1, round-1 QA WPN-4). Segment tests use the typed queries `hitTestSeg` (§7, round-2 QA WPN-8). Reads `ctx.profile.fireRate/damage`, `ctx.squad`, `ctx.zombies`, `ctx.boss`, `ctx.gates`, `ctx.pickups`, `ctx.world.bossActive/bossX/bossZ/squadHalfWidth`, `ctx.quality.params.tracers`. `step` is not called in the title idle scene.

### 6.12 `js/fx.js`

```js
export class Fx {
  constructor(ctx); init():this; reset():void; dispose():void; update(dt, unscaledDt):void;
  // GPU particles: CPU writes attributes once per spawned particle (ring buffer), zero per-frame cost
  emit(kind:'spark'|'blood'|'muzzle'|'smoke'|'gold'|'shard'|'confetti'|'dust'|'flame'|'splash'|'coinSparkle', x, y, z, count, opts?:{ vx, vy, vz, spread, size, colorIdx, life, gravity }):void;
  burst(preset:string, x, y, z):void;      // presets in JUICE.bursts: hitSpark 4, gateSpark 3, blood 8, bloodSmall 6, max 12 gold, flip 16 blue, rocket 30, splat 10, bossDeath 200 confetti, coinFountain 60
  // digit-atlas quads
  floater(text:string, x, y, z, colorHex:number, opts?:{ rise = JUICE.floater.rise, life = JUICE.floater.life, size = JUICE.floater.size }):void;   // 24 floaters × 5 glyph quads (round 7: was 4) ("−1", "−3", "+17", "+n" medic); deaths within JUICE.floater.mergeMs (round 7: 300 ms) merge into one "−k", lossScale × size (lossScaleBig from k ≥ lossBigFrom)
  damageNumber(value:number, x, y, z, colorHex:number):void;                                      // 32 numbers × 5 quads; brutes/bosses only. Round 7 (r7 "9999"): a shown number is never a clamped value — a merge that would pass 9 999 shows its total so far and starts a new number, a single hit above 9 999 reads "9999+"
  label:{ setCount(n:number):void; tweenTo(n:number):void; snapTo(n:number):void; showMult(text:string, seconds:number):void; setWorldPos(x, y, z):void };  // the squad count label: 6 digit quads + 3 badge quads, world billboard, screen-space SQUAD.label.px; GDD §5 behaviour lives here and is driven by events (below)
    // round-3 FTUE: read-only fields x, y, z, hasPos, height (glyph quad, m), width / plateH (the backing plate, m; written each frame
    // the label is drawn) — gates' label-yield test reads them; the label (and plate) fade out over LABEL_HIDE_S while render.dollying
    // (the boss-intro dolly drops the squad under the letterbox, which clipped it into a stray digit) and back in when it ends
  // camera / time / screen
  addTrauma(t:number):void; getShake(out:{ x:number, y:number, roll:number }):void;   // shake = T², decay JUICE.shake.decay, clamp JUICE.shake.clamp; ×0 under reduced motion
  slowmo(scale:number, seconds:number):void;    // → ctx.engine.setTimeScale (clamped to WORLD.timeScaleMin)
  flash(colorHex:number, seconds:number, alpha = 1):void;   // full-screen via ui.flash(); reduced motion → 150 ms dim
  vignette(colorHex:number, seconds:number, alpha?:number):void;   // → ui.vignette(colorHex, seconds, alpha) — round 7 (contract C → D): alpha 0..1 scales the edge alpha (omitted = full)
    // round 7 (r7 teaching:1): a squad:loss with cause 'bite' / 'spit' pulses it red for JUICE.flashes.biteVignette s at alpha
    // min(1, biteVignetteAlpha + biteVignetteAlphaPer·(k − 1)) (k = the merged "−k"), at most once per biteVignetteGap s (real time)
  fovKick(deg:number, seconds:number):void; getFovKick():number;   // read by render.update
  setDesaturate(t01:number):void;               // → ctx.render.setDesaturate(t01) (game-over grade); fx also calls render.gameOverCamera(true) on level:failed and (false) on level:start
  // decals + shards + flights
  decal(kind:'ring'|'arc'|'lane'|'splat'|'xband'|'swipe', x, z, size:number, seconds:number /* Infinity = until removed */, colorHex:number, onTop?:boolean):number;  moveDecal(id, x, z):void;  decalProgress(id, t01):void;  removeDecal(id):void;
    // round 7: 'swipe' = the brute's frontal arc (JUICE.telegraph); onTop (default false) = a telegraph that sits on the formation (slam ring,
    // charge stripe, car band, acid marks, brute swipe): drawn by `fx.decalsTop` without the depth test, so a big squad never hides it
  shatter(x, z, width, height, colorHex, towardCamera:boolean):void;   // JUICE.shatter.shards (24) shards for JUICE.shatter.seconds from the shard pool (96)
  coinFlight(x, y, z, amount:number):void;      // 3D → screen flight to the HUD coin icon (DOM sprite), icon punch; ≤ 16 concurrent, extra amounts merge
  iconFlight(item:string, x, y, z):void;        // GDD J12: icon rises PICKUPS.iconRise (1.5 m) then flies to the HUD pill (DOM sprite from textures.iconDataURL);
    // round 7 (r7 S5 "burn icon on the count"): it slides sideways clear of the count label's plate (offset decided once per flight, eased in over
    // the first 30 % of the rise), and a troop's icon (no HUD pill: it flew to an empty spot at the top) drops into the squad instead
  haptic(pattern:number|number[]):void;         // guarded, respects settings.haptics; patterns from JUICE.haptics
}
```

Owns: two particle `InstancedMesh`es (additive: spark/muzzle/gold/confetti/flame/coinSparkle; normal: blood/smoke/splash/shard), each allocated ONCE at `QUALITY_TIERS.high.particles / 2` (512) with attributes `p0 (vec3) v0 (vec3) birth life size colorIdx kind`; the live cap `ctx.quality.params.particles / 2` is enforced by `emit()` (oldest-ring-slot reuse) and `mesh.count` is clamped to it; on `quality:change` only the cap changes. The vertex shader computes `t = uTime − birth`, `p = p0 + v0·t + 0.5·g(kind)·t²`, billboard from camera basis uniforms, size and alpha over life, and collapses expired particles to zero size. `floaters` InstancedMesh (24 × 5 = 120 digit-atlas quads) and `numbers` InstancedMesh (32 × 5 = 160 quads; round 7: 5 quads each, so a 4-digit "−k" keeps its sign and an overflowing hit has room for "9999+"), both billboarded in-shader with `iPopTime`; the count label (6 + 3 quads) with its backing plate `fx.labelPlate` and HDR clamp `fx.labelClamp` (round-2 QA FX-10: MIN-blended cap under and around the plate; both hide with the label; renderOrders numbers 40, clamp 41, plate 42, floaters 43, label 44 — integers only, a fractional renderOrder deoptimises every module through the shared InstancedMesh map); label and plate live ONLY on `render.overlayLayer` (drawn after bloom, §6.3), the clamp and the floaters on layers 0 + overlay (drawn in both passes) (round-2 cross-group, FX-10 residual: with 3+ HDR laser beams behind the label the digit / plate contrast went from ≈ 3.4:1 to 5.85:1 in every laser frame, `docs/build/qa2/cross.md`); `decals` InstancedMesh (`JUICE.caps.decals` 24, `iDecal` vec4 = kind, progress, size, colorIdx; additive; procedural ring/arc/lane stripe/splat/x-band/swipe in-shader; 1 draw) and, round 7 (r7 boss telegraph visibility), `decalsTop` — the same slots' onTop decals drawn by a clone of the decal material with depthTest off (same shaders → the same program, shared uniform objects; renderOrder 1, so particles and numbers still draw over it; 1 more draw while a telegraph is up). The acid arc (r7 teaching:7) has a diagonal × centre, every other rim dash in `PALETTE.fx.hazard` and a 0.10 base disc (it read as a heal pad); the shard pool (96 `BoxGeometry(0.5, 0.4, 0.05)` instances, CPU-integrated); 16 DOM coin sprites + 4 DOM icon sprites. Event subscriptions implement GDD §12 J1–J22 exactly (the gate panel pop and emissive flash are done by `gates` itself; the hit flash by `zombies`/`squad` through `setHit`), plus: `boss:shield` → ring gauge decal at the boss core (`decal('ring')` kept while `up`, `decalProgress(hits/needed)`); `item:pickup` → `iconFlight`; `coin:pickup` → `coinFlight` (J19); `level:failed` → `setDesaturate` over 0.8 s + `render.gameOverCamera(true)`; `squad:count` reason `'gate'` with delta < 0 → nothing (squad renders the dissolve).

Count label driver (fx is the ONLY caller of `tweenTo`/`snapTo`/`showMult`/`setCount`): `squad:count` reason `'start'` → `label.setCount(count)`; `squad:count` with reason ∉ {`'start'`, `'gate'`} → `delta > 0` ? `label.tweenTo(count)` : `label.snapTo(count)`; `squad:count` with reason `'gate'` is ignored by the label (it arrives before `gate:pass`, which carries the op); `gate:pass` → op `'x'`, or `'/'` when `gate.flipped`: `label.showMult('x' + value, JUICE.label.multShow)` then `label.tweenTo(after)` after `multShow` (0.3 s); op `'+'`: `label.tweenTo(after)`; op `'-'` or un-flipped `'/'`: `label.snapTo(after)`; op `'item'`: nothing. Position comes from `squad.render()` via `setWorldPos` every frame. `reset()` clears everything and the trauma. The label is drawn only in `playing` and `paused` (the boss-death theatre runs in `playing`): entering `complete` / `gameover` fades it, its badge and plate out over 0.25 s, any other state hides it at once (round-2 QA FX-11). Damage numbers of a tracked target (the boss, a brute) use a per-target slot grid (2 columns × `JUICE.damageNumber.bossSlots` rows, `bossSide` / `bossRise` / `bossScale`, ≤ `JUICE.caps.bossNumbers` live) anchored on the target and riding along with it (round-2 QA FX-12). Round 4 (r3 FTUE residual 4): the boss's own grid has `JUICE.damageNumber.bossRows` (2) rows per side, and its accumulator's merge window is max(`mergeSeconds`, `JUICE.floater.life` / (2 × bossRows)) with a jitter that only lengthens it, so a boss slot is never reused before its number has faded (brutes unchanged). `troop:fire` (sniper) draws the red beam from `y` to `ty` (CHR-14 / FX-13). On the first `title` state fx runs a one-time JIT / type-feedback warm-up (`_primeFeedback`, ≈ 30 ms, audio ticks muted) over every pool branch, then `reset()` (round-2 QA FX-14).

### 6.13 `js/audio.js` — class shape (full cue contract in §11)

```js
export class AudioSystem {
  constructor(ctx); init():this; dispose():void; update(unscaledDt):void;
  get unlocked():boolean; get needsGesture():boolean;
  unlock():void;        // called synchronously by engine's gesture listeners (§6.2) on EVERY gesture until unlocked. Round-2 AUD-8: the context and the whole graph are created in init() (boot step 7, 'suspended' until a gesture; a construction failure there is retried once here, inside the gesture); unlock() primes old iOS and calls context.resume() on every invocation (≈ 0.4 ms); emits audio:unlocked only when context.state === 'running' (checked in the resume promise, on statechange and by a cheap poll in update() — a context already running at boot emits it too); statechange to 'suspended'/'interrupted' sets needsGesture = true (engine re-arms its listeners)
  play(name:string, opts?:{ volume = 1, pitch = 1, pos?:{ x, z }, detune?:number }):void;   // fire-and-forget; queued and started in update() with AudioContext scheduling; silently dropped before unlock
  setMusic(mode:'none'|'menu'|'level'|'runup'|'boss'):void;
  duck(db:number, seconds:number):void;   // seconds = 0 → immediate; duck(0, 0) restores
  setVolumes({ sound, music }):void;  setMuted(m:boolean):void;
  setTimeScale(s:number):void;         // rate r = MUSIC.rate.base + MUSIC.rate.perScale·s: AudioBufferSourceNodes → playbackRate, OscillatorNodes (no playbackRate) → detune = 1200·log2(r); the music scheduler scales its beat interval by 1/r.
                                       // Round-2 AUD-4: sounding sources → cancel + hold + setTargetAtTime(new, now, MUSIC.paramSmoothS); sources scheduled to start later (cue delayMs, layer delays, music lookahead) get their start value rewritten at their start time; cues flagged AUDIO_CUES.*.noTimeScale (fanfare, chime3, pass_up (round 3), ui_*) are built at rate 1 and skipped
}
```

`visibilitychange` hidden → `context.suspend()`; visible → `resume()` attempt, and if the state is not running within 500 ms `needsGesture = true`. The music scheduler runs from `setInterval(25)` started at the context's first 'running' (independent of rAF; round-2 AUD-8) with `MUSIC.lookaheadMs` lookahead; `update()` only starts queued cues and resets voice counters. Graph (round-2 AUD-5, = GDD §13): buses (sfx / gun → `_gunDuck` / music) → compressor (knee `MUSIC.compressor.knee` 6) → safety limiter (`MUSIC.limiter`) → WaveShaper soft clip (identity up to |x| 0.8) → master mute gain → destination. Mode switches (round-2 AUD-12): `level:start`, `boss:fight` and entering a menu state from another mode restart the loop at bar 0 (pending notes released, sounding notes faded over `MUSIC.modeSwitch.crossfadeMs`); every other switch lands on the next beat (`MUSIC.modeSwitch.beatSteps`). `boss:dead` holds a −10 dB dip (`MUSIC.duck.bossDead`) and then fades the music to 'none' (AUD-13); gate passes, stings and flips duck the gun bus (`MUSIC.duck.gunOnReward`, AUD-10). Round-3 AUD-10: compressor → `_bedDuck` (`MUSIC.duck.bedOnReward`, dipped on a positive `gate:pass`) → limiter, and cues with `bus: 'reward'` (`pass_up`, `hup`) sum into `_rewardBus` (`MUSIC.buses.reward` · sound fader), which feeds the limiter directly (no bus compression). A fresh music start after a full stop releases the old loop's notes still ringing under the closed fade, and the drone's LFO depth fades with the drone (round 3). Round 4: the J5 `flip` (cue key `noAtten`: panned, never distance-attenuated) and the J13 `sting_*` are reward-bus cues too, and their listeners (`_onGateFlip`, `_onWeaponStart`) dip `_bedDuck` like a positive pass; a reward moment starting < `MUSIC.duck.rewardStack[1]` s after another plays `rewardStack[0]` dB. Faders are linear in amplitude (`faderGain(v) = v / 100`; 50 = −6 dB), with `MUSIC.buses.music` −15.94 so the default 80 is unchanged. First-use warm-up: right after the graph, `requestIdleCallback` slices (`MUSIC.warm`; a timer without it) create one OscillatorNode per type (the context caches the wave tables) and dry-build every `AUDIO_CUES` recipe through `_startCue` into an unconnected `_warmSink` (`_warming`: no source started, no live bookkeeping, local randomness), releasing each at once; WaveShapers are pooled per drive (`MUSIC.queue.shaperPool`, `voice.nodeDrive`); a cue with `delayMs` ≥ `MUSIC.queue.deferMinMs` waits in a `deferCapacity` table and is built `deferLeadMs` before its start (exact on the audio clock; `_clearQueue` drops it like a queued cue). Round 5 (r4-audio needsOtherGroup 1, lead decisions): a cue with `prerender` (`hup`, `pass_up`, `roar` at pitch 1 and 0.6, `fanfare`) and the music pad chords (`MUSIC.instruments.pad.prerender`) are rendered once, as warm-up items after the dry builds, by `_renderJob`: the same `_startCue` / `_musicNote` code with `_ac` swapped to an `OfflineAudioContext` (rate 1, local randomness, fresh WaveShapers, the lead delay trimmed through `_layerShiftS`), one buffer per (pitch/stretch entry × chorus size × variant), one per distinct chord. `_startCue` then plays a matching instance (stretch equal, pitch within `MUSIC.prerender.pitchTol`, rate 1 or `noTimeScale`) as ONE AudioBufferSource into its cue gain (`_startPre`; playbackRate = the pitch ratio, time-scaled through the voice's source table), else builds the layer chains (`_startLayers`) as before; a downbeat plays a rendered chord through one envelope GainNode at the mode's pad level (`_padChord`; the GainNode is the voice's `env` for loop-restart fades). Without `OfflineAudioContext`, or until a render finishes, everything is live. The reward bus gain is `MUSIC.buses.reward · rewardFaderGain(sound, music)`: the sound fader, lifted toward the music fader while that is higher by at most `MUSIC.rewardLift.maxDb` (6) — sound 0 silent, sound ≥ music unchanged. The fallback music fader is 70 (save's round-5 default).

### 6.14 `js/ui.js` — class shape (full DOM contract in §10)

```js
export class UI {
  constructor(ctx); init():this; update(unscaledDt):void; dispose():void;
  setBootProgress(t01:number):void; hideBoot():void;   // hideBoot waits for (or starts) prewarm() before #scr-boot hides (round-2 UI-19); round 3: the boot fill is a full-width strip slid by transform translateX(f − 100 %) inside the bar's rounded clip (compositor-only, like the HUD progress fill — a width write re-laid out and re-rastered the bar every boot frame), and #boot-bar (role=progressbar) carries aria-valuenow = the integer percent
  setBootCaption(index:number):void;                    // round-2 UI-29: caption of the running boot step (STRINGS.boot.steps: page, textures, models, audio, systems, shaders, and round 3 the UI prewarm); main calls it before each step (§4.1)
  prewarm(onProgress?:(t:number)=>void):Promise<void>;  // round-2 UI-19: lays out and rasterises every first-use surface (results, game over, endless results, levels, shop, settings, pause, title, HUD, boss chrome, hint, shield, pill 0, icons, card icons, level grid, result bitmaps) behind #scr-boot via the .prewarm class; pill 0 also runs its real pop-in animation (KF_POP_IN: the animated, composited pill with the ring mask and icon drop-shadow cost a ≈ 48 ms first-pickup GPU flush on desktop and in the artifact shell when only the static pill was prewarmed — round-2 release verification) and (round 3) the earned results star its J17 slam (KF_STAR from 3×). Round-3 boot polish: a list of steps run one animation frame at a time (rAF, 100 ms fallback) — JS-only steps (icon / bitmap encodes, CPU-backed canvases) share a frame up to 12 ms, a step that shows a surface ends its frame, each surface stays displayed 3 frames then loses .prewarm (the HUD is two surfaces — boss chrome, then hint / shield / pill — sharing their #hud parent); onProgress(0..1) weighted by step cost (a surface = 8 encodes); all at once was one ≈ 0.6 s frozen frame at 4× CPU throttling plus ≈ 0.5 s of GPU raster; idempotent (a later call returns the same promise)
  showScreen(id:'boot'|'title'|'levels'|'shop'|'settings'|'pause'|'complete'|'gameover'|'endless'|'guide'|null, data?):void;  // null = HUD only, closes every overlay; called by ui's own state:change listener (§6.2 mapping) and for the settings / FIELD GUIDE overlays (round 7: 'guide' is an overlay like 'settings' — over the title, or in place of the pause panel; never a state); sets render.dirty
  refit():void;          // writes the unitless --ui custom property from #app.clientWidth/clientHeight (§10.2); called from init() and render.resize()
  showError(message:string, fatal:boolean):void;
  flash(colorHex, seconds, alpha):void;  vignette(colorHex, seconds, alpha?):void;   // round 7 (teaching:1, the C → D contract): alpha 0..1 multiplies the vignette's strength (absent = 1)
  showHint(id, text, opts?):void;  hideHint():void;   // round 7: opts = the hint:show payload's { hold, until, priority, event } (§5.1, §10.3 #hint)
  coinIconScreenPos(out:{ x, y }):void;   // target for coin flights
  powerupIconScreenPos(item, out:{ x, y }):void;   // target for icon flights
  debugPanel:HTMLElement|null;  setDebugText(text):void;
  confirmReset():void;   // "Tap again to confirm" flow
  showOverlay(text:string):void;  hideOverlay():void;   // context-loss / blocking messages (#overlay-msg)
}
```

The boss bar, letterbox and name card have no public methods: ui drives them internally from `boss:spawn/intro/fight/hp/phase/shield/dead` and hides them on `level:start` (restart during an intro leaves nothing behind). Round 7: the bars retract at `boss:fight` and return for a campaign boss's death cinematic (perf-render:7, lead decision); the name card carries the archetype's tagline and, on `BOSSES.milestones`, the label DISTRICT FINALE; the intro dolly docks the squad count in the bottom bar; `boss:phase` flashes "PHASE 2" / "ENRAGED" in place of the bar's name for `JUICE.ui.bossPhaseLabelSeconds` (r7 fix: at every size, §10.3). Round-5 integration (r5-perf-audio needsOtherGroup 2): the boss-chrome prewarm surface also runs their real entry animations once (the bar's `KF_SLIDE_DOWN`, both letterbox bars, the name card's `KF_POP_IN`), cancelled by `_prewarmRestore`, so the boss arrival frame is not their first animation / composite — as for pill 0 and the district banner. ui copies every multi-frame payload into its own fields on receipt (`_complete = { level, stars, coins, breakdown, … }`, `_failed`, `_endless`, `_bossName`), never holding the emitter's object. Listeners only store values and set dirty bits; all DOM writes happen in `update()`.

Round-3 polish (screens): `showScreen` sets `.under` on the screen the settings overlay stacks over (the title) — css hides that screen's content once the overlay has faded in, so nothing shows through the scrim — and `#hud.results` while a results screen (complete / gameover / endless) is up: the HUD keeps only its coin pill (whose count settles there) and its effects; the LEVEL label, progress bar, pills and boss chrome collided with the results titles in landscape. The game-over screen names the failed level (`#gameover-level`, `STRINGS.gameover.level` = the shared `LEVEL N` template of the HUD, title caption and results title). The armor shield pill also hides while the projected count label is outside the visible band (the app box minus the letterbox bars while they are up): the boss-intro dolly drops the squad below the screen, and the clamped pill sat alone in the bottom bar.

### 6.15 `js/save.js`

```js
export class SaveStore {
  constructor(ctx); load():void; flush():void /* debounced 250 ms, guarded */; get data():SaveData;
  profile():Profile;                                  // derived from UPGRADES and data.upgrades ranks
  addCoins(n:number):void; spend(n:number):boolean;   // emit save:change { reason:'coins' }
  setUpgrade(id:UpgradeId, rank:number):void;         // reassigns ctx.profile = this.profile(); emits save:change { reason:'upgrade' }
  recordLevel(level:number, r:{ stars, survivors, score, coins }):void;  // keeps bests; unlocks level+1 when stars ≥ 1
  recordEndless(r:{ distance, score }):void;          // bests + runs++
  addStats(partial:{ runs?, kills?, gatesPassed?, coinsEarned?, playSeconds? }):void;   // adds into data.stats
  markHint(id:string):void; hintDone(id:string):boolean;
  setSetting(key:string, value):void; get settings():Settings;   // the CALLER (ui) emits settings:change afterwards
  setLastIncome(n:number):void;
  reset():void;                                       // defaults + flush; reassigns ctx.profile
  get volatile():boolean;                             // storage unavailable or a write failed
}
```

`load()` and `reset()` reassign `ctx.profile`; the save store never mutates state from an event listener (its listeners only `flush()`, §5).

### 6.16 `js/level.js`

```js
export class LevelRunner {
  constructor(ctx); init():this; dispose():void;   // init(): subscribes (§5), phase = 'idle'
  get initSteps():number; initStep():number;       // round-3 verify (§4.1 step 9 slices): one slice — a throwaway levelgen attempt of the first generated level (L2, seed 1002), so
                                                   // the generator is JIT-warm before the first idle prefetch (a page's first attempt runs ≈ 75 ms at 4× CPU against 8–14 ms warm and
                                                   // used to land right after the L1 results appeared); never throws
  start(level:number, seed?:number):void;   // campaign level; script = LEVELS.authored[level] ?? generateLevel(level, seed ?? 1000 + level)
  startEndless(seed?:number):void;
  restart():void;                           // same level, same seed — except a campaign RETRY after a failure: with a fail streak the default seed is rerolled (round-3 QA LVL-21: seedBase + L + LEVELS.curve.retrySeedStep·consecutive failures; authored levels keep theirs), so the retry is a fresh layout
  quitToTitle():void;                       // the only entry into the title idle scene (also called by main at boot)
  get district():District;                  // round 5 (docs/DISTRICTS.md §8.5): the district shown. start / startEndless / restart: after the undo list (bloom
                                            // restored with setBloomStrength() — no argument = the district's bloomBase — and env.setFog(d.fog.near, fogFar × d.fog.farScale, 0)),
                                            // before env.setLevel: render.setDistrict(d, 0) with d = districtForLevel(L) (endless: districtForChunk(1)); level:start carries
                                            // district: d.id and is followed by district:change { reason 'start', seconds 0, banner: the previous run this session was not
                                            // the same level (endless: not endless) }. quitToTitle: districtForLevel(save.data.highestUnlocked), render.setDistrict(d, 0),
                                            // district:change { reason 'title', banner false }. Endless step: rotation to districtForChunk(D) (§3.2: gate-clear window,
                                            // next gate ≥ fadeNextGateFrac·fadeSeconds·v ahead, no boss alive, or fadeDeferMaxM past the boundary) → render.setDistrict(next,
                                            // DISTRICT_RULES.fadeSeconds) + district:change { reason 'rotate', banner true }. render.setDistrict is called only when present
  step(dt):void;  idleStep(dt):void;        // idleStep: title scene (phase 'idle'; ctx.world.roadSpeed = WORLD.idleRoadSpeed; every WORLD.idleGateEveryM metres gates.spawnEvent({ layout:'pair', lanes:[{ op:'+', value:17, cap:68 }, { op:'x', value:2, cap:5, step:9999 }], decorative:true }); squad count = profile.startSquad; no zombies, no bullets, no coins)
  get level():number; get endless():boolean; get script():LevelScript; get phase():'idle'|'run'|'runup'|'bossIntro'|'boss'|'won'|'lost';
  expectedSquad(z = 0):number;              // E at the squad front now: linear interpolation of script.expected [{at, e}] at distance − z
  waveHz():number;                          // hz of the most recently spawned wave = hpByType[t] / ZOMBIES[t].baseHp for any type t present (all types share one hz); 1.0 before the first wave; during the boss fight the run-up hz
  showHint(id:string, arg?, opts?):boolean; // if (!save.hintDone(id) && id ∉ run.hintsShown) { run.hintsShown.push(id); emit hint:show { id, text, hold, until, priority, event }; return true } — text = HINTS[id](arg) for a template hint, else HINTS[id]. level does NOT mark the hint: ui calls save.markHint(id) once it has been on screen JUICE.ui.hintMarkSeconds (round 7; round-2 QA LVL-24 / UI-25: at display). Round 7 (teaching:0 / :3 / :5): opts { hold, until, priority, event } (§5.1); the gate lessons ('red' / 'single' at their event's spawn, 'div' at a ÷ lane's, 'lesser' at the first event whose every lane is − or ÷ — r7 fix: and that no other lesson was bound to during its spawnEvent (_spawnTaught: L3's first [÷2 | −13] teaches 'div', so 'lesser' waits for the next lesser pair, L5's) — are bound to their gate event (until = its id, hold = its time to the squad; hint:release at its pass / miss; r7 fix round 2: 'lesser' also at gate:flip of its event's − gate and gate:step to ×1 of its ÷ gate — `_lesserEv`, `_lesserResolved` — since "BOTH LANES HURT" is then false), "GRAB THE CRATE" until a crate opens (≤ JUICE.ui.crateHintHoldSeconds; r7 fix round 2: its script row shows nothing when no closed crate is left ahead, `pickups.hasClosedCrateAhead()` — the squad can shoot its crate open before the row, 30 m after the spawn), each boss attack's lesson (LESSON_OF: dodge.slam / .charge / .car / .acid, boss.stomp — r7 fix round 2: a template given `weapons.storedCount`, naming the FIRE lever only with a weapon stored; also a spitter's glob, zombie:telegraph 'spit') is a priority hint held for its telegraph + JUICE.ui.hintHoldPadSeconds and slowed the first time ever (fx.slowmo(JUICE.slowmo.bossTutorial[0], delay / [0] + [1])); boss:phase → 'boss.<base archetype>.<n>'; boss:fight → 'boss' and, with a weapon stored, 'bossFire' (campaign); the first special on a generated campaign level → 'specials'; a weapon's name hint at weapon:start (not its pickup); an item hint is skipped (and marked) when its NEW card showed this run. gates.spawnEvent's own forward of ev.hint (no opts) is ignored while _fireGates spawns that event (_spawningEv); _fireGates shows it itself afterwards with the event id. _codexSeen(id, card) marks 'seen:<id>' once ever and, for a CODEX.cards entry, emits codex:new { id }
                                            // round-3 FTUE: level also listens to boss:attack (any kind but stomp / chew → showHint('dodge')) and item:pickup (not a duplicate, HINTS[item] defined → showHint(item): the first pickup of each special names it; hint id = item id)
```
