```js
                                            // round 4 (r3 FTUE §6.2): gate:spawn of a ÷ lane whose script lane can still flip to ×2 (lane.cap > 1, or no script lane) → showHint('div') — the ÷ ladder is taught once ever at the first such spawn (L3's ÷2 | −13 on the canonical seed); endless lanes the gate guard capped at ×1 never show it
  requestStart(level:number, seed?:number):boolean; // round-5 integration — the menus' start (ui: PLAY, NEXT, a level tile): start() at once when the script is authored or memoized (returns true);
                                            // else its generation moves to the front of the prefetch queue and runs in slices of 8 ms of attempts (one at least) — round 7 (flow-coins:3): 0 ms
                                            // setTimeout tasks between frames, not one per animation frame (0.3–2 s each on a no-GPU box); a slice in a hidden tab re-arms on rAF, so a run never starts unseen
                                            // while the menu stays up, and the run starts in the frame the script is ready (returns false). A state change, a newer request or a direct
                                            // start / startEndless / quitToTitle drops it (the generation stays queued for idle time). No level-start frame waits on a whole generateLevel
                                            // (L6 / L13 / L19 / L28: 24–32 attempts, 49–141 ms unthrottled, ≈ 0.2–0.6 s at 4×). start() keeps its synchronous contract (boot with ?level=N, tools)
  get starting():boolean;                   // round-5 integration: a requestStart is waiting for its script (ui shows .starting on the tapped button)
  requestRestart():boolean;                 // round-5 integration: restart() for the menus (RETRY / REPLAY / RESTART) through requestStart — same level and seed rule; endless: startEndless(seed)
  prefetch(level:number, seed?:number):void; // round-2 QA LVL-18: generates the campaign script in idle time (requestIdleCallback, 1 s timeout) into a memo keyed `${curveL}|${seed}`; level also prefetches on state:change (complete → L+1, title / levels → highest unlocked, gameover → the same level). start() reads the memo (deep-frozen, read-only scripts) and falls back to the synchronous generateLevel on a miss (ctx.debug.levelGenMs / levelGenHits / levelGenMisses). Round-2 cross-group (UI report item 3): the prefetch drives levelgen.levelGenerator one generation attempt at a time, as many as the idle deadline allows (≥ 6 ms left; one attempt minimum per slot; 8 ms slices without requestIdleCallback), so no idle task runs a whole generation; a start() that misses while that key is being prefetched finishes the partial generation instead of starting over
  finish(won:boolean):void;                 // COMMIT (stars/coins per GDD §9, save.*) then PRESENT (state complete|gameover, level:complete | level:failed | endless:end). round-2 QA LVL-19: the commit runs exactly once per run; quitToTitle / start / restart / startEndless during a 'won' / 'lost' theatre commit without presenting. The results deadline runs on ctx.time.theatre (CORE-7) or level's own step-driven real-time clock, so it never elapses while paused (round-2 QA LVL-29)
  get run():RunStats;
  // private: _addCoins(n, source) — the ONLY coin accounting, called solely from the coin:pickup listener
}
// RunStats (plain object, re-created per run):
{ level, seed, endless, phase, distance, length, roadSpeedBase, elapsed, coins, breakdown:{ kills, gates, piles, boss, bonus },
  kills, killsByType:{ walker, runner, crawler, brute, spitter }, gatesPassed, gatesMaxed, gatesFlipped, peakSquad, soldiersLost,
  lostToRed, bossStartTime, bossSeconds, survivors, stars, score, replay:boolean, waveHz, chunks,
  hitsPerGate:number[], finalVsBase:number[], lostPerWave:number[], hintsShown:string[],
  bossHpScale, assist, rubberEvents,        // round-2 QA LVL-14 (count × shop boss HP scale), LVL-21 (fail-streak assist soldiers), LVL-17 (endless rubber-band rewrites)
  redCapped, bossTtkBand }                  // round 7 (debug): campaign gate events _capRedLanes capped; 1 when _bossTtkBand raised the boss HP to the fight floor (r7 fix round 3: or the weak floor)
```

`start(level, seed)`: builds the script (`LEVELS.authored[level] ?? generateLevel(level, seed ?? LEVELS.seedBase + level)`; beyond `LEVELS.count` the curve uses L = 30 and seed `1000 + L`, so the campaign loops), then, BEFORE resetting systems, undoes every leftover engine/render/ui/audio state: `if (state.is('paused')) engine.resume()`; `engine.setTimeScale(1, 0)`; `engine.lockInput(false)`; `engine.resetLevelClock()`; `engine.resetSteering()`; `ctx.world` fields overwritten in place: `roadSpeedBase = script.roadSpeed, roadSpeed = roadSpeedBase, distance = 0, squadX = 0, squadHalfWidth = 0, squadDepth = 0, squadCount = 0, invulnerable = false, bossActive = false, bossZ = 0, bossX = 0`; `render.cancelDolly()`; `render.setBloomStrength(RENDER.bloom.strength)`; `render.setDesaturate(0)`; `render.gameOverCamera(false)`; `render.idleCamera(false)`; `render.env.setFog(WORLD.fog.near, params.fogFar, 0)` (round 5: the district's `fog.near` and `params.fogFar × fog.farScale`, and `setBloomStrength()` restores the district's bloom base); `audio.duck(0, 0)`; `weapons.pauseTimers(false)`. Round 5 (docs/DISTRICTS.md §3, §8.5): then `render.setDistrict(d, 0)` with d = `districtForLevel(level)` (endless: `districtForChunk(1)`), before `render.env.setLevel`; `level:start` carries `district: d.id` and is followed by `district:change { reason: 'start' }`; `quitToTitle()` applies `districtForLevel(save.data.highestUnlocked)` with `district:change { reason: 'title', banner: false }`; endless rotates every `DISTRICT_RULES.endlessChunksPerDistrict` chunks once no live gate event is at z < `DISTRICT_RULES.fadeGateClearZ`, the next `'gates'` event is ≥ `fadeNextGateFrac × fadeSeconds × roadSpeed` ahead and no boss is alive (`world.bossActive`), waiting at most `fadeDeferMaxM` past the boundary, with `render.setDistrict(next, DISTRICT_RULES.fadeSeconds)`; the run-up fog uses the district's `fog.runupNear`. Then `ctx.rng = new Rng(seed)`, `ctx.rngFx = ctx.rng.fork(7)`, `ctx.run = newRunStats()` (`replay = save.data.levels[level]?.clears > 0`), `render.env.setLevel(seed, params.propDensity)`, resets systems in the §6 order (squad last: its `squad:count` start event sees an empty world), `engine.freezeQuality(QUALITY_TIERS.scaler.freezeLevelStart)`, `state.set('playing')`, emits `level:start` (ui hides boss bar/letterbox/hint/overlays, audio switches to `'level'`, fx cancels the game-over grade). `restart()` = `start(level, seed)` with the same seed; `startEndless(seed)` = the same list with the endless script (`generateChunk(1, seed, 0)`), `endless = true`. `quitToTitle()`: the same undo list, then `zombies.killAll('cleanup')`, every system `reset()`, `state.set('title')`, `render.idleCamera(true)`, `audio.setMusic('menu')`, phase `'idle'`.

`step(dt)`: `roadSpeed = lerpExp(roadSpeed, target, LEVELS.curve.roadLerpRate, dt)` (target = `roadSpeedBase`, or `roadSpeedBase·WORLD.bossRoadFactor` from the boss event, campaign only); `distance += roadSpeed·dt`; `run.elapsed` (scaled) accumulates; fires every event with `at ≤ distance` through the cursor:

- `gates` → `gates.spawnEvent(ev)`; each `guards[i]` → `zombies.spawnWave({ pattern:'line', units, x: laneX, z: WORLD.spawnZ + WORLD.guardOffset }, hp)` with `hp = ev.guards[i].hp ?? solveWaveHp(expectedAt(distance + (70 − guardOffset)·v/(v + s̄)), v, units, bandMid)` and `opts.guardEventId/guardLane` on each spawn.
- `wave` → `s̄ = Σ units_t·ZOMBIES[t].speed / N`; `contactAt = distance + 70·v/(v + s̄)`; `E = expectedAt(contactAt)` (private helper: linear interpolation of `script.expected` by distance); `hpByType = ev.hp ?? scale(solveWaveHp(E, v, units, bandMid), ev.hpMult ?? 1)` with `bandMid` from `LEAK_BANDS` for the level; `zombies.spawnWave(ev, hpByType)`; `run.waveHz = hz`; a `hint` shows at spawn; `hintOnLoss` arms (below).
- `crate` → `pickups.spawnCrate(item, x)`; `coins` → `pickups.spawnCoinLine(x, count)`; `hint` → `showHint(id)`.
- `runup` → phase `runup`, `render.env.setFog(d.fog.runupNear, params.fogFar × d.fog.farScale, 1)` (round 5: the run's district d; District 1 = `WORLD.fog.runupNear`, ×1), emits `level:runup { secondsToBoss }`; then `bursts` (default 4) × `zombies.spawnWave({ pattern, units: units/bursts rounded, spread: 8 }, hp)` every `burstGap` (2) s with `hp = ev.hp ?? solveWaveHp(expectedAt(contactAt), v, units, bandMid)`; `run.waveHz` = this hz (used by troops during the boss fight).
- Round-5 integration: the levels screen ('levels' state) also prefetches every other unlocked level, nearest the frontier first, and the memo holds `LEVELS.count + 10` scripts (3–6 KB of JSON each); `BOSS_WARM_LEAD_M` (45 m) before a boss event (campaign: armed at the run start; endless: when a mini-boss chunk is appended) level dry-runs `_fireBoss`'s own helpers once — archetype resolution, the minion union, `solveWaveHp`, the live HP multiplier, the boss HP scale (endless: the pressure copy) — with every result discarded and nothing written, so the boss hand-off frame is not their first call (r5-perf-audio needsOtherGroup 1).
- `boss` → phase `bossIntro`, road target `roadSpeedBase·WORLD.bossRoadFactor`, `boss.spawn({ id, archetype, hp, phases, minionHp, mini: endless })` where `minionHp = ev.minionHp ?? solveWaveHp(script.eBoss, roadSpeedBase, unionUnits, bandMid)` and `unionUnits` = the per-type sum of every `spawn` action in the resolved archetype's phases plus `{ walker: BOSSES.minionBurst.count }` (level resolves `BOSSES.archetypes[archetype]` through `base`; the boss never computes HP). `gates`/`pickups` stop spawning from here.
- Round 5d, endless (GDD §10.1, docs/build/r5d-endless.md): (a) **HP cap** — every endless wave / guard table, after `_liveHpMult`, is clamped per type to `levelgen.endlessHpCap(E, p)` = `LEVELS.endless.hpCapSeconds[type]` × the on-curve design DPS at E (`_capEndlessHp`; p = the shop's fire rate × damage, `LEVELS.endless.profileExp`); (b) **mini-boss hold** — `_fireBoss` of a `mini` event sets `_holdOn`; while it is on the script cursor stops at the first `gates` / `wave` / `crate` / `coins` event (`HOLD_TYPES`; `hint` / `boss` / `runup` pass, chunk, district and score bookkeeping go on, the boss's minions are boss.js's); `boss:dead` → `_releaseHold()`: every script event from the cursor on (held and later) has `at += shift` with `shift = distance + LEVELS.endless.miniBossHold.releaseM − evs[cursor].at` (nothing when ≤ 0), `_holdShiftM` accumulates it, `_appendChunk` generates later chunks at `chunkEnd + _holdShiftM` (the shifted frame) and `_guardEndlessGate` reads an event's chunk as `floor((ev.at − _holdShiftM) / chunk) + 1`, as does `_expectedAt` (E(z) of the road at d is E of chunk `floor((d − _holdShiftM) / chunk) + 1`, so the waves' HP solve, their HP cap and `expectedSquad()` stay on the chunk the road was generated for; docs/build/r5d-audit.md); `ctx.debug.holdShiftM` mirrors it; both reset per run; (c) **pool clamp** — `_clampEndlessUnits(units)` runs on every endless wave (after `_crowdEndlessWave`) and on every endless gate guard squad before the spawn: `units[t] = min(units[t], ZOMBIES[t].pool − zombies.aliveOfType(t) − poolReserve[t])` with `poolReserve = levelgen.endlessPoolReserve()` (`LEVELS.endless.poolReserve`, the mini-boss's minion union), trimmed zombies counted in `ctx.debug.dropped['level.waveClamp']` — the live backstop of the generator's static pool fit (§6.17); in-place on the run's own (never frozen) events, allocation-free.
- Round 7, campaign balance (GDD §4.4 / §6.3 / §7; all runtime, the generator, the scripts and the validator are unchanged): (a) **red-lane cap** — `_fireGates` passes a campaign event through `_capRedLanes(ev)` before `showHint` / `_pendingEvent` / `gates.spawnEvent` / `_bindEvent`, so the bound event is the capped one: on a generated level every `'-'` lane with `value > lim`, `lim = max(GATE_RULES.generation.minValue, util.roundDesign(frac · squad.count))`, `frac = LEVELS.curve.redCap.single` for a single layout else `.multi`, gets `value = lim` and `flipCap` = its authored flipCap (`redCap.keepFlipCap`; else `min(flipCap, lim)`) in a shallow copy (`Object.assign({}, ev, { lanes })`, the frozen script untouched, one allocation per capped event), `run.redCapped++`; authored levels and endless (`_guardEndlessGate`) are not capped. A capped lane may therefore spawn with `flipCap > value` (gates.js already reads both). (b) **low side** — `_lowSideMult(E)` = `max(LEAK_MODEL.lowSide.floor, C / E)^exp` when C < E, else 1 (1 in endless, on authored levels, without the key) multiplies the live HP of every wave (`_fireWave`), gate guard (`_fireGates`, once per event) and run-up (`_fireRunup`) with E = `_expectedAt(distance)` at the spawn (never the contact point), and the campaign boss minions (`_fireBoss`, E = E_boss). (c) **boss HP** — `_bossHpScale` clamps the count scale to `[BOSSES.squadScale.minMult, maxMult]` in the campaign (`[1, maxMult]` in endless) and `_fireBoss` drops the `max(table, …)` floor when `minMult < 1` (endless keeps it); then `_bossTtkBand(hp, mini, eBoss)` (pure) returns `max(hp, round50(C·fireRate·damage·bossDamage·ttkBand.acc · tMin))` for a campaign boss met by C ≥ `ttkBand.fromRatio`·E_boss, `tMin = (milestone ? milestoneMin : min) · m^−shopExp` (milestone = `min(L, loopAfter)` ∈ `BOSSES.milestones`) — r7 fix: weighted in over `ttkBand.fadeWidth`·E_boss from `fromRatio`, HP = hp + w·(floor − hp), w = (C/E_boss − fromRatio) / fadeWidth clamped to 1 (no HP step where the band switches on); r7 fix round 3: first, at any C, the weak floor hp = max(hp, round50(C·fireRate·damage·bossDamage·acc · `ttkBand.weakMin` · m^−shopExp)) — returned as is below `fromRatio`·E_boss, and the base the band fades in from — `run.bossTtkBand` = 1 when either raised the HP; `_warmBossPath` dry-runs `_lowSideMult` and `_bossTtkBand` too.

`level:progress` 10 Hz; stats from subscribed events (`zombie:die` → kills/killsByType; `gate:hit` → `hitsPerGate`; `gate:max` → `gatesMaxed`; `gate:flip` → `gatesFlipped`; `gate:pass` → `gatesPassed`, `finalVsBase`, `lostToRed` (op `'-'`/`'/'` with `after < before`); `squad:loss` → `soldiersLost`, `lostPerWave`; `squad:count` → `peakSquad`; `boss:fight` → `bossStartTime`; `boss:dead` → `bossSeconds`). Hints: `level:start` shows nothing by itself (the L1 script carries `{ at: 0, type:'hint', id:'steer' }`); `gate:hit` → the first hit on a gate whose lane has `hintOnHit` → `showHint`; `gate:pass` → `lane.hintOnPass.id` when `after ≥ ifCountAtLeast`, `lane.hintIfOtherBetter` when `other && other.wouldBe > after`; `squad:loss` with cause `'bite'` → the armed `hintOnLoss` of the most recently spawned wave (then disarmed); `hintsShown` records them. On `boss:dead` → phase `won`: `run.survivors = squad.count` sampled immediately, `squad.setInvulnerable(Infinity)`, `zombies.killAll('cleanup')` (no coins), `_finishAt = theatre + BOSSES.death.resultsAfterMs (3000)` → `finish(true)` when the theatre clock reaches `_finishAt` (`ctx.time.theatre`, else level's own step-driven real-time clock: unaffected by the 0.25 slow-mo, frozen while paused; round-2 LVL-29). On `squad:dead` → phase `lost` → `fx.slowmo(JUICE.slowmo.squadDead[0], JUICE.slowmo.squadDead[1])`, `_finishAt = theatre + 1200` → `finish(false)`. Endless: when `distance ≥ chunkEnd − 70` append `generateChunk(D + 1, seed, chunkEnd)`, `run.chunks++`, emit `endless:chunk`.

Coins: `_addCoins(n, source)` is private and called solely from the `coin:pickup` listener: `delta = round(n · (source ∈ {'kill','boss'} ? profile.coinMult : 1) · (run.replay || endless ? ECONOMY.replayPayout : 1))`; `run.coins += delta`; breakdown mapping (`ECONOMY.breakdownOf`) `kill → kills`, `gate`/`max` → `gates`, `pile`/`crate`/`duplicate`/`refund` → `piles` (round 7: duplicates and refunds were `bonus`), `boss → boss`, `bonus → bonus`; emits `coins:change { total: save.data.coins + run.coins, runTotal: run.coins, delta, source }`. Nothing else credits coins.

`finish(won)`: campaign won → `stars` per GDD §9.3 from `run.survivors` (round 7: `survivors ≥ starNeed(E_boss, 2 | 3)`, the data.js helper the results / title captions print), `levelBonus = ECONOMY.levelBonus(L)·(1 + ECONOMY.starBonus[stars]) + ECONOMY.survivorCoins·max(0, survivors − ECONOMY.survivorFrom·E_boss)` (round-3 QA LVL-23) credited as `coin:pickup { amount: levelBonus, source:'bonus' }` (no coinMult, replay ×0.6 applies; kill coins earned during a campaign boss fight are × ECONOMY.bossFightKillMult in `_addCoins`), `score` per `SCORE`, `payout = run.coins`; `save.addCoins(payout)`, `save.recordLevel(level, { stars, survivors, score, coins: payout })`, `save.setLastIncome(payout)`, `save.addStats({ runs: 1, kills: run.kills, gatesPassed: run.gatesPassed, coinsEarned: payout, playSeconds: run.elapsed })`, `state.set('complete', { level, stars, coins: payout, breakdown })` (fresh object; round 7: `breakdown.bonusStars` = round(star part × replay factor) clamped to `bonus`, `bonusSurvivors` = the rest), emits `level:complete`. Campaign lost → `payout = round(run.coins·ECONOMY.gameOverKeep)` + the consolation `levelBonus(L)·ECONOMY.failBonusFrac·fraction` (paid as a 'bonus' coin:pickup; round-2 QA LVL-21), and the level's fail streak grows (round-3 QA LVL-21: after LEVELS.curve.assistAfterFails consecutive failures start() adds ceil(assistStartFrac·E_boss·k) soldiers, k ≤ assistMaxSteps, with `addSoldiers(n, 'assist')` and emits `hint:show { id:'assist', text: LEVELS.curve.assistHint(n) }` on every assisted attempt — not a once-ever hint); `save.addCoins(payout)`, `save.addStats(...)`, `state.set('gameover', { level, fraction, coinsKept: payout })`, emits `level:failed { level, fraction, coinsKept }`. Endless (always `finish(false)` at C = 0) → `score` per `SCORE.endless`, `payout = run.coins` (already ×0.6 per coin), `save.addCoins(payout)`, `save.recordEndless({ distance, score })`, `save.addStats(...)`, `state.set('complete', { endless: true, distance, score, best, coins: payout, chunks })`, emits `endless:end` (NOT `level:failed`).

### 6.17 `js/levelgen.js` (pure; no THREE, no DOM; node-importable)

```js
export function curve(L:number):{ len, v, gates, waves, pop, eStart, s0Assumed, eBoss, bossHp, bossId, milestone, band:[lo, hi] };
  // GDD §10 formulas; bossId = BOSSES.rotation[min(L, 30) − 1]; bossHp = round50(BOSSES.hpPerE·eBoss·max(milestone ? BOSSES.milestoneMult : 1, archetype.hpMult ?? 1)) — the two multipliers never stack (GDD §10 table)
export function generateLevel(L:number, seed:number):LevelScript;      // L ≥ 2, deterministic for (L, seed); re-rolls until validateLevel().ok
export function levelGenerator(L:number, seed:number):{ step():LevelScript|null, readonly done:boolean };   // round-2 cross-group: generateLevel as resumable steps (one attempt = buildAttempt + validateLevel per step(); null while more are due, then the chosen script); generateLevel loops it, so results are identical; used by level.prefetch
export function leakCalib(leak:number):number;                         // round-2 QA LVL-15: LEAK_MODEL.calib × piecewise calibByLeak multiplier of the wave-DPS model
export function eventWorstCase(...):number;                            // round-2 QA LVL-17: C × best lane of every unpassed endless gate event in the window (rubber band)
export function endlessGateGuard(ev, count, worst, E, v, fire, before, gainCap):'cap'|'keep'|null;   // round-2 QA LVL-17, round 3: the endless gate rewrite rule shared by level (_guardEndlessGate) and the validator — every lane scaled so the best adds ≤ gainCap (endlessParams(D).gainCap), × lanes / capped + lanes while the worst case ahead exceeds keepMult·E, no ÷ → x2 flip; the ÷2 | ÷2 high band is gone
export function endlessPressure(D:number):number;                      // round-3 QA LVL-17: (1 + pressure.perD)^max(0, D − pressure.fromD) — endless wave HP / population, mini-boss HP / attacks, gate gain cap, score
export function endlessHpCap(E:number, p:number, out?):Record<type, number>;   // round 5d: the per-zombie HP ceiling of an endless wave / guard at squad E — LEVELS.endless.hpCapSeconds[type] × E·FIRE.rateBase·FIRE.dmgBase·FIRE.accuracy·p^profileExp (Infinity without a cap); level._capEndlessHp, the validator's hp cap column
export function endlessPoolReserve(out?):Record<type, number>;         // round 5d: pool slots every endless wave / guard leaves free for the mini-boss's minions — LEVELS.endless.poolReserve, else the rotation's minion union; generateChunk's pool fit and level._clampEndlessUnits budget pool − reserve
export function endlessPoolPeaks(events, v, out:{ per:number[], total:number }):object;   // round 5d (tools): the validator's pool model over a chunk — peak live count per type (spawn → contact, waves + guards, peaks at spawn moments) and the peak total
export function endlessDesignMult(D:number):number;                    // round-3 QA LVL-17: endless waves are designed for designMult·E(D) (ramped from ×1 at D1 to ×designMult at designRampD)
export function levelCalib(L:number):number;                           // round-3 QA LVL-15: LEAK_MODEL.calibByLevel (piecewise-linear) — the campaign level's capacity factor in the wave / guard solve (generator and validator)
export function simulateEndless(...):object;                           // round-2 QA LVL-17: the validator's endless C/E line (rank 3 / rank 0)
export function generateChunk(D:number, seed:number, startAt:number):Event[];   // endless chunk (GDD §10.1); mini-boss events carry mini: true; gates sized from E(min(D, gateEMaxD)) (round-3 QA LVL-17); rubberBandAdjust (round 1) is removed — level applies the low-side band itself
export function endlessParams(D:number):{ D, v, e, eGate, gates, waves, wavesBase, need, pop, popBase, popWave, miniBoss, afterMiniBoss, Leq, band, bandMid, bandIdx, chunk, gainCap, hpCap, miniBossArchetype, miniBossHp, newest };   // per-chunk parameters (round 3: eGate, gainCap; round 5d: pop = population per wave after the pressure (popBase before it), hpCap = endlessHpCap(E, 1), and the pool-safe ramp — wavesBase = the wave count of GDD §10.1's formula, need = pop × wavesBase (the population the chunk owes), waves = that count or, once pop exceeds a wave's pool shares (Σ floor(pool / wavePoolShare) = 65), ceil(need / 65) up to LEVELS.endless.waves.maxPerChunk and the chunk's wave window / minSpacingM, popWave = ceil(need / waves))
export function validateLevel(script:LevelScript):{ ok:boolean, errors:string[], warnings:string[], trace:{ at, e, note }[] };  // E-trace ±15 %, leak bands, Σ EV ≥ 0.9·E_boss, spacing rules, pool capacities, mandatory lane caps
export function solveWaveHp(E:number, v:number, units:Record<type, number>, bandMid:number):Record<type, number>;   // GDD §6.3 with LEAK_MODEL constants; one hz for every type in units
export function gateDefaults(op, value, E, v):{ cap, flipCap, step };     // GDD §4.2 caps and step costs; ALWAYS applied by the generator so every Lane leaves levelgen with its caps filled
export function expectedHits(E:number, v:number):number;                  // H(z)
```

`LevelScript` shape is in §8.7. The generator places gate events every `len / gates` metres starting at `LEVELS.curve.gateStartM` (60), waves `waveAfterGateM` (20) past each gate's arrival, pickups at `pickupRange` (35–85 %) of the length with `PICKUPS.gateShare` (50 %) delivered by item lanes and the rest by crates, a `runup` at `len − LEVELS.curve.runup.metres` and the `boss` at `len`; pattern values, single/triple rules and sanity rules come from `GATE_RULES.generation` (§8.2), wave composition from `WAVE_WEIGHTS`/`WAVE_RULES`; it writes `expected: [{ at, e }]` from its own E-trace (cautious line) so `level.expectedSquad()` and the HUD agree with the design.

Endless chunks, round 5d (GDD §10.1, docs/build/r5d-endless.md) — `generateChunk(D, seed, startAt)` is pool-safe by construction: each wave is composed at `endlessParams(D).popWave`, clamped per type to its pool share (`floor(ZOMBIES[t].pool / LEVELS.endless.wavePoolShare)`) with a type's excess moved into the other types' headroom (`capWaveToPoolShare`), placed (QA LVL-4b stagger, the one-wave-per-gate nudge), sized on the chunk's line, and then the whole chunk is fitted to the pools (`fitEndlessPools`): in the validator's model (a zombie lives from its spawn distance to its contact; the live count peaks at spawn moments; the chunk's waves and gate guards) every type stays within `ZOMBIES[t].pool − endlessPoolReserve()[t]` — the pile at a type's peak moment is thinned evenly (every wave alive there scaled by one factor, floored, repeated while a peak remains), then an exact pass in spawn order clamps what rounding left; a trimmed wave's HP is re-solved on the same line; guards are never trimmed and a wave is never emptied. Chunks never overlap in that model (a wave's contact lies ≥ SPAWN_DIST + waveAfterGateM into its chunk, every window is shorter than SPAWN_DIST, the previous chunk's spans end by its end), which `validateLevel`'s pool check over the concatenated run proves per seed (`tools/validate-levels.js`, D1–30). The mini-boss hold of §6.16 shifts every event uniformly, so the model survives it.

---

## 7. Collision and hit-test ownership

All tests are in XZ with a per-target radius (capsules are vertical; bullets fly at y = `FIRE.bullet.y` 0.95 and zombies/boss are compared only where their `hitY` range covers 0.95 — crawlers are hittable only when `z > ZOMBIES.crawler.hittableZ` (−8) or lunging). Gates and crates are tested in XZ only and never filtered by y (`GATE_RULES.panel.hitIgnoresY`). Every tester calls the target system's query; targets never query testers. All four `out` objects carry `t ∈ [0,1]` along the swept segment; weapons keeps module-level scratch `out` objects per target type.

| Tester (module) | Target | Query | Resolution |
|---|---|---|---|
| Bullet (weapons) | zombie | `zombies.hitTestSeg(SEG, FIRE.bullet.radius, out)` with `SEG = [px, pz, x, z]` (fallback `hitTestSegment(px, pz, x, z, r, out)`) → nearest `t` along the swept segment | candidate |
| Bullet (weapons) | boss | `boss.hitTestSeg(SEG, FIRE.bullet.radius, out)` (nearest living twin; fallback `hitTestSegment`) | candidate |
| Bullet (weapons) | gate panel | `gates.hitTestSeg(SEG, out)` (armed or locked, z < `gateStopZ`, XZ only; fallback `hitTestSegment`) | candidate (skipped for kind `drone`) |
| Bullet (weapons) | crate | `pickups.hitTestSeg(SEG, out)` (closed crates, XZ only; fallback `hitTestSegment`) | candidate (skipped for `drone`) |
| | | **the candidate with the smallest `t` wins; ties → zombie**; the bullet is consumed; zombie: `zombies.damage(id, w·d·damageMult·(burning ? 1 + burnBonus : 1), x, y, z, 'bullet')`; boss: `boss.damage(w·d·damageMult, w, x, y, z)`; gate: `gates.applyHit(gate.id, w, x, y, z)` (no damageMult); crate: `pickups.hit(id)` | |
| Rocket (weapons) | all | on first candidate hit or at `WEAPONS.rocket.maxRange` (30 m): `zombies.damageInCircle(x, z, 2.5, squadDps()·1.0, 'explosion')`, `boss.damage(squadDps()·1.0, 8·w, x, 4.5, z)` if within 2.5 + 1.6 m, `gates.applyHit(gate, 8·w, x, 0.95, z)` for an armed/locked panel within 1.8 m (x) and 1.0 m (z) (weighted hits, w = max(1, C/40), round-1 QA WPN-4) | AOE, no piercing |
| Rail bolt (weapons) | all in lane | `zombies.damageInLine(x, 0, 0.4, squadDps()·3.0, 'bullet')` (whole road, z ∈ [spawnZ, 0]), `boss.damage(squadDps()·3.0, 10·w, bx, 4.5, bz)` if \|bossX − x\| ≤ 2.0, `gates.applyHit(g, 10·w, …)` for every armed/locked gate whose lane contains x (WPN-4) | pierces everything |
| Laser beam (weapons) | all in line | per step `zombies.damageInLine(bx, bz, 0.25, dps_beam·dt, 'bullet')`, `boss.damage(dps_beam·dt, 10·w·dt, bx, 4.5, bz)` when the beam crosses the capsule, `gates.applyHit(g, 10·w·dt, …)` for the panel the beam crosses (WPN-4) | pierces |
| Sniper (troops) | highest-HP zombie / boss | `zombies.highestHp(out)` then `zombies.damage(out.id, dmg, out.x, 1.0, out.z, 'bullet')`; `boss.damage(min(dmg·bossMult, bossCapFrac·boss.maxHp), 1, bx, 4.5, bz)` when no zombie and `bossActive` | instant, 1 target, no bullet |
| Flamethrower (troops) | zombies in cone | shared zombie scan over the `TROOPS.flamer.coneDeg`/2 cone, `range` 13 m: `dps·dt` per zombie, never below `TROOPS.anim.flamerHpFloor` (25 %) of `baseHp·hz`, + `setBurning(id, 2)`; every `flamerVolleyEvery` s a jet damages one target (lethal only vs spitters / brutes holding at range) (round-2 QA CHR-12) | continuous, non-lethal cone |
| Drone (troops) | nearest zombie | `weapons.spawnBullet(..., 'drone')` aimed at `zombies.nearest` / `nearestOfType('runner')`; drone bullets hit zombies and the boss only (`boss.damage(…, 0, …)`) | bullet rules, no gates/crates |
| APC (troops) | crawlers | `zombies.damageInCircle(apcX, apcZ − 1.7, 1.4, 9999, 'crush')` each step for crawler ids only (filter by `typeOf`) | kill |
| APC explosion (troops) | zombies | `zombies.damageInCircle(x, z, 3, squadDps()·2, 'explosion')` once | AOE |
| Zombie (zombies) | squad | `troops.apcIntersects(x, z, r)` → `apcAbsorb`; else `squad.intersectsCircle(x, z, r)` and `!squad.invulnerable` → bite | per GDD §6.1 |
| Spit glob (zombies/boss) | squad | impact at target (x, z): `\|squad.x − x\| < 1.2 && \|squad.centerZ − z\| < 1.2` (formation centre) → `squad.removeSoldiers(k, 'spit')` | — |
| Boss attack (boss) | squad | slam: `\|squad.x − ax\| ≤ r`, x only (formation centre x; ax = the squad x at the telegraph start, r = 1.8 m since round 7 — escapable from anywhere within ±`steerRange`); charge/lane: the 2.5 m stripe contains the formation centre x; car: 3 m x-band contains the formation centre x; chew: `boss.z ≥ BOSSES.valve.z` | per GDD §7 |
| Pickup (pickups) | squad | `squad.intersectsCircle(x, z, magnetRadius)` for coins (pull inside radius, collect ≤ 0.8 m), `PICKUPS.collectRadius` (1.5) for crates | collect |
| Gate pass (gates) | squad | at gate z ≥ −4 the lane with the centre nearest the steering target `input.targetX` commits (round 7, intent; the squad slides into it); at gate z ≥ 0 that committed lane passes (never geometry) | apply |
| Explosion (`explosion` event) | zombies/boss | emitter calls `damageInCircle` itself; the event is for fx/audio only | — |

Typed-segment queries (round-2 QA WPN-8 / ENE-16 / GAT-9): `zombies.hitTestSeg(seg, radius, out)`, `boss.hitTestSeg(seg, radius, out)`, `gates.hitTestSeg(seg, out)` and `pickups.hitTestSeg(seg, out)` read `seg`, the caller's module-level `Float64Array(4)` `[x0, z0, x1, z1]`, with the same semantics and `out` shape as `hitTestSegment` (which stays for other callers). Doubles passed as arguments to a non-inlined call are boxed into fresh HeapNumbers per call; the typed array is not, so weapons' per-bullet sweeps, rail / laser / rocket line tests and the gate walk allocate nothing. weapons checks `typeof target.hitTestSeg === 'function'` once per step and falls back to `hitTestSegment` (boxing the four values once per sweep) for a target without it. Query radii are module constants, passed as the same tagged value every call.

Swept test implementation (`hitTestSegment` / `hitTestSeg`): closest point on segment (p0 → p1) to the target centre in XZ; hit if distance ≤ radius + bulletRadius; `t` = parameter along the segment; bins: z-bins of 2 m from −80 to +20 (`Int16Array` head per bin, `next` per zombie), candidates from the bins covering [min(z0,z1) − 1, max(z0,z1) + 1].

---

## 8. Data tables (`js/data.js`)

`data.js` exports frozen plain objects (`Object.freeze` deep at module load). Keys below are final; numbers are the GDD's and are repeated here only where a shape needs illustration. Every id string used in events, saves and scripts comes from these tables. The listings below show the original shapes; `js/data.js` is authoritative for current values, and each subsection notes the keys QA rounds added or moved.

Round-2 key changes (cross-group summary; details in each fixer report under `docs/build/qa2/fix-*.md`):
- §8.1: `FIRE.bossAimYieldM` 6 (WPN-7 boss re-aim yield window), `WORLD.camera.titleLandscape { pos, look }` (VIS-20), `WORLD.camera.countDolly` (VIS-19), `SQUAD.steer.landscapeWidthFactor` 0.7 (CORE-8).
- §8.3: `ZOMBIES.brute.maxContactZ` −0.3, `contactHomingZ` 4.0, `maxSwings` 6, `backoffM` 2.0 (ENE-12; `backoffM` added by the cross-group pass); `ZOMBIES.common.introAheadM` deleted (levelgen reads `LEVELS.curve.introAheadM`); `BOSSES.hitYRange`, `retreatSpeed`, `gaitRefMs`, `phaseEntryCool`, `targetZ`, `introSparkEvery`, `squadScale`, `shopScale`, `anim.sacGain`, `anim.flashTint`, `anim.lightIntensity` 20, `anim.lightOffsetZ` −1.0 (ENE-13/14/18/19, LVL-14); `LEAK_MODEL` calibration keys (LVL-15).
- §8.4: `TROOPS.anim` is now the ONE home of troops.js's feel / animation numbers (troops.js reads it as ANIM; the cross-group pass moved the remaining local literals in: `droneAhead`, `droneMaxX`, the flamer aim / volley keys, `flamerHpFloor`, `flamePilotReach`, `sniperSideMargin`, `sniperMinX`, `droneDiscFlicker`; `sniperRearMargin` removed); `TROOPS.sniper.scale` 1.05 / `crateH` 0.25, `TROOPS.drone.hover` 5.2, `TROOPS.anim.droneSideX` 1.2, `TROOPS.apc.ahead` 3.5, `TROOPS.flamer.dps` 6 (CHR-9 / CHR-12); `WEAPONS.rocket.smoke.colorIdx` (WPN-10); `PICKUPS.crate.hitFlashGain` / `hitFlashRetrigger` (GAT-10).
- §8.8: `MUSIC.buses / compressor.knee / limiter / duck.bossDead / duck.gunOnReward / queue / modeSwitch / instruments.kick.click`, `AUDIO_CUES.*.noTimeScale`, `voices.gainExp`, the roar boss keys (AUD-4…13); `JUICE.ui.hintBottomPx / hintMaxWidthPct / hintAlpha / hintQueueMax / confirmMinS / resizeDebounceMs` (UI-18/20/25, CORE-10; `hintY` retired); `JUICE.damageNumber.bossScale / bossSide / bossRise / bossSlots`, `JUICE.caps.bossNumbers`, `JUICE.label.plateAlpha / platePad` (FX-10/12); `QUALITY_TIERS.med.bloomMips` 3 (VIS-13), `QUALITY_TIERS.scaler.targetMinMs / raiseBackoff` (CORE-6); `PALETTE.glyph.emissive` 0.82 (GAT-7); `STRINGS.boot.steps`, `STRINGS.hud.level` (UI-29/33).
- §8.9: `RENDER.title { hemi, sunAzimuthDeg }` (VIS-15), `RENDER.env.manhole` (VIS-23), `RENDER.atlas.strokePx` 18 (VIS-12).
- Round-3 FTUE review (`docs/build/r3-ftue.md`): `JUICE.ui.hintMinSeconds` 1.5 (a hint yields this early when another waits); `HINTS.dodge` and one `HINTS['<item id>']` per `PICKUP_SCHEDULE` id (first-pickup names); a no-break space before every em dash in `HINTS`; `STRINGS.shop.available` (aria suffix of a badged SHOP button) and `STRINGS.complete.newBest`; `LEVELS.authored[1]` crate hint row `at` 120 → 133 (inside the ×2 pair's commit window — a hint row, no balance number); `JUICE.damageNumber.bossSide` 0.12 → 0.3 (a row's two boss numbers no longer read as one).
- Round 4, visual / fx (`docs/build/r4-visual-fx.md`): `JUICE.damageNumber.bossRows` 2 (rows per side of the boss's own damage-number grid; brutes keep `bossSlots` 3).
- Round 4, level (`docs/build/r4-level.md`): `HINTS.div` (the ÷ ladder, shown by level at the first ÷ lane that can flip to ×2). `LEVELS.endless.profileExp` stays 0 (re-measured: the shop already moves the strong player's endless death by ≈ 4 chunks from rank 0 to rank 10). `GATE_RULES.glyph.labelYieldS` / `labelYieldMargin` were already moved to data.js by the round-3 verifier.
- Round 5, integration (`docs/build/r5-integrate.md`): `WORLD.idleGateSink { z0Pad: 0.5, rate: 2 }` (decorative title gates sink into the road behind the title squad, §6.9) and `DISTRICT_RULES.puddleReflect` 0.5 (the wet-road puddles' sky reflection gain, environment.js `uGrWet`; was a `DATA-MISSING` module literal; 0.3 since the integration's readability pass). Both are read guarded, with these values as fallbacks.
- Round 5, art review (`docs/build/r5-art.md`): `DISTRICT_RULES.puddleReflectFloor` 0.3, `puddleFloorFadeM` [18, 35] and `poolPuddleGlint` 0.12 (environment.js `gr:road`, §6.3), read guarded with these values as fallbacks; district data: Night City `road.pools.intensity` 1.2 → 2.4, `road.puddleRoughness` 0.18 → 0.32 in Overcast, Night, Toxic and Frozen.
- Round 4, release (`docs/build/r4-release.md`; r4-level needsOtherGroup 1): audio.js reads the round-4 keys `MUSIC.queue.deferMinMs` / `deferLeadMs` / `deferCapacity` / `shaperPool` and `MUSIC.warm.*` guarded, with their shipped values as fallbacks, and plays no reward-stack trim without `MUSIC.duck.rewardStack`, so the module still loads against a data.js that lacks them (a mid-edit tree, or a network-first launch answered from an older cached data.js) instead of failing the boot.
- The cross-group pass also wired ≈ 60 literals marked `DATA-MISSING` whose data key already existed with the same value (zombies, weapons, squad, render, materials, fx) to read data.js.

### 8.2 `GATE_RULES`

```js
export const GATE_RULES = { panel: { w: 3.6, wTriple: 2.4, h: 2.4, bottom: 1.0, depth: 0.4, opacity: 0.38, opacityHC: 0.6, hitIgnoresY: true, hitFlash: 0.12 }, glyph: { h3: 1.7, h4: 1.35, icon: 0.7, pop: 0.28, popTime: 0.12, labelYieldS: 0.1, labelYieldMargin: 1.1 /* round-3 FTUE label yield */ },
  intakePerSec: 40, plus: { capMult: 4, capMin: 12, maxCoins: 5, coinPerHit: 1 }, minus: { flipFlash: 0.25, recolor: 0.4 },
  mult: { base: 2, base3FromLevel: 6, stepMin: 12, stepFraction: { intake: 0.9, capReach: 1.02, capBlock: 1.1 }, cap: 5, punch: 1.35, openReach: 0.9 },   // the step cost K_M from the intake model (QA LVL-27, data.js)
  div: { stepMin: 8, stepFraction: { perD: { 2: 0.45, 3: 0.3 }, flipBlock: 1.1 }, ladder: [3, 2, 1], flipTo: 2, flipStepMult: 2 },   // K_÷ = max(stepMin, perD[D]·I, flipBlock·Icap / (rungs to ×1 + flipStepMult))
  hits: { fraction: 0.5, shootersCap: 40, windowM: 34.5 }, coins: { pass: 3, max: 5 },
  fx: { /* …, */ attnGain: 0.8, attnRadPerS: 12 /* round 7: pulseEvent */ }, reach: { rateTauS: 0.4, minRate: 0.5 } /* round 7 (r7 teaching:4): the ring's "reaches it in time" projection */,
  colors: { plus: { tint: 0x2E9BFF, frame: 0x1A6FD6 }, mult: { tint: 0x3BE57A, frame: 0x1FA85A }, minus: { tint: 0xFF3B4E, frame: 0xB81E2E }, div: { tint: 0xE0346E, frame: 0xA1204B },
            zero: { tint: 0xB8B8B8, frame: 0x8A8A8A }, item: { tint: 0xC77DFF, frame: 0x8E3FD6 }, max: 0xFFD166 },
  generation: {
    layoutWeights: { early: { pair: 75, single: 25, triple: 0 }, mid: { pair: 70, single: 15, triple: 15 }, tripleFromLevel: 7 /* L4–6: the triple weight is folded into pair */ },
    patterns: [ { id: 'plusPlus',  w: [30, 20, 15], a: [0.25, 0.6], bOfA: [0.5, 0.8] },           // +A | +B: A = round(E·U(a)), B = round(A·U(bOfA)); smaller value on the lane with fewer zombies ahead
                { id: 'plusMult',  w: [30, 25, 25], aOfE: [0.7, 1.3] },                           // +A | xM: A = round(E·(M−1)·U(aOfE))
                { id: 'minusDiv',  w: [15, 20, 20], aOfE: [0.7, 1.3], aMaxH: 1.3, divThreeFrom: 8 }, // −A | ÷D: A = round(E·(1 − 1/D)·U(aOfE)), A ≤ aMaxH·H; D = 3 allowed from L8
                { id: 'multMinus', w: [15, 15, 15], a: [0.3, 0.5], flipMaxH: 0.8 },               // xM | −A: A = round(E·U(a)); flippable iff A ≤ flipMaxH·H
                { id: 'plusMinus', w: [10, 10, 10], bMaxH: 0.8, bOfE: 0.3, aPlus: 4 },            // +A | −B: B = round(min(bMaxH·H, E·bOfE)), A = floor(B/2) + aPlus
                { id: 'itemPlus',  w: [0, 10, 15], aOfE: 0.35 } ],                                 // item | +A: A = round(E·aOfE)
    single: { plusOnlyThrough: 2, aOfE: 0.2, w: { plus: 50, mult: 30, minus: 20 }, minusMaxH: 0.9 },
    triple: { bOfE: 0.4 },                                                                        // [+A, xM, −B], exactly one red lane
    mult3: { fromLevel: 6, chance: 0.25 },
    rules: { noDivBeforeM: 150, noDivLevels: 3, twoRedsFrom: 4, lesserEvilFrom: 3, guardAheadM: 12, maxedHitsFrac: 0.6, breakevenBand: 0.3 },
    bands: [[1, 3], [4, 9], [10, 99]], minValue: 3, maxValue: 999, noRedBelowE: 8, evFraction: 0.9, hitsAssumed: 0.6 } };
```

### 8.5 `UPGRADES`, `ECONOMY`, `STARS`

```js
export const UPGRADES = {  // ids used by save.upgrades and the shop
  squad:      { name: 'Start squad',     base: 150, ranks: 10, effect: n => 5 + 2 * n,          fmt: v => `${v} soldiers` },
  fireRate:   { name: 'Fire rate',       base: 200, ranks: 10, effect: n => 2.0 * (1 + 0.1 * n), fmt: v => `${v.toFixed(1)}/s` },
  damage:     { name: 'Damage',          base: 200, ranks: 10, effect: n => 1 + 0.15 * n,        fmt: v => `×${v.toFixed(2)}` },
  coinMult:   { name: 'Coin multiplier', base: 120, ranks: 10, effect: n => 1 + 0.10 * n,        fmt: v => `×${v.toFixed(1)}` },
  bossDamage: { name: 'Boss damage',     base: 180, ranks: 10, effect: n => 1 + 0.15 * n,        fmt: v => `×${v.toFixed(2)}` } };
export const upgradeCost = (id, n) => round10(UPGRADES[id].base * Math.pow(ECONOMY.upgradeGrowth, n - 1));   // n = 1..10; round 7: the growth is a data key (1.35, was the literal 1.4)
export const ECONOMY = { kill: { walker: 1, runner: 2, crawler: 1, brute: 15, spitter: 6 }, upgradeGrowth: 1.35 /* r7 */, bossCoins: L => 15 + 5 * L, gatePass: 3, gateMax: 5, levelBonus: L => 20 + 10 * L,
  starBonus: [0, -0.5, 0, 1.0], failBonusFrac: 1.0, survivorCoins: 1.4, survivorFrom: 0.6, bossFightKillMult: 0.25,   // round-3 QA LVL-21 / LVL-23 (GDD §9.1)
  replayPayout: 0.6, endlessPayout: 0.6, gameOverKeep: 0.5, duplicate: 25 };
export const STARS = { two: 0.5, three: 0.8 };   // round-3 QA LVL-22
export function starNeed(eBoss, k) /* round 7: survivors for k stars — 0 (k ≤ 1), ceil(STARS.two·E_boss − 1e-9), ceil(STARS.three·E_boss − 1e-9) */;
export function eBossOf(L) /* round 7: E_boss of level L before its script exists — the authored value, else levelgen.curve's formula */;
// round 7: ECONOMY.breakdownOf { …, duplicate: 'piles', refund: 'piles' }; UPGRADES.*.desc = the shop card's "what a rank buys" line — what a rank does,
// never how much of a fight it wins (r7 final fix: bossDamage.desc "Every shot at a boss hits harder", was "Bosses fall faster — best for a small squad":
// BOSSES.shopScale cancels most of a damage rank on the curve and the weak floor BOSSES.ttkBand.weakMin most of it at any squad size, GDD §9.2 / §7)
export const SCORE = { kill: 10, peak: 15, survivor: 20, flipped: 200, noRedLoss: 500, endless: { metre: 1, kill: 10, gate: 25, miniBoss: 500, streakSeconds: 60, streakMult: 2, pressure: true } };   // round-3 QA LVL-17: endless score × the pressure
```

```js
           eBoss: { base: 140, growth: 1.08, cap: 600 }, runup: { base: 15, cap: 30, metres: 60, bursts: 4, burstGap: 2, pattern: 'horde' },
           gateStartM: 60, waveAfterGateM: 20, pickupRange: [0.35, 0.85], roadLerpRate: 3, loopAfter: 30 },
  endless: { chunk: 300, speed: { base: 9, per: 0.15, cap: 12 }, e: { base: 150, growth: 1.06, cap: 600 }, gates: [3, 2], gatesSwitch: 4, waves: { base: 2, per4: 1, cap: 3 }, pop: { base: 10, per: 2, cap: 16 }, miniBossEvery: 5, miniBossHpPerE: 30, rubber: { low: 10, lowBase: 8, lowFrac: 0.3, highMult: 2 },
             // round-3 QA LVL-17 (GDD §10.1, data.js documents each key): pressure { fromD, perD, hpShare, popShare, gainShare }, designMult, designRampD, liveExp, profileExp, gateEMaxD, gainCapFrac; round 2's keepMult / plusCapFrac / crowdMax / bandPerD / bandMax / heavyPerD stay
             pressure: { fromD: 4, perD: 0.07, hpShare: 1, popShare: 1, gainShare: 1 }, designMult: 9, designRampD: 3, liveExp: 0, profileExp: 0, gateEMaxD: 4, gainCapFrac: 0.35 } };
// round 5c (lead): LEVELS.endless.miniMinions { hpMult 1, pressureShare 0, profileExp 0.5 }, miniBossLeadM 100, miniBossTailM 100, miniBossWaveCut 1, miniBossHpShare 0, miniBossKillsShare 1
// round 5d (lead config T3 + docs/build/r5d-endless.md): LEVELS.endless.pop { base 8, per 4, cap 32 }, waves { base 2, per4 1, cap 3, extraFromD 12, extraEveryD 8, extraCap 2, maxPerChunk 6, minSpacingM 30 },
//   hpCapSeconds { walker 0.5, runner 0.4, crawler 0.35, brute 3.0, spitter 1.2 } (the per-zombie HP ceiling, §6.17 endlessHpCap), miniBossHold { releaseM 60 } (the road's events wait for the kill, §6.16),
//   wavePoolShare 4 (a wave ≤ a quarter of every pool, excess into the other types' headroom), poolReserve { walker 6, runner 6, crawler 10, brute 2, spitter 2 } (pool slots kept for the mini-boss's minions, spitters for toppling bodies: the generator's pool fit and level's spawn clamp budget pool − reserve)
// round-3 QA LVL-21: LEVELS.curve.assistAfterFails 2, assistStartFrac 0.08, assistMaxSteps 4, assistHint n => 'REINFORCEMENTS: +n SOLDIERS', retrySeedStep 100
// round 7 (GDD §4.4): LEVELS.curve.redCap { multi 0.5, single 0.4, keepFlipCap true } — a generated campaign '−' lane shows at most multi / single × the live count at spawn (level._capRedLanes, §6.16); keepFlipCap keeps its authored flipCap
// round-3 QA LVL-15 / LVL-22: LEAK_MODEL.calibByLevel [[L, mult], …], LEAK_MODEL.earlyRamp 0.4, WAVE_RULES.small { perE, min, heavyMinE, bruteAsWalkers, runnerPerE, runnerMin, keepWeight }, GATE_RULES.generation.noSteerMax 0.85
// round 5 (docs/build/r5-level-balance.md): LEAK_MODEL.calibByLevel extends to L30 (L13–L30 fitted); BOSSES.shopScale { exp 0.9, maxMult 10 } (was 0.75 / 4);
//   levelgen's former DATA-MISSING literals now read data: GATE_RULES.generation.designHits 0.75, lineGuards { maxTarget, maxWeight, cautiousTarget, cautiousWeight },
//   faceItem { mult, add }, secondSlotMaxErr 0.15, noSteerPenalty 2, patterns[itemPlus].aScale [0.5, 1.3]; WAVE_RULES.introHpFrac 0.8;
//   LEVELS.curve.cautiousFloor 0.2, weakLineFail 0.02, crateHintAfterM 30, coinLineEveryGates 2, generator.weakAttempts 32 (attempt budget while the best attempt's
//   weak line is under cautiousFloor); already-present keys now read by levelgen: LEVELS.curve.traceTolerance / pickupClearM / generator.*, GATE_RULES.generation.cautiousHits /
//   guards, WAVE_PATTERNS[*].weight, WAVE_RULES.burstEvery, LEVELS.endless.crates / coinLines (each read falls back to its round-4 value on a mixed build)
```

### 8.7 Level script schema (authored and generated share it)

```js
LevelScript = { id:number, name:string, seed:number, length:number, roadSpeed:number, eBoss:number, expected:[{ at:number, e:number }], events:Event[] }
Event = { at:number, type:'gates', layout:'single'|'pair'|'triple', lanes:Lane[], guards?:[{ lane:number, units:Record<type, number>, hp?:Record<type, number> }], hint?:string }
      | { at, type:'wave', pattern:'line'|'column'|'horde'|'flank'|'pincer'|'wall', units:Record<type, number>, spread?:number, x?:number, hp?:Record<type, number>, hpMult?:number, hint?:string, hintOnLoss?:string }
      | { at, type:'crate', item:string, x:number }
      | { at, type:'coins', x:number, count?:number }
      | { at, type:'hint', id:string }
      | { at, type:'runup', units:Record<type, number>, bursts = 4, burstGap = 2 /* s */, pattern = 'horde', hp?:Record<type, number> }
      | { at, type:'boss', id:string, archetype:keyof BOSSES.archetypes, hp:number /* total */, phases?:Phase[], minionHp?:Record<type, number>, mini?:boolean }
Lane  = { op:'+'|'-'|'x'|'/'|'item', value?:number /* required unless op === 'item' */,
          cap:number      /* REQUIRED for '+' (value cap), 'x' and '/' (multiplier cap: GATE_RULES.mult.cap / div.flipTo); 0 for 'item' */,
          flipCap?:number /* REQUIRED for '-': the post-flip positive cap (= base) */,
          step?:number    /* REQUIRED for 'x' and '/': weighted hits per step */,
          item?:string, hintOnHit?:string, hintOnPass?:{ ifCountAtLeast?:number, id:string }, hintIfOtherBetter?:string }
          // caps never default at runtime: generateLevel/generateChunk fill them through gateDefaults(); validateLevel fails on a missing one
Phase = { until:number, attack:string, interval:number, kills?:number, killsPct?:number, min?:number, killsPerLevel?:number, radius?:number, width?:number, band?:number, telegraph?:number, preview?:number, splashes?:number, duration?:number, onEnter?:Action[] }
        // kills resolved per attack: kills_total = (killsPct ? max(min, round(killsPct·C)) : kills) + floor(L · (killsPerLevel ?? 0))
Action = { spawn:Record<type, number> } | { attack:string, ...params } | { shield:number } | { platesOff:true, damageMult:number }
```

`at` = road distance (m) at which the object spawns at the horizon. `hint` on a gates/wave event = show at spawn; `hintOnLoss` on a wave = show on the first `squad:loss` with cause `'bite'` after the wave has spawned (hints are once-ever, so no per-wave bookkeeping is needed); `Lane.hintOnHit` = show on the first registered hit on that lane; `Lane.hintOnPass` = show on pass when `after ≥ ifCountAtLeast`; `Lane.hintIfOtherBetter` = show on pass when the un-passed lane's `wouldBe` exceeds `after`. `LEVEL_1` is the GDD §11 table transcribed verbatim (including the `at: 0` `steer` hint row and the `at: 28` wave A row) with `seed: 1001` and `expected` = the GDD §11 cautious line: `[{at:0,e:5},{at:70,e:25},{at:136,e:44},{at:160,e:42},{at:240,e:66},{at:262,e:64},{at:336,e:164},{at:362,e:158},{at:400,e:158}]`.

```js
  pauseFps: 30, menuLiteFrameMs: 40, menuSettleMs: 1000, menuSettleMaxFrameMs: 250 /* r7 flow-coins:1 (+ r7 fix: real-time settle, none above 250 ms), §4.2 */, restoreGiveUpMs: 12000 /* r7 flow-coins:4, §6.2 */,
```

```js
  sun: { color: 0xFFD2A0, intensity: 3.0, offset: [8, 12, -14], frustum: { l: -14, r: 14, t: 40, b: -12, near: 1, far: 60 }, bias: -0.0005, normalBias: 0.02, shadowRadiusPcf: 1 },
  hemi: { sky: 0x7FB0E0, ground: 0x5A4634, intensity: 0.9 },
  exposure: 1.05,

  grade: { shadowTint: 0x1E6C7A, shadowMix: 0.08, shadowLuma: 0.3, highlightTint: 0xFFB070, highlightMix: 0.10, highlightLuma: 0.7, saturation: 1.12, vignette: { strength: 0.35, a: 0.5, b: 1.4 } },
  fogColor: 0xD8744A, skyFogMix: 0.15,
```
