### 8.10 `DISTRICTS`, `DISTRICT_PROPS`, `DISTRICT_RULES`, `districtForLevel`, `districtForChunk` (round 5)

The full schema and values are in docs/DISTRICTS.md §2 and §8.1.
- `DISTRICTS` is a deep-frozen array of 6 districts with index 1–6: `{ id, index, name, label, levels, accent, sky, fog, sun, hemi, exposure, bloom, grade, road, ground, building, props, lamps, beacon, smoke, weather, zombie }`.
- `DISTRICTS[0]` reads every value from `WORLD`, `RENDER` and `PALETTE`, so L1–5 cannot drift. Every district has the same key tree.
- `DISTRICT_PROPS` maps each prop kind to `{ slot, per, placer, builtin? | tris, half, layout, colors, emissive?, poseY?, smokeY?, castShadow? }`: the 8 built-in kinds plus 16 models.js kinds.
- `DISTRICT_RULES` holds `slots`, `levelsPerDistrict` (5), `endlessChunksPerDistrict` (3), `fadeSeconds` (4), `fadeGateClearZ`, `fadeDeferMaxM`, `fadeNextGateFrac`, `swapDip`, `hiddenBeyondZ`, `edgeClearX`, `puddleReflect` (round-5 integration: 0.5, then 0.3), `puddleReflectFloor` (0.3), `puddleFloorFadeM` ([18, 35]) and `poolPuddleGlint` (0.12) (round-5 art review) and `banner`.
- Data rules (validator `E:/Temp/claude/gate-rush-evidence/r5/techart-review/tools/validate-districts.mjs`): weather never outshines the value glyph (luma·gain ≤ 0.5 on Normal, luma·gain·opacity ≤ 0.26 on Additive; embers exempt); the light azimuth stays within ±31° of −z; road light pools need `lamps.layout / lit` 'all' and `layout` 'all' in the district rotating into them; anchored smoke needs the anchor kind's `smokeY`.
- `districtForLevel(L)` and `districtForChunk(D)` return the frozen entry: ⌊(L − 1) / 5⌋ mod 6 and ⌊(D − 1) / 3⌋ mod 6; a non-finite value or one below 1 gives District 1.

---

## 9. Rendering architecture

### 9.1 Scene graph (flat; no deep Groups; `matrixAutoUpdate = false` on statics)

```
scene (fog: THREE.Fog)
├─ env.sky            Mesh SphereGeometry(150,24,12) BackSide ShaderMaterial, follows camera, renderOrder −10, frustumCulled false
├─ env.ground         Mesh PlaneGeometry(200,400) #5A4634, receiveShadow
├─ env.road           (round-5 integration: ONE Mesh, the six segment planes merged, attribute aGrSeg = segment index; the vertex shader moves segment k to uGrSegZ[k], written by _sync — one draw instead of six, frustumCulled false, vGrLocal still segment-local; was env.segments[6], each its own Mesh) PlaneGeometry(8,30,1,6) asphalt, ONE shared MeshStandardMaterial, receiveShadow; the road texture covers 8 × 15 m (repeat.set(1, 2), RepeatWrapping on v); segment k's geometry has v offset k·2 baked at build so seams tile across the ring — no UV scrolling, no onBeforeCompile on the road (segments physically move) (round 5: one `gr:road` onBeforeCompile program — district asphalt / paint multipliers, puddles, Night light pools in segment-local z — replaces the plain one, compiled at boot; docs/DISTRICTS.md §6.2)
├─ env.curbs          InstancedMesh ×2 (12)
├─ env.props          8 × InstancedMesh (capacities: barricade 48, sandbags 32, car 24, building 32, tree 40 (round 5: 60), rubble 48, lamppost 24, smoke 12), castShadow (not smoke; round 5: nor a kind with castShadow false)
├─ env.dust           Points (300), vertex-shader wrapped; round 5: re-themed per district (dust / rain / ash / spores / embers / snow; blending Additive or Normal, not a program key)
├─ squad.instances    InstancedMesh soldier (300), castShadow, customDepthMaterial
├─ zombies.*          5 × InstancedMesh (120/60/40/16/24), castShadow except crawler; zombies.hpBars InstancedMesh (260); zombies.globs InstancedMesh (32)
├─ boss.rig           Group: Bones (joints, core, plate0–3, chain0–1) + THREE SkinnedMeshes 'skin' (castShadow), 'armour', 'glow' + the Colossus shield Mesh: 3 main draws per rig (+1 shield up), Hulk Twins 6 (round-2 CHR-11, round 5b, §6.4)
├─ troops.*           ≤ 5 Mesh/Group, castShadow
├─ gates.frames       InstancedMesh (12, iFrame vec4 = typeIdx, tintT, maxed, emissiveBoost); gates.panels InstancedMesh (12, PlaneGeometry 3.6×2.4, gatePanel() ShaderMaterial transparent/DoubleSide/depthWrite false, iPanel vec4 = tintIdx, stripeMix, flash, ring01 + iScale float — ring gauge and MAX badge drawn by the panel shader); gates.glyphs InstancedMesh (60); gates.glows InstancedMesh (12, additive)   → 4 draws
├─ pickups.crates[8] Mesh (1 shared material), pickups.coins InstancedMesh (64)
├─ weapons.tracers    InstancedMesh (256, HDR instanceColor); weapons.muzzles InstancedMesh (64); weapons.rockets[8]; weapons.rail Mesh; weapons.lasers[8]
├─ fx.particlesAdd    InstancedMesh (512 allocated = high.particles/2, AdditiveBlending, depthWrite false; count ≤ params.particles/2)
├─ fx.particlesNorm   InstancedMesh (512 allocated, NormalBlending, depthWrite false; count ≤ params.particles/2)
├─ fx.floaters        InstancedMesh (96 = 24 floaters × 4 quads; layers 0 + overlay); fx.numbers InstancedMesh (128 = 32 numbers × 4 quads); fx.label (6 + 3 quads), fx.labelPlate + fx.labelClamp (1 quad each) — label / plate on render.overlayLayer only, clamp on layers 0 + overlay, drawn after bloom (round-2 FX-10, cross-group); fx.decals InstancedMesh (12, iDecal vec4 = kind, progress, size, colorIdx; 1 draw); fx.shards InstancedMesh (96)
├─ render.lights.sun  DirectionalLight + target; hemi; bossCore PointLight (off by default); laser PointLight (off)
└─ render.blobShadows InstancedMesh (567, Low tier only; slot ranges §6.3)
```

Draw-call budget (High, main pass): environment 10 + props 8 + characters 6 + boss ≤ 4 (round 5b: 3 per rig + 1 Colossus shield; the Hulk Twins' two rigs are 6) + troops ≤ 5 + gates 4 + pickups 3 (≤ 2 crates visible at once + coins 1) + weapons ≤ 4 (+ 8 rockets + 8 lasers only while active) + fx 6 (2 particles, floaters, numbers, label, decals) + shards 1 = 50 at the cap; typical frame ≈ 44. Round-5 integration (measured per frame, smoke `callsPeak`): the table had drifted — environment 17 (sky, ground, 6 road segments, curbs, manhole, smoke, dust and the 8 prop slots, with the props), troops up to 15 (sniper 4, medic 3, flamer 2, drone 3, APC 3, models.buildTroopMesh), fx 10 with the label overlay — and heavy frames (three troops, five zombie types, globs, the label) drew 51–54 main draws in every district; the road ring is now one draw (`env.road`), so the environment is 12, and the round-5 integration matrix peaks at 46 main draws (L16, High) and 44 (endless). The cap is still ≈ 60 with every troop and a boss on screen (docs/build/r5-integrate.md §5). Shadow pass ≈ 20 (casters only). Post: RenderPass 1 + UnrealBloomPass 11 fullscreen quads (5 mips × 2 blur directions + composite) + GradeOutputPass 1 = 13. Round-2 amendments: the label plate and clamp add 2 draws while the label is visible (FX-10); on composer tiers the label and its plate, plus a second clamp draw and a second floaters draw, are issued by the post-bloom overlay pass (cross-group FX-10 residual; ≤ 4 draws, counted as MAIN in callStats — they are scene draws — so the main budget sees the label as before plus at most one clamp and one floaters draw), on Low they stay in the single scene render; Medium blurs 3 bloom mips (VIS-13: post 10 instead of 14). Measurement: `renderer.info.autoReset = false`, `engine.beginFrame` calls `renderer.info.reset()`, `engine.endFrame` reads `info.render.calls` (= shadow + main + post), budget check `calls ≤ 50 + 20 + 14`; `render.draw()` samples `info.render.calls` right after the RenderPass (via the pass's `onAfterRender`-style hook) so the overlay prints the three parts separately.

### 9.2 Instancing and per-instance data

| Pool | Mesh | Per-instance data | Written by |
|---|---|---|---|
| Characters (6 classes) | `CharacterInstances` | `instanceMatrix` (pos, yaw, topple, scale) + `iAnim` (phase, speed, hitTime, fireTime) + `iState` (death01, tint rgb) — 2 attribute slots | owner's `render(alpha)`; `commit()` once per frame |
| Tracers | InstancedMesh + `instanceColor` | matrix (pos along the bullet, yaw to direction, length 0.9) + HDR colour per kind written straight into the Float32Array | weapons.render |
| Particles | InstancedMesh | `p0 v0 birth life size colorIdx kind` (static once written) | fx.emit |
| Glyph quads | InstancedMesh | matrix (billboard not needed: gates face +Z; floaters/numbers/label billboarded in-shader with a `uBillboard` flag) + `iGlyph iPopTime iColor` | gates / fx |
| Gate frames / panels | InstancedMesh | matrix + `iFrame` vec4 / `iPanel` vec4 + `iScale` (per-gate flash, tint lerp, ring, MAX — never shared uniforms) | gates.render |
| Decals | InstancedMesh | matrix + `iDecal` vec4 (kind, progress, size, colorIdx) | fx |
| HP bars | InstancedMesh | matrix + `iHp` | zombies.render |
| Props | InstancedMesh | matrix only, rewritten on scroll | env.scroll |
| Coins / shards / muzzles / blob shadows | InstancedMesh | matrix only | owners |

All `InstancedBufferAttribute`s use `DynamicDrawUsage`; `needsUpdate = true` exactly once per mesh per frame with `addUpdateRange(0, count·itemSize)`; `mesh.count = active`; `frustumCulled = false` on every pooled mesh (bounds are stale by design). Colour per instance: `instanceColor` only where listed; otherwise colour is baked per vertex (`vertexColors`) or chosen in-shader from `iType`/`colorIdx`.

### 9.3 Shadows, fog, lights

`renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true` is set ONCE at init and never toggled (toggling it does not change the lights hash, so compiled programs would keep stale shadow defines). Tier changes toggle `sun.castShadow = params.shadows` (this bumps the lights state version so programs recompile automatically). Shadow type: High `PCFSoftShadowMap`, Medium `PCFShadowMap` with `sun.shadow.radius = RENDER.sun.shadowRadiusPcf` (1) — PCFSoft's 3×3 bilinear taps are the single largest fragment cost on a Mali-G57 at 1.5 DPR; a type change sets `renderer.shadowMap.type`, `renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate = true` and `materials.markAllNeedsUpdate()`. Map size change: `sun.shadow.mapSize.set(n, n); if (sun.shadow.map) { sun.shadow.map.dispose(); sun.shadow.map = null; }` (a live map ignores a size change otherwise). Sun: `RENDER.sun` colour/intensity, ortho frustum `RENDER.sun.frustum` (left −14 right 14 top 40 bottom −12 near 1 far 60) (round 5: colour, intensity and the offset vector come from the district; the bottom bound is rescaled by the light elevation so shadows reach z ≈ −23 on the road in every district, docs/DISTRICTS.md §6.1), `bias`/`normalBias`; the sun (offset `RENDER.sun.offset` from the squad) and its target move with `squadX` every frame. Casters: soldiers, zombies (crawlers never), boss, troops, props; on Medium the character meshes beyond `shadowCasters` are excluded by setting `castShadow` per class (zombies nearest first: walker/runner/brute/spitter on, crawler off; props on). Receivers: ground, road segments. Fog: `THREE.Fog(RENDER.fogColor, WORLD.fog.near, params.fogFar)`; sky dome mixes its bottom `RENDER.skyFogMix` to the fog colour. Low tier: `sun.castShadow = false`; `render.blobShadows` (CircleGeometry(`RENDER.blobShadow.radius`, 12), #000 @ `opacity`, capacity 567) positioned under characters by each owner's `render()` through `render.blobShadowSlot(i, x, z, scale)` in its slot range (§6.3; returns immediately on other tiers).

### 9.4 Post-processing and colour

Before creating the renderer, `canvas.getContext('webgl2', { failIfMajorPerformanceCaveat: true })` is probed on a throwaway canvas, and when that returns `null` again on a fresh canvas with `false` (round 7, perf-render:2: a context only the second probe gets is one the browser itself flags as slow — Chrome refuses the first for its automatic SwiftShader fallback and for WARP — and counts as software GL like a GPU string matching the `software` heuristics rule, `isSoftwareGpu`); both `null` → `throw new Error(STRINGS.noWebGL2)` (three r170 is WebGL2-only; `showFatal` renders it with RELOAD). `renderer = new WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: initialTier === 'low' && !software /* round 7: 4x MSAA on the default framebuffer was the largest cost after resolution on software GL */, alpha: false, stencil: false, depth: true, powerPreference: 'high-performance', preserveDrawingBuffer: false })`: `antialias` is a context-creation flag decided once from the initial tier; a runtime drop to Low renders without AA (accepted); a boot on Low that later rises keeps canvas MSAA (harmless: the composer blits a fullscreen quad). `outputColorSpace = SRGBColorSpace`, `toneMapping = ACESFilmicToneMapping`, `toneMappingExposure = RENDER.exposure`, `renderer.info.autoReset = false`. Software GL (round 7) also boots on Low with a Low auto ceiling and render scale `QUALITY_TIERS.renderScale.softwareStart` (0.7) unless `?quality=` forces a tier. Console note (round 7, perf-render:4): four identical `GL Driver Message (OpenGL, Performance, GL_CLOSE_PATH_NV, High): GPU stall due to ReadPixels` warnings on a browser without GPU compositing are Chrome's software compositor copying each presented WebGL frame back to shared memory (ANGLE's Vulkan performance warning; Chrome stops repeating it after 4) — not a game readback: js/ has no readPixels / readRenderTargetPixels, and a bare page clearing one WebGL canvas per frame prints the same 4. Their real cost (a full-frame RGBA copy per frame) shrinks with the render scale.

High/Medium: `EffectComposer` with a `WebGLRenderTarget` of `HalfFloatType` when `renderer.extensions.has('EXT_color_buffer_float') || renderer.extensions.has('EXT_color_buffer_half_float')` (WebGL 2 needs either; most Android Chrome devices expose the former), otherwise `UnsignedByteType` and `params.bloom` forced to `'off'` for the session (tier label unchanged); `samples = isIOS ? params.msaaIOS : params.msaa`. Passes: `RenderPass` → `UnrealBloomPass` (`TierBloomPass`) → overlay `RenderPass` (round-2 cross-group, FX-10 residual: only `render.overlayLayer` — the count label, its plate, a second pass of its HDR clamp, the floaters — drawn into the bloomed buffer without clear, so no bloom haze lands on the digits; §6.3) → `GradeOutputPass`. Bloom: `new UnrealBloomPass(new Vector2(rtW, rtH), RENDER.bloom.strength, radius, threshold)` on High (mip 0 = rt/2, the pass's built-in 5 mips) and `new UnrealBloomPass(new Vector2(rtW/2, rtH/2), …)` on Medium (mip 0 = rt/4). Round-2 QA VIS-13: Medium blurs only `QUALITY_TIERS.med.bloomMips` (3) mips — `TierBloomPass` keeps all 5 blur materials and the 5-mip composite program (the missing mips are bound to the last blurred level with a zero tint), so High and Medium still share identical programs and a High ⇄ Medium switch links nothing (VIS-1). `rtW/rtH` = composer target size in device pixels (`w·dpr`, `h·dpr`). A tier change High ⇄ Medium disposes the pass (`pass.dispose()`) and creates a new one; `applyQuality` is the only place targets/passes are created. `GradeOutputPass extends OutputPass`: the constructor calls `super()`, then `this.material.fragmentShader = GRADE_FRAG`, `Object.assign(this.material.uniforms, { uShadowTint:{value:new Color(RENDER.grade.shadowTint)}, uShadowMix:{value:0.08}, uHighTint:{value:new Color(RENDER.grade.highlightTint)}, uHighMix:{value:0.10}, uSat:{value:1.12}, uVignette:{value:0.35}, uDesat:{value:0}, uAspect:{value:1} })`, `this.material.needsUpdate = true` (OutputPass builds a GLSL3 `RawShaderMaterial` and sets its defines in `render()`; the subclass must keep the `#include <tonemapping_fragment>` / `#include <colorspace_fragment>` order). `GRADE_FRAG` is the r170 `OutputShader.fragmentShader` with, after `#include <tonemapping_fragment>` and before `#include <colorspace_fragment>`: `luma = dot(rgb, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722)); rgb = mix(rgb, rgb·uShadowTint·2.0, uShadowMix·(1 − smoothstep(0.0, 0.3, luma))); rgb = mix(rgb, rgb·uHighTint, uHighMix·smoothstep(0.7, 1.0, luma)); rgb = mix(vec3(luma), rgb, uSat); rgb = mix(rgb, vec3(luma), uDesat); rgb *= 1 − uVignette·smoothstep(0.5, 1.4, length((vUv − 0.5)·vec2(uAspect, 1))·2.0)`. `render.setDesaturate(t01)` writes `uDesat` (used by `fx.setDesaturate`); `resize()` writes `uAspect`.

HDR materials: on the composer path tone mapping is done once by `GradeOutputPass` for every pixel (materials are never tone-mapped when rendering into a render target in r170, so `toneMapped` is irrelevant there); bloom runs on linear HDR before tone mapping, which is why colour values 2–4 (glyphs, tracers, lasers, boss core, lit windows, gate frames at `RENDER.gateFrameEmissive` 1.6) bloom. On Low the same materials are `toneMapped:false` so their 2–4 values clip to white instead of being compressed. LDR content stays `toneMapped: true` so Low gives it the same ACES curve the output pass gives it on High / Medium: 1.2.0 release (r5-art request 1), the gates' item-icon quad (an LDR pictogram, `iColor` 1) is `toneMapped: true` — it was `false` and rendered darker on Low only (main colour Y 0.36 against 0.49); High / Medium compile the identical program (tone mapping is compiled out for render targets), and the glyph fallback quad keeps `false` (HDR). Low: `renderer.render(scene, camera)` directly; gate glow quads and `+RENDER.lowTierEmissiveBoost` emissive on gate frames substitute for bloom; `setDesaturate` is a no-op.

### 9.5 Resize, DPR, context loss

Size source = `#app.clientWidth/clientHeight` read inside a `ResizeObserver` on `#app` (guarded; fallback `window.resize`), plus `screen.orientation.addEventListener('change')` when available; both feed `render.resize()` on the leading AND trailing edge of a burst (`JUICE.ui.resizeDebounceMs` 120 ms after the last event), and an event that flips portrait ⇄ landscape against the applied size resizes at once (round-2 CORE-10; engine owns the listeners, §6.2). `visualViewport` is NOT used for sizing (it shrinks with pinch-zoom and the software keyboard; inside the artifact iframe the iframe's own layout box is authoritative). `resize()`: `dpr = max(1, min(devicePixelRatio, params.dpr, sqrt(QUALITY_TIERS.pixelCap / (w·h))))` (the pixel cap may be exceeded only when the clamp to 1.0 forces it, e.g. large desktop windows) × the render scale (round 7, perf-render:1: `quality.renderScale` < 1 only under Auto on software GL (whose tier is the lowest; r7 final fix: never on a hardware GPU), floored so the backing store stays ≥ `renderScale.minBackingPx` (300) device px tall; CSS stays 100 %, so the browser upscales the canvas like page zoom and the DOM HUD stays sharp; `render.setRenderScale(s)` applies a new rung at the start of the next `draw()`, re-allocation and redraw in one task); `renderer.setPixelRatio(dpr)`; `renderer.setSize(w, h, false)` (CSS size stays 100 %) — round 7 (perf-render:6): ONLY when the size or DPR changed (`resize()` no longer forces it; the trailing resize of every burst re-allocated, i.e. cleared, an unchanged canvas), which sets `_sizeDirty` until the next draw, and engine's `_doResize` then draws once in the same task (the ResizeObserver callback or the trailing timer): a re-allocated WebGL canvas has no frame to present until it is drawn again, and Chrome composited the element without it — the bare `#app` background (#0F1626) showed behind the HUD for a frame, 250 ms and more at 4 fps (the tester's "zoom blanks the canvas for ~2 s"). (The context is `alpha: false`, so this was not a transparent buffer being composited: a test that painted `#app` magenta saw magenta, not black, in those frames.) A same-size resize changes nothing and requests no redraw; if composer: `composer.setPixelRatio(dpr); composer.setSize(w, h)` (EffectComposer captures the pixel ratio at construction, so the ratio call must come first; this resizes the HalfFloat target and every bloom mip in place — no reallocation, no leak); camera aspect + preset (§2.1, portrait when `aspect < 0.9`) tweened over `WORLD.camera.presetTween`; `uAspect`; `ui.refit()`; `render.dirty = true`; emits `resize`. Render targets are recreated ONLY in `applyQuality` when `params.msaa` or `params.bloom` changes, and then the old `WebGLRenderTarget.dispose()` and `bloomPass.dispose()` are called before the new ones are built.

Context loss: `webglcontextlost` → `preventDefault`, `ui.showOverlay(STRINGS.reloadingGfx)`, engine pauses (round 7: and arms the give-up — after `QUALITY_TIERS.restoreGiveUpMs` (12 s) of visible time without a restore the fatal box `STRINGS.errors.gpuGone` with RELOAD covers the overlay, §6.2); `webglcontextrestored` → `render.rebuild()`, `ui.hideOverlay()`. three r170 re-creates programs/buffers/textures lazily on restore, so `rebuild()` only: re-applies `renderer.setPixelRatio/setSize/shadowMap.type/toneMapping/outputColorSpace`, calls `composer.setSize`, sets `renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate = true`, calls `textures.restoreAll()` — round 6: every `TextureFactory` canvas is RE-DRAWN from its recipe, then `texture.needsUpdate = true` (the canvases do NOT necessarily persist: see below) — and marks `render.dirty`. No `dispose()` and no re-creation of materials. Round-2 QA VIS-21: render's own lost-context GL objects (composer and bloom targets, the stale bloom pass, the warm target, `sun.shadow.map`) are DROPPED without `dispose()` (disposing them logged `INVALID_OPERATION` warnings), the composer is rebuilt for the committed tier, and the canvas is resized only when size / DPR changed; three's shared full-screen quad geometry (Pass.js) is never disposed by a retired pass.

Procedural-canvas loss (round 6, docs/build/r6-textures.md — the owner's phone lost every digit after 2 h asleep): Chrome GPU-backs 2D canvases by default, and when Android reclaims the GPU process every such canvas fires `contextlost` and comes back (`contextrestored`) BLANK while the WebGL context is lost and restored; the old `rebuild()` then uploaded the blank canvases (gate digits, MAX, count label, floaters, damage numbers, item icons, road paint, windows — reproduced exactly with CDP `Browser.crashGpuProcess`). Defences, all in `TextureFactory`: (1) the kept canvases are CPU-backed (`getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true })`) and survive a GPU loss; each recipe is rasterised on a scratch canvas with the default (GPU) context and copied in as pixels (`getImageData` → `putImageData`; NOT `drawImage`, which turns the destination into a GPU-backed canvas that is lost with the GPU again), falling back to painting directly when the scratch is lost; smoke (putImageData) paints directly. Cost at 4× CPU, paint + upload of all canvases: 142 ms (GPU-only canvases 113 ms, CPU rasterisation 197 ms); the build's road and atlas steps become ≈ 95–145 ms tasks (boot acceptance: no gap > 300 ms). (2) `restoreAll()` on `webglcontextrestored` re-draws every canvas (fixed seeds: pixel-identical) and re-uploads. (3) `contextlost` / `contextrestored` listeners on every kept canvas (for a browser that ignores the hint): lost marks the canvas (no `preventDefault` — on a 2D context that forbids the restore), restored re-draws that recipe + `needsUpdate`. (4) `verify()` on `visibilitychange` → visible, `pageshow` and the lifecycle `resume`: a 1×1 `getImageData` per canvas at a pixel its recipe always inks (the atlas: the ÷ bar centre); any blank → re-draw all; an atlas drawn with the fallback face is re-drawn once `document.fonts` has Lilita One (`_ensureFont` re-requests it otherwise; `document.fonts` 'loadingdone' also triggers that re-draw). A re-draw sets `render.dirty` through `onRepaint`. A normal resume costs < 1 ms (no re-draw); a re-draw of everything ≈ 40 ms on desktop, behind the restore overlay or in the one resume frame after a real loss. `setRoadAnisotropy` re-draws the road first if its probe reads blank. UI icon pills are data / blob URLs and unaffected.

### 9.6 Procedural textures

All canvases are drawn in `TextureFactory.build()` during boot (≈ 120 ms total, progress reported), after the renderer exists, and cached on the factory with their paint recipe (round 6: one record per recipe — canvases, textures, `paint(canvases)`, probe pixel, 2D-loss mask; re-drawn only by the §9.5 loss paths); `needsUpdate` is set once after drawing. No module creates a canvas after boot except `ui` (icon data URLs come from the factory cache) and `tools/make-icons.js` (node). Sizes: road 512×1024, digit atlas 1024×256, glow 128×128, smoke 256×256, windows 256×256, particle 64×64, muzzle 128×64, dust 32×32, icons 512×64. `colorSpace = SRGBColorSpace` on colour maps; `anisotropy = min(params.anisotropy, renderer.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy())` on the road (8 / 4 / 1 by tier) and 4 on the digit atlas; `generateMipmaps` true for road, windows and the digit atlas (`LinearMipmapLinearFilter` — mips only affect minification, i.e. the 17–36 px far gates, where no mips would shimmer; magnification stays `LinearFilter`).

---

## 10. UI contract

### 10.1 DOM structure (identical in both shells)

```html
<div id="app" data-shell="index|artifact" tabindex="-1">
  <canvas id="gl-canvas" aria-hidden="true"></canvas>
  <div id="ui">
    <div id="hud" class="screen" hidden>
      <button id="btn-pause" class="icon-btn" aria-label="Pause">‖</button>
      <div id="progress"><span id="progress-label">LVL 1</span><div id="progress-bar"><div id="progress-fill"></div><div id="progress-squad"></div><div id="progress-skull"></div><div id="progress-ticks"></div></div></div>
      <div id="coins" class="pill"><span class="coin-icon"></span><span id="coins-value">0</span></div>
      <div id="powerups"><!-- ≤ 3 × <div class="pill pu" data-id><div class="ring"></div><span class="pu-icon"></span><span class="pu-time"></span></div>; round 4: 32 px ring, the pill's ::before is a light disc under the 20 px icon --></div>
      <div id="shield" class="pill" hidden><span class="shield-icon"></span><span id="shield-value"></span></div>
      <button id="btn-fire" type="button" aria-label="FIRE" hidden><span class="fire-ring"></span><span class="fire-disc"></span><span class="fire-icon" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="fire-label" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="fire-badge" aria-hidden="true" hidden></span><span class="fire-key" aria-hidden="true"></span></button>   <!-- round 6; round 7: the SPACE key cap (css: fine pointers only) -->
      <div id="bossbar" hidden><div id="boss-name"></div><div id="boss-track"><div id="boss-fill"></div><i class="tick" style="left:60%"></i><i class="tick" style="left:25%"></i></div></div>
      <div id="letterbox" class="lb-top" hidden></div><div id="letterbox-bottom" class="lb-bottom" hidden></div>
      <div id="hint" hidden><span id="hint-hand"></span><span id="hint-text"></span></div>
      <div id="namecard" hidden></div>   <!-- ui builds .nc-label (BOSS / round 7: DISTRICT FINALE) + .nc-name + .nc-tag (round 7: the archetype's tagline); #boss-name holds .bn-name + .bn-phase (round 7: the phase flash); #letterbox-bottom gets .lb-count (round 7: the squad count during the intro dolly) -->
      <div id="district-banner" role="status" aria-live="polite" hidden><span class="db-kicker" hidden></span><span class="db-text"></span></div>   <!-- round 5: district intro banner, docs/DISTRICTS.md §5; round 7: .db-kicker ("NEW DISTRICT 2") on the big card (.db-new) -->
      <div id="newcard" role="status" aria-live="polite" hidden><span class="ncd-icon" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="ncd-body"><span class="ncd-kicker">NEW</span><span class="ncd-name"></span><span class="ncd-line"></span></span></div>   <!-- round 7 (teaching:5): the NEW card -->
      <div id="flash"></div><div id="vignette"></div><div id="coin-flights"></div>
    </div>
    <div id="scr-boot" class="screen" role="dialog" aria-modal="true"><div class="logo font-wait">GATE RUSH</div><div id="boot-bar" role="progressbar" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100" aria-valuenow="0" aria-labelledby="boot-text"><div id="boot-fill" class="creep"></div></div><div id="boot-text">Building the apocalypse…</div></div>
    <div id="scr-title" class="screen" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" hidden>
      <button id="btn-settings" class="icon-btn" aria-label="Settings">⚙</button><button id="btn-guide" class="icon-btn" aria-label="Field guide">?</button><div id="title-coins" class="pill"></div>   <!-- round 7: the FIELD GUIDE's "?" (second slot; #btn-fullscreen third) -->
      <div class="logo logo-big">GATE RUSH</div>
      <button id="btn-play" class="btn primary">PLAY</button><div id="title-caption" class="caption"></div>
      <div class="row"><button id="btn-shop" class="btn secondary">SHOP</button><button id="btn-levels" class="btn secondary">LEVELS</button></div>
      <button id="btn-endless" class="btn secondary" hidden>ENDLESS</button><button id="btn-fullscreen" class="icon-btn" aria-label="Fullscreen" hidden>⛶</button>
      <button id="btn-update" class="pill update-pill" hidden><span class="upd-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="upd-text"></span></button>   <!-- round 5: "Update ready — tap to reload" (sw.js v4, §15.4); index shell only -->
      <div id="version" class="micro"></div>
    </div>
    <div id="scr-levels" class="screen" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" hidden><h1>LEVELS</h1><p id="levels-legend" class="caption"><span class="legend-skull" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="legend-text">DISTRICT FINALE · TOUGHER BOSS</span></p><div id="level-grid"></div><button class="btn secondary back" data-back>BACK</button></div>
    <div id="scr-shop" class="screen" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" hidden><h1>SHOP</h1><div id="shop-coins" class="pill"></div><div id="shop-note" class="caption"></div><div id="shop-list"><!-- 5 × .card[data-id] with .card-icon, .card-body (.card-name .card-what .pips .card-effect), .card-act (button.buy .card-then .card-estimate); round 7: #shop-note, .card-what, .card-then --></div><button class="btn secondary back" data-back>BACK</button></div>
    <div id="scr-settings" class="screen overlay" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" hidden>   <!-- round 7: each row label is a .row-label span (ui writes it from STRINGS.settings) -->
      <h1>SETTINGS</h1>
      <label class="row"><span class="row-label">Sound</span><input id="set-sound" type="range" min="0" max="100"></label>
      <label class="row"><span class="row-label">Music</span><input id="set-music" type="range" min="0" max="100"></label>
      <label class="row"><span class="row-label">Haptics</span><input id="set-haptics" type="checkbox" class="toggle"></label>
      <label class="row"><span class="row-label">Reduced motion</span><input id="set-reduced" type="checkbox" class="toggle"></label>
      <label class="row"><span class="row-label">High-contrast gates</span><input id="set-hc" type="checkbox" class="toggle"></label>
      <label class="row"><span class="row-label">Large text</span><input id="set-large" type="checkbox" class="toggle"></label>
      <div class="row"><span class="row-label">Drag sensitivity</span><div class="seg" id="set-sens" data-options="0.75,1,1.3"></div></div>
      <label class="row" id="row-fullscreen" hidden><span class="row-label">Fullscreen</span><input id="set-fullscreen" type="checkbox" class="toggle"></label>   <!-- round 7: shown where fullscreen is available (ui._fsAvailable) -->
      <div class="row" id="row-quality"><span class="row-label">Quality</span><div class="seg" id="set-quality" data-options="auto,low,med,high"></div></div>   <!-- r7 fix: the last control row -->
      <p id="quality-note" class="row-note">Auto adapts to your device and is fastest on a slow one. High looks best; switching can pause a few seconds.</p>   <!-- round 7 -->
      <button class="btn secondary back" data-back>BACK</button>
      <button id="btn-reset-save" class="btn danger">RESET SAVE</button>
    </div>
    <div id="scr-guide" class="screen overlay" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" hidden><h1>FIELD GUIDE</h1><div id="guide-list" role="list"></div><button class="btn secondary back" data-back>BACK</button></div>   <!-- round 7 (teaching:5): ui builds the sections (.gd-head, .gd-note) and rows (.gd-row > .ci icon + .gd-body > .gd-name (.gd-title .gd-new) + .gd-line) once -->
    <div id="scr-pause" class="screen overlay" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" hidden><div class="panel"><button id="btn-pause-guide" class="icon-btn" aria-label="Field guide">?</button><h1>PAUSED</h1><button id="btn-resume" class="btn primary">RESUME</button><button id="btn-restart" class="btn secondary">RESTART</button><button id="btn-pause-settings" class="btn secondary">SETTINGS</button><button id="btn-quit" class="btn secondary">QUIT</button><p id="pause-controls" class="micro"></p></div></div>   <!-- round 7: #pause-controls -->
    <div id="scr-complete" class="screen" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" hidden><button id="btn-complete-home" class="icon-btn home-btn" aria-label="Home"><svg class="home-glyph" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="…"/></svg></button><h1 id="complete-title">LEVEL 1 CLEAR</h1><div id="stars"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div><div id="complete-coins" class="h2"></div><ul id="complete-breakdown"></ul><div id="complete-best" class="caption"></div><button id="btn-next" class="btn primary">NEXT</button><div class="row"><button id="btn-replay" class="btn secondary">REPLAY</button><button id="btn-complete-shop" class="btn secondary">SHOP</button></div></div>
    <div id="scr-gameover" class="screen" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" hidden><button id="btn-home" class="icon-btn home-btn" aria-label="Home"><svg class="home-glyph" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="…"/></svg></button><div id="gameover-level" class="kicker"></div><h1 class="danger">SQUAD LOST</h1><div id="gameover-info" class="caption"></div><button id="btn-retry" class="btn primary">RETRY</button><div id="retry-note" class="micro" hidden>New road each retry</div><div class="row"><button id="btn-gameover-shop" class="btn secondary">SHOP</button></div></div>   <!-- round 7: #retry-note -->
    <div id="scr-endless" class="screen" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" hidden><button id="btn-endless-home" class="icon-btn home-btn" aria-label="Home"><svg class="home-glyph" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="…"/></svg></button><h1>RUN OVER</h1><div id="endless-stats"></div><button id="btn-endless-retry" class="btn primary">RETRY</button></div>
    <!-- round 4: the three results screens open with the same HOME icon button (48 × 48, absolute top-left inside the safe area, out of the flex column); its house is inline SVG in currentColor and ui writes only its aria-label (STRINGS.aria.home), never textContent. The BOOTGUARD script removes the boot logo's .font-wait once document.fonts.load('400 1em "Lilita One"') settles or after 1.2 s. -->
    <div id="overlay-msg" class="screen overlay" hidden><div class="panel" id="overlay-text"></div></div>
    <div id="error-box" role="alert" hidden><div id="error-text"></div><button id="btn-reload" class="btn primary">RELOAD</button></div>
    <pre id="debug" hidden></pre>
  </div>
</div>
```

Screens are `position: absolute; inset: 0` flex columns inside `#ui` (which fills `#app`); exactly one non-overlay screen is visible at a time (`hidden` attribute), overlays (`settings`, `guide` (round 7), `pause`, `overlay-msg`) stack above it. `showScreen(id)` sets `hidden`, moves focus to the first button, emits `ui:screen`, sets `render.dirty`, and applies a 160 ms fade (`.screen.enter`), disabled under reduced motion. The HUD is visible in `playing`, `paused` and during `complete`/`gameover` behind the panel — but the world-space squad count label (fx, not DOM) is not: it fades out over 0.25 s on entering `complete`/`gameover` (round-2 QA FX-11, GDD §9.3 / §14). Nothing in the UI layer intercepts pointer events except interactive controls and modal overlays (§10.2), so steering drags reach `#app` wherever they start.

Round 7 (scrolling, layout:0 / :1 / :5). Scroll boxes: `#level-grid`, `#shop-list`, `#endless-stats`, `#scr-settings` (as before) and, as a safety net, `#scr-title`, `#scr-complete`, `#scr-gameover`, `#scr-endless`, `#scr-pause` (`overflow-y: auto`, `overscroll-behavior: contain`, `touch-action: pan-y`, hidden scrollbar; they scroll only when their stack overflows, and the centred ones centre with `justify-content: safe center`, so an overflowing stack starts at its top). `#scr-title` stays `pointer-events: none`. The decorative `#scr-complete::before` sunburst and the `#scr-title::before` scrim are `position: fixed` (same place: the screen box is the viewport) so they add no scroll range and stay put. Mouse users (`(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)`) get a thin scrollbar on the four list boxes (`scrollbar-gutter: stable both-edges` on the three lists); touch screens keep `::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none }`. ui forwards input to the current screen's scroll box, by the `SCROLL_LIST_ID` table in ui.js (screen id → box id: title → `scr-title`, levels → `level-grid`, shop → `shop-list`, endless → `endless-stats`, guide → `guide-list` (round 7, WP-D3); add one entry to give another screen the same forwarding): a `wheel` on `#app` (passive listener, `_wheel`) scrolls it by `deltaY` (× 40 px per line, × the box height per page) unless ctrl is held (zoom) or the browser scrolls that wheel itself (`_nativeScroll`: the target or an ancestor below `#app` is a vertical `auto` / `scroll` box whose content overflows — the list itself, a results screen that scrolls, SETTINGS); PageUp / PageDown / Home / End (`_pageKey`, in `_key`, no modifier) scroll it by 0.85 of its height / to its ends while the focus is outside it (SHOP opens with the focus on BACK; inside the box the browser scrolls the focused control's own box). Both write `scrollTop` directly in the DOM handler, like `_key` / `_click`. r7 fix round 3 (edge fades): `#level-grid`, `#shop-list` and `#guide-list` (`FADE_LIST_IDS`) mask an edge only while more content lies beyond it — css `--fade-t` / `--fade-b` are 0 unless the list has `.fade-t` (scrolled down) / `.fade-b` (not at its end); `ui._listFade(list)` sets them from `scrollTop` / `scrollHeight` / `clientHeight` on the list's passive `scroll` event and from the ResizeObserver (`_observeList`: the box and its rows — a screen shown, a resize, a shop card whose estimate line came or went; the lazily built grid and guide are observed when built), writing on change. The always-on 18 px shop fade had dimmed the last card when the list fitted exactly or, up to 340 px tall, at its end. r7 final fix (lead decision 3): an edge fades only when CONTENT lies beyond it — the content spans [`paddingTop`, `scrollHeight` − `paddingBottom`] of the scroll range (the list's live computed style, `_fadeStyle`, one `getComputedStyle` per list per session), so `.fade-t` needs `scrollTop` > `paddingTop` + 1 and `.fade-b` `scrollTop` < max − `paddingBottom` − 1; a list whose cards fitted while its own bottom pad overflowed (360 × 640; Large text 568 × 320 / 585 × 340) had dimmed its last card on open. Landscape SETTINGS (a grid up to 900 px tall) has `reading-flow: grid-rows`, so Tab and the reading order follow the rows (BACK / RESET SAVE in the title row and Quality first under 560 px wide, all last in the DOM); browsers without `reading-flow` keep the DOM order.

### 10.2 CSS variables (`:root` in `css/game.css`)

`--ui` is a unitless number written by `ui.refit()` (called from `init()` and from `render.resize()`): `const w = app.clientWidth, h = app.clientHeight; root.style.setProperty('--ui', clamp(0.85, Math.min(w / 390, h / 844), 1.25).toFixed(3))`; `css/game.css` declares only the fallback `:root { --ui: 1; }` (a `clamp()` mixing `<number>`s with `100vw/390` lengths is invalid CSS, and `100vh` is wrong on iOS Safari anyway). All viewport-height layout uses `100%` of `#app`, never `100vh`. Safe areas: `--safe-top: max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-top))`, `--safe-bottom: max(16px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom))`, `--safe-left: max(8px, env(safe-area-inset-left))`, `--safe-right: max(8px, env(safe-area-inset-right))` — HUD corners use all four; inside the artifact iframe `env()` is 0 and the `max()` fallbacks apply. Fonts/colours: `--font-display: "Lilita One", "Arial Black", Impact, sans-serif`, `--font-body: "Nunito", system-ui, sans-serif`, `--bg-panel: rgba(15,22,38,0.88)`, `--primary: #FFB02E`, `--primary-text: #1A1200`, `--primary-border: #B5741A`, `--secondary: #2E9BFF`, `--secondary-text: #06111F`, `--danger: #FF3B4E`, `--success: #3BE57A`, `--text: #F4F1EA`, `--text-muted: #9AA3B5`, `--coin: #FFD23F`, `--boss-bar: #FF3B4E`, `--boss-track: #2A0F14`, `--focus: #2E9BFF`, `--radius: 16px`, `--thumb-zone: 180px`, type scale `--fs-display: calc(56px * var(--ui))` … `--fs-micro: calc(12px * var(--ui))` with a 12 px floor since round-2 QA UI-27 (`--fs-small: max(12.5px, …)`, `--fs-micro: max(12px, …)`, `--fs-readout: max(12px, 13px·ui)` for `.card-effect` / `.tile-stars`); hint pill variables written by `ui.init` from data (UI-18): `--hint-bottom` (`JUICE.ui.hintBottomPx` 28, pill bottom-anchored at safe-bottom + 28·u), `--hint-maxw` (`hintMaxWidthPct` 86 %), `--hint-alpha` (`hintAlpha` 0.72) — `--hint-y` is retired; `html.large-text` multiplies the scale by 1.15; `html.reduced-motion` zeroes transition durations; `html.hc-gates` is read by render for panel opacity.

Layout and pointer rules (the steering contract depends on them):

```css
html { -webkit-text-size-adjust: 100%; }
html, body, #app { touch-action: none; overscroll-behavior: none; -webkit-user-select: none; user-select: none; -webkit-touch-callout: none; }
body { margin: 0; background: #0F1626; }
#app { position: fixed; inset: 0; overflow: hidden; outline: none; }
#gl-canvas { position: absolute; inset: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%; display: block; }
#ui, .screen { pointer-events: none; }                                   /* drags fall through to #app */
.screen > *, .btn, .icon-btn, .pill, .seg, input, .card, #error-box, #overlay-msg .panel { pointer-events: auto; }
#scr-pause, #scr-settings, #scr-boot, #overlay-msg, #scr-complete, #scr-gameover, #scr-endless { pointer-events: auto; }  /* modal: block the game */
#hud > :not(#btn-pause):not(#btn-fire) { pointer-events: none; }         /* HUD chrome never swallows a drag (round 6: FIRE is a button) */
#flash, #vignette, #coin-flights, #letterbox, #letterbox-bottom { pointer-events: none; }
```

Engine ignores a `pointerdown` for steering when `event.target.closest('button, input, .seg, .card, [data-ui]')` matches (§6.2); everything else bubbles to `#app`.

Round 7 (flow-coins:2, screen switching). `state:change` still only stores the pending screen (`_onState`), but it is applied (`_flushPendingScreen`: clears the fallback timer and `.starting`, then `showScreen`) at the first of: the end of the `_click` / `_key` task whose button or key set the state (DOM writes only; focus still moves at the start of the next `update()`), the next `update()`, or a `JUICE.ui.pendingScreenFallbackMs` (250 ms) `setTimeout` armed by `_onState` — so a starved animation frame can never hold a screen switch. Stale-click guard: the window-capture `pointerdown` stores `Event.timeStamp` (trusted presses); `showScreen` to a new target sets `_shownStamp = ∞` and `_shownAt = performance.now()` and marks the frame that will render the switch — the current one when it runs inside `update()`, else the next (`_shownWait`); that frame arms a 0 ms timer (`_armPaintMark`), which runs once its rendering is done and sets `_shownStamp = min(now, _shownAt + JUICE.ui.staleClickMaxMs)`; a `pointerdown` handled before that timer (Chrome runs queued input first, so it was pressed during the painting frame) closes the mark at that instant. `_click` drops a trusted click with `detail > 0` whose press is older than the stamp (while it is unknown: `_shownAt + staleClickMaxMs`) — it was aimed at the screen this one replaced (a second NEXT pressed during the first one's long start, a results HOME that lands on the HUD pause button). A press after the paint always counts. Keyboard / assistive clicks (`detail` 0) and synthetic ones (engine's Back guard `btn.click()`, harness `element.click()`) are never dropped; without `performance.now()` the guard is off. Round 7 fix (`_staleAt(stamp, id)`): (a) the HUD of a new run (`showScreen(null)` from any screen but `pause`: `_hudArm`) holds its `#btn-pause` and `#btn-fire` for `JUICE.ui.hudArmMs` (1000 ms) after its paint — the results HOME press 0.73 s after the next level painted still paused it; a resume never arms, Esc / P / Space are keys; (b) `_firePointer` applies the same rule to FIRE's press (it fires on `pointerdown`); (c) `#btn-reload` (the error box, above every screen) is never stale; (d) a switch outside `update()` also requests ui's own animation frame (`_paintRaf` → `_onPaintFrame`, which consumes `_shownWait`), so a stopped engine loop (frame error) does not leave the screen "unpainted" for `staleClickMaxMs`. Only a page whose `requestAnimationFrame` itself is starved while it still paints (a harness stub; a real browser runs the animation-frame callbacks in the same rendering step that paints) falls back to `staleClickMaxMs`.

### 10.3 Which events update which element

| Element | Event / source | Update rule |
|---|---|---|
| `#coins-value` | `coins:change` → `total` (= saved coins + run coins) | text on change; 300 ms count tween via rAF in ui. Round 7 (flow-coins:5 / :6 / :7): the ONE wallet model (`_coinValue` / `_coinTo` / the tween) writes `#coins-value`, `#title-coins` and `#shop-coins` together (`_writeWallet`, on change; `fmtCoins` full number); outside play only the save moves it (`_walletSync(coins, animate)`: a purchase tweens once from the value on screen; title / shop fills set it; not while playing / paused, where `coins:change` stays authoritative); the tween runs on real time (rdt = the performance.now() step, ≤ `JUICE.ui.realDtMax` 0.25 s); during the fresh results theatre the count drives it (`_cmpDrive`, see `#scr-complete`) |
| `#title-coins`, `#shop-coins` | `save:change`, `showScreen` | `save.data.coins` — round 7: views of the one wallet model above (no separate shop count-down any more) |
| `#progress-fill`, `#progress-squad` | `level:progress` (10 Hz) | compositor-only transforms (round-2 QA UI-22): the full-width fill slides inside a rounded clip (`translateX(f·100 − 100 %)`), the full-width marker by `translateX(f·100 %)`; strings from tables built once, written only when the 0.1 % step changes, no transition |
| `#progress-skull` | `level:runup` → class `pulse` | 1 Hz pulse |
| `#progress-ticks` | `level:start` (script events of type `gates` with an item lane, `crate`) | 6 px ticks at `at/length` |
| `#powerups` pills | `weapon:start/end`, `gear:start/end` — only ids with a finite `duration` get a pill (weapons, chip, booster); armor uses `#shield`, magnet has no pill; `ui.update` reads `weapons.weaponTimeLeft`/`gear` at 10 Hz | ring `conic-gradient` by fraction, seconds text; blink class in the last 2 s |
| `#shield` | `armor:change` | hidden when plates = 0, and (round 3) while the projected count label is off the visible band (outside the app box, or under a letterbox bar while they are up), and (round-3 FTUE) while `render.dollying` (the boss-intro dolly: the label is faded by fx and the clamp parked the pill alone in the bottom bar) |
| `#bossbar` | `boss:spawn` (slide in 400 ms, name from the payload), `boss:hp`, `boss:phase`, `boss:shield` (bar greyed while `up`), `boss:dead` (hide after 1.5 s), `level:start` (hide immediately) | fill width, colour by phase (default / orange / red pulsing). Round 7 (teaching:3): `#boss-name` holds `.bn-name` (the archetype) and `.bn-phase`; `boss:phase` ≥ 2 shows "PHASE 2" / "ENRAGED" (`STRINGS.boss.phase(n)` / `enraged`) in place of the name for `JUICE.ui.bossPhaseLabelSeconds` of theatre time (`#bossbar.phase-flash` hides `.bn-name`; r7 fix: at every size — "NAME · PHASE 2" ran into the progress skull where the bar hangs under the progress bar). With Large text the bar's top is safe-top + 124u (was 110u) outside the landscape ≤ 500 px layout, the boss-on power-up row with it. Round 7 integration: in that layout under 640 px wide the `#progress-label` ("LEVEL N") is hidden while `#hud.boss-on` (and the bar is 160u under 480 px), so the progress skull no longer sits on the boss bar's left end; r7 fix round 2: there `#boss-name` is left-aligned over the bar and runs 36u past its right end, into the gap before the coin pill, so "SPITTER QUEEN" / "COLOSSUS PRIME" print in full at 512 × 297 (ellipsized on a 105 px box, as in 1.2.2); r7 fix round 3: at most to the HUD coin pill — `right: −min(36u, --bn-room)`, `--bn-room` = the px between the track's right end and `#coins`' left edge + the name's 4 px right pad − 1 (`ui._bossNameRoom`, from the ResizeObserver that now also watches `#coins` and `#boss-track`: layout offsets in `#hud`'s frame, written on change), so with Large text and a 4–5 digit balance the name ellipsizes before the pill instead of running 7 px into it |
| `#district-banner` (round 5, docs/DISTRICTS.md §5) | `district:change` with `banner: true` while `playing` (label, accent → `--db-accent` / `--db-glow`); `level:start`, leaving `playing` / `paused`, `#hud.boss-on` and a newer banner clear it | written only when a banner shows (text, kicker, accent, `.db-new`). Round 7 (explain:4): `first` → the big card `.db-new` — `.db-kicker` "NEW DISTRICT n" (`STRINGS.district.kicker`; "ENDLESS RUN" at an endless start) over the name a size up, `DISTRICT_RULES.banner.newSeconds` (2.8 s); otherwise the small tag "NAME · k/5" / "NAME · FINALE" (`STRINGS.district.tag` / `finale` from `step` / `steps`), 1.6 s — at every run start, retries included; r7 fix: an endless start that is not new (`step` 0: a RETRY) shows the name alone (the round-5 `label` only for a payload without a name). One Web Animation (0.22 s fade + 6 px rise, hold, 0.3 s fade; the big card also scales 0.96 → 1), created paused: its `currentTime` follows the theatre clock (`_updateBanner`, whole ms, written on change), so a slow machine shows the whole fade and a pause holds it; hidden at a `_tclock` deadline; reduced motion: opacity only. A request while the boss bar is up is dropped. Centred in the `#powerups` row; landscape phones (max-height 500): top-left under the pause button, ≤ 50 % − safe-left − 48 px, the name a size down (585 × 340: "OVERCAST HIGHWAY · FINALE" fits; the old 100 px margin ellipsized "NIGHT …"). The prewarm runs the big variant with the longest name |
| `#btn-fire` (round 6, stored weapons) | every frame from `weapons.storedCount` / `storedId(0)` / `weapon` / `weaponTimeLeft` (getter reads; DOM written only on change), `level:start` | shown while a weapon is stored or running; `.ready` (nothing running): the next stored weapon's 128 px pictogram in a full ring of its colour, halo pulse (static under reduced motion), FIRE tag; `.busy` (one running): the ring drains with its time left, the pictogram (next stored, else the running one) dims, `aria-disabled`; `.fire-badge` "×2" (`STRINGS.fire.badge`) with two stored; aria-label `STRINGS.fire.aria` / `ariaBusy`. Input: `pointerdown` (primary, `preventDefault`: no focus, keyboard steering stays on `#app`) / Space while `playing` / gamepad A (rising edge, polled only after `gamepadconnected` while the button shows) / a bare click (Enter, assistive tech; the click after a pointer or key activation is swallowed) → `weapons.requestFire()`; refused → a small head-shake; no `ui:button` (the weapon sting is the feedback). Icon flights of weapon pickups land on its pictogram (`powerupIconScreenPos`). `#hud.fire-on` while shown: the bottom hint pill moves left of it. Round 7: `.fire-key` ("SPACE", `STRINGS.fire.key`) shows under the FIRE tag on `(pointer: fine)`, not while busy; Space / gamepad A with nothing stored or running → `_fireEmpty()`: class `empty` (dark ring, dim disc, no pictogram, "EMPTY" tag, `aria-label` `STRINGS.fire.ariaEmpty`, pointer-events none), unhidden with `#hud.fire-on` and `KF_FIRE_EMPTY` for `JUICE.ui.fireEmptyMs` (one at a time; a weapon arriving meanwhile ends it and pops in as usual); gamepad A is polled all through a run (after `gamepadconnected`), not only while the button shows |
| `#hud.pu-on` (round 5) | visible power-up pill count crosses 0 (`_syncPills`) | the district banner drops a row (`+ --pill-h + 8u`) in portrait |
| `#title-caption` `.caption-district` (round 5) | `showScreen('title')` | `districtForLevel(highestUnlocked).label` with an accent dot (`--d-accent`) as a second caption line — the district the title scene shows (DISTRICTS.md §3.5) |
| `#level-grid .tile` band (round 5) | `_fillLevels` (tile built / renumbered) | `.tile-band` child: `--d-accent` rule at the top (longer on `.band-start`, the district's first level), `--d-tint` wash at the foot; `data-district`; the aria-label ends with the district name |
| `#btn-update` (round 5) | the worker registration: a worker `installed` while another is `active` (`updatefound` / `statechange`, or `reg.waiting` at init); a `controllerchange` caused by another tab | shown on the title (`#scr-title.has-update` gives it a row); tap → `postMessage({ type: 'SKIP_WAITING' })` to the waiting worker, reload on `controllerchange` (fallback reload after 4 s); `registration.update()` 2.5 s after the launch's first title, then at most every 30 min on entering the title. Never in the artifact shells (`ctx.config.isArtifact`) |
| `#letterbox*`, `#namecard` | `boss:intro` → letterbox on (not for `mini`), name card; round 7 (perf-render:7, lead decision): `boss:fight` → letterbox off (the fight is full screen; at 200 % zoom the bars took 48 of 297 px), `boss:dead` of a campaign boss → on again for the death cinematic; `level:start` → off; the results screen hides the HUD under it | round 7: the name card is `.nc-label` "BOSS" or "DISTRICT FINALE" (explain:3, `STRINGS.boss.finale` on `BOSSES.milestones`), `.nc-name`, `.nc-tag` (teaching:3, `STRINGS.boss.tagline[archetype]` from `boss:spawn.archetype`; r7 fix: built as `.nc-ph` phrase boxes — inline-blocks split at " · " and DASH, each ending in its `.nc-dot` mark — with a `.nc-gap` between them, the only break opportunity; the read-first phase of the next update (`_breakTagline`, layout clean, the card still at its pop-in's start) turns a gap whose next phrase starts lower into the line break (`.nc-br`) and hides the mark before it, so no line ends on " ·"); shown `BOSSES.intro.nameCard` s, the first time ever per archetype (save hint `bosscard.<archetype>`) `BOSSES.intro.fightAfter` s. `.lb-count` (explain:6) in `#letterbox-bottom`: while the bars are up AND `render.dollying` (the intro dolly, whose camera drops the squad under the bars while fx fades its label) it shows "N SOLDIERS" (`STRINGS.boss.squad`, `squad.count`, written on change) — at 2–4 fps the intro lasted 13–24 s with no count on screen |
| `#hint` | `hint:show` (`{ id, text, hold, until, priority, event }`), `hint:release` | round-2 QA UI-25: **queued** — the pill on screen plus at most `JUICE.ui.hintQueueMax` (round 7: 3) waiting, de-duplicated by id, dropped when older than `JUICE.ui.hintQueueMaxAgeS` (10) s of theatre time (round 7: a stale head leaves the queue at once; the time a priority lesson holds the screen does not age the hints behind it), cleared on `level:start` and whenever the state leaves playing / paused (quit, results, menus); the boss fight's lessons are dropped at `boss:dead`. A boss phase's lesson (`boss.<arch>.<n>`) drops the same archetype's earlier phase lesson still waiting (a fast fight reached phase 3 before phase 2's was shown; unshown, it stays unmarked). Round 7 (teaching:0): a hint stays max(`hintSeconds` 3.0, `hintBaseSeconds` 1.0 + `hintPerWordSeconds` 0.3 × words) ≤ `hintMaxSeconds` 5.5 s of theatre time (the theatre clock: the engine's frame step, paused time excluded); a held hint (`hold` / `until`) stays max(that, `hold`) ≤ `hintHoldMaxSeconds` 12 s or until its `hint:release`; a waiting hint takes over after `hintMinSeconds` (2.5 s; round 3: 1.5) on screen — a held one after its hold, a priority one never; r7 fix: a held hint whose hold is nearly spent when its turn comes (hold − time queued < `hintMinSeconds`; not a cut one coming back) is dropped unshown and unmarked (`_nextHint`): it would flash and be released at once; a priority hint (a boss attack's lesson) is queued ahead of the others and cuts a normal hint on screen to `hintCutSeconds` 0.15 s, and the cut hint comes back first (silently) unless it had been read. ui calls `save.markHint(id)` once a hint has been on screen `JUICE.ui.hintMarkSeconds` (1.5 s; round 3: at display), so a lesson cut early, dropped or shown behind a menu comes back — r7 final fix (lead decision 5): and only while `_attended()`, i.e. `engine.inputIdleS()` ≤ `JUICE.ui.hintIdleS` (20 s of real time since the last trusted pointer press / steering move, key, gamepad stick or button; no engine → attended), so a lesson shown to an idle screen stays unread (the `_hintMarked` flag still stops a second mark this showing; a later run shows it again); the boss name card's first-time hold (`bosscard.<arch>`) follows the same rule, and level's `_onItemPickup` marks the pickup lesson a NEW card stood in for only while `inputIdleS()` ≤ `hintIdleS` (`seen:<id>` is unaffected; `read:<id>` is written only by closing the guide); a steer hint hidden because the player steered counts as read, and so — r7 fix round 2, `_hintActed` — does 'fire' at `weapon:start` (only a FIRE press starts a weapon): waiting, it leaves the queue unshown; on screen, it leaves at once (it waited 2.5 s behind ×3 and then asked for the press that had already started the shotgun). Each first showing plays `ui_tap`; `#hint[data-tone]` = warn (red rim: red, single, lesser, bite, runner, crawler, brute, spitter, `dodge.*`, `boss.<arch>.<n>`, boss.stomp) / gain (green: more, x2, div, tryx2) / info (no rim); the first time ever 'red', 'single' or 'div' is on screen, `fx.slowmo(JUICE.slowmo.lesson)` (0.45 for 1.2 s); a hint bound to a gate event calls `ctx.gates.pulseEvent(eventId, seconds)` while it is up (and `0` when it leaves; WP-C's API — the typeof guard went at the round-7 integration, both sides being in the build). Text ≥ 20 css px (`max(21px × --ui, 20px)`; landscape ≥ 900 px wide `max(24px × --ui, 22px)`), panel alpha 0.86. The hand icon (steer, shoot) sways like a drag for both (round-3 FTUE). UI-18: bottom-anchored pill (safe-bottom + `JUICE.ui.hintBottomPx`), no layout read in the frame; on wide boxes it slides sideways (`translate`) off the projected count label using the pill box cached by a ResizeObserver. Round 7 integration (merged-HUD audit): on a landscape box ≤ 400 px tall (a landscape phone, a laptop window at ≥ 150 % zoom) the pill rests on the real bottom inset only (`max(6px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom))`, 5u vertical padding) with an 18 px text floor (16 px from 320 px tall down) — a two-line lesson at safe-bottom + 28 covered the count label there with no side free for the slide |

Input: the icon (click), the toggle (`change`, inside the click's / Space's user activation; not a saved setting — no `settings:change`), and F (`_fullscreenKey` in `_key`: not held, no modifier, not in a text field) → `_toggleFullscreen()` (exit when in fullscreen, else `requestFullscreen({ navigationUI: 'hide' })` then `lockPortrait()`); a refusal re-syncs the controls. `fullscreenchange` to not-fullscreen while `playing` → `engine.pause()` (the browser keeps the Esc that exits fullscreen; `pause()` is not a toggle) |

Buttons call systems directly (every click also emits `ui:button { id }` — round 6: except `#btn-fire`, which calls `weapons.requestFire()` on its pointerdown and emits no `ui:button`): PLAY → `level.start(save.data.highestUnlocked)`; level tile → `level.start(n)` (round-5 integration: PLAY, NEXT and the level tiles go through `level.requestStart(n)`, RETRY / REPLAY / RESTART through `level.requestRestart()` — an authored or memoized script starts at once; a miss generates in frame slices first, and the tapped button wears `.starting` (round 7, flow-coins:3: css draws a spinner — `.btn.starting::after` inside the right edge, `.tile.starting::before` a corner badge, a still ring under reduced motion — and ui sets `aria-busy="true"`) until the next screen change; a build without `requestStart` falls back to `start()`); SHOP → `state.set('shop')`; LEVELS → `state.set('levels')`; BACK (`data-back`) → if the settings overlay is open: close it (`ui.showScreen(mapping of state.current)`), else `state.set(state.prev ∈ {title, complete, gameover} ? state.prev : 'title')`; SETTINGS (title or pause) → `ui.showScreen('settings')` (overlay, no state change); ENDLESS → `level.startEndless()`; RESUME → `engine.resume()`; RESTART → `level.restart()`; QUIT / HOME (`btn-complete-home`, `btn-home`, `btn-endless-home`: the results screens' top-left house icon, round 4) → `level.quitToTitle()` (a HOME during the results theatre commits through quitToTitle's `_commitPending`, nothing is lost); NEXT → `level.start(level + 1)` for every level (beyond `LEVELS.count` the curve uses L = 30 and seed `1000 + L`, GDD §10); REPLAY → `level.restart()`; RETRY → `level.restart()` (endless: `level.startEndless()`); settings controls → `save.setSetting(key, value)` then `events.emit('settings:change', { key, value })` (round 7: except the Fullscreen toggle, which is not a setting — it calls `ui._toggleFullscreen()`, see the `#row-fullscreen` row above) — engine listens for key `quality` (`setQuality`), audio for `sound`/`music` (`setVolumes`), render for `hcGates`/`reducedMotion`, fx for `haptics`/`reducedMotion`, ui itself for `largeText`/`reducedMotion` (`<html>` classes); no direct `engine.setQuality`/`audio.setVolumes` calls from ui. RESET SAVE → `ui.confirmReset()` ("Tap again to confirm" for 3 s; a confirming tap within `JUICE.ui.confirmMinS` (0.6 s) of arming is ignored so a double-tap / double-click never wipes the save, round-2 QA UI-20; leaving Settings disarms; then `save.reset()` + `location.reload()` guarded). Fonts: `<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">`, `<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>`, `<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Lilita+One&family=Nunito:wght@600;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">` in both shells.
