## 11. Audio contract

`audio.play(name, { volume = 1, pitch = 1, pos?, detune? })`: `pitch` multiplies the recipe's base frequency (and `playbackRate` for noise buffers); `pos.x` pans ±`MUSIC.pan.maxPan` by road x and attenuates by |z| (`MUSIC.pan.attenDbAt40m`); cues before `unlocked` are dropped; cues are queued by listeners and started in `update()` (step G). Graph: cue → bus gain (`MUSIC.buses`: `sfx` −6 dB, `gun` −10 dB with a `MUSIC.gunVoices` 12-voice limiter per second, `music` −8 dB) → master `DynamicsCompressor` (`MUSIC.compressor`: −18 dB, ratio 6, attack 3 ms, release 120 ms) → master gain (mute) → destination. Round-2 amendments (AUD-5/6/8/10/12, = GDD §13): buses sfx −12 / gun −16 / music −14 dB; the gun bus passes a `_gunDuck` gain; compressor knee 6 → safety limiter (`MUSIC.limiter`) → soft clip WaveShaper → master; same-frame duplicates of `ugh / thud / roar / pop / screech / hup` merge (`coalesceMs`, `stackDb`); the context and graph are built in `init()` at boot (unlock only resumes); round-3 AUD-10: a post-compressor `reward` bus (`MUSIC.buses.reward`, cues with `bus: 'reward'`) into the limiter and a post-compressor bed duck (`MUSIC.duck.bedOnReward`) on a positive `gate:pass`; chorus voices with `voices.spread` get one StereoPanner each (√2 pan-law compensation); every positional cue passes its StereoPanner (x = 0 included, so the pan law is the same everywhere); round 4: `flip` and `sting_*` on the reward bus with the bed duck (`noAtten` on `flip`), `MUSIC.duck.rewardStack`, linear faders, the first-use warm-up / WaveShaper pool / deferred delayed cues (§6.13); `level:start`, `boss:fight` and menu entry restart the music loop at bar 0, every other mode switch lands on the next beat; `boss:dead` holds a −10 dB dip then fades to 'none'. Noise sources are three pre-rendered 1 s `AudioBuffer`s (white, brown, band-passed) created once at unlock; every cue is built from `OscillatorNode`/`AudioBufferSourceNode` + `GainNode` envelopes + at most one `BiquadFilterNode`, scheduled with `AudioContext.currentTime`, and nodes are released by `onended`. Unlock: owned by `engine` (§6.2) — `pointerdown`, `pointerup`, `touchend`, `click`, `keydown` capture listeners on `#app` call `audio.unlock()` synchronously; `unlock()` creates the context once (`new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)()` in try/catch), calls `resume()` on every invocation and emits `audio:unlocked` only when `state === 'running'`; a `statechange` to `suspended`/`interrupted` (iOS calls/Siri) sets `needsGesture` so engine re-arms the listeners; `visibilitychange` hidden → `suspend()`, visible → `resume()` attempt (500 ms check). The music scheduler runs from `setInterval(MUSIC.schedulerMs)` with `MUSIC.lookaheadMs` lookahead, independent of rAF.

Cue list (recipes in GDD §13, emitted by `audio` on these events): `shot` ← `shot` (voice limiter, volume +1 dB per 8 shooters, cap +12 dB); `thip` ← `zombie:hit` (not killed, ≤ 20/s); `tick` ← `gate:hit` (coalesced ≤ 1 per `JUICE.caps.tickCoalesceMs` per gate, pitch from value − base); `max` ← `gate:max`; `flip` ← `gate:flip`; `whomp` ← `gate:step`; `pass_up` + `hup` ← `gate:pass` (after > before) / `pass_down` ← `gate:pass` (after < before); `crunch` ← `zombie:die` (shared instance per 50 ms, +3 dB per extra kill, cap +9 dB); `ugh` ← `squad:loss`; `roar` + `thud` ← `zombie:telegraph` ring + `zombie:bite` brute; `screech` ← `zombie:telegraph` screech; `splat` ← `spit:impact`; `pop` + `jingle` ← `crate:open`; `sting_*` ← `weapon:start`; `ticktock` ← weapon last 2 s (audio polls `weapons.weaponTimeLeft` in `update`; gear with `Infinity` never ticks); `rocket` ← `weapon:fire { id:'weapon.rocket' }`; `rail` ← `weapon:fire { id:'weapon.railgun' }`; `crack` ← `weapon:fire { id:'troop.sniper' }`; `crash` ← `boss:phase`; `warn` ← `boss:attack` kinds slam / charge / lane / car / stomp at the telegraph START (round 7, r7 teaching:3: the wind-up; the slam / stomp `thud` still lands after `delay`); `heal` ← `squad:count` reason `'medic'` with delta > 0 (round 7, r7 teaching:6: the medic's tick is heard); `roar` (rate 0.4) + `fanfare` ← `boss:dead`; `chime3` ← `level:complete`; `drone_down` ← `level:failed` and `endless:end`; `coin` ← `coin:pickup` (semitone ladder within `MUSIC.coinLadderMs`); `ui_tap` ← `ui:button`; `ui_buy` ← `shop:buy`; `ui_star` ← stars (ui calls `play` directly). Music: `setMusic('menu')` on `state:change` to title/shop/levels, `'level'` on `level:start`, `'runup'` on `level:runup`, `'boss'` on `boss:fight`, `'none'` on `level:complete`/`level:failed`/`endless:end` after the fanfare; `duck(MUSIC.duck.gate)` on `gate:pass`, `duck(MUSIC.duck.bossDead)` on `boss:dead`; `duck(MUSIC.duck.pause, 0)` while paused (called by `engine.pause()`, restored by `resume()` and `level.start()`). Settings (via `settings:change`): `sound` 0–100 → sfx/gun/reward bus gain, `music` 0–100 → music bus (both linear in amplitude since round 4: 50 = −6 dB; round 5: the reward bus is lifted toward the music fader while music > sound, ≤ `MUSIC.rewardLift.maxDb`; music defaults to 70), `M` key → `setMuted`. Round 5: `prerender` cues and the pad chords play pre-rendered buffers (§6.13).

---

## 12. Save schema (`gaterush.v1` in localStorage, JSON)

```js
{ v: 1, createdAt: <ms>, updatedAt: <ms>,
  coins: 0,
  upgrades: { squad: 0, fireRate: 0, damage: 0, coinMult: 0, bossDamage: 0 },        // ranks 0..10
  highestUnlocked: 1,                                                                 // highest playable level
  levels: { /* "1": { stars: 0..3, bestSurvivors: n, bestScore: n, bestCoins: n, clears: n } */ },
  endless: { bestDistance: 0, bestScore: 0, runs: 0 },
  hints: { /* id: true */ },
  settings: { sound: 100, music: 70 /* round 5 lead decision (was 80) */, haptics: true, quality: 'auto', reducedMotion: null, hcGates: false, largeText: false, sensitivity: 1 },  // reducedMotion null = follow the media query
  stats: { runs: 0, kills: 0, gatesPassed: 0, coinsEarned: 0, playSeconds: 0 },
  lastRunIncome: 0 }
```

Defaults are the object above. `load()`: `storage.get` → `JSON.parse` in try/catch → if missing or unparsable use defaults; if `v < 1` or fields are missing run `migrate(data)` which fills missing keys from defaults (additive migration; a future `v: 2` adds a case in `MIGRATIONS[1]`); never throws. `flush()` is debounced 250 ms and also called on `visibilitychange` (hidden) and `pagehide`; writes are `storage.set` (guarded; a failed write keeps the in-memory copy and sets `save.volatile = true`, shown as a one-time caption "Progress won't be saved in this browser"). `profile()` = `{ startSquad: UPGRADES.squad.effect(r), fireRate: …, damage: …, coinMult: …, bossDamage: …, ranks: {...} }`.

---

## 13. Debug and testing hooks

- `window.__gateRushDiag()` (round 7, always installed by `engine.init`): returns — and prints to the console — a multi-line report: version, state, visible screens, visibility / focus, loop and context state, quality (tier, auto, ceiling, detected tier, software GL, render scale, DPR, canvas size, sustained frame time), the GPU string, then the last `QUALITY_TIERS.diag.entries` (64) events oldest first, in seconds since page load: state changes, the visible `#ui .screen` ids 300 ms after each state change, frame gaps > 1 s while the page stayed visible, a watchdog line (1 s interval) when no rAF frame ran for > 2 s while visible, every window `pointerdown` (capture: target `tag#id.class` + pointer type + state), errors (bus `error`, window `error` incl. load errors, unhandled rejections, a frame error), context lost / restored / give-up, quality and render-scale changes, lite menus, pause / resume, blur / focus (and the focus poll), hidden / visible. The ring is preallocated and written only on events, never per frame. The `?debug=1` overlay shows the newest `diag.overlayLines` (4). Testers run it in the console when a screen freezes (the round-7 results freeze was not reproducible without it).
- `window.__game = ctx` (always). `ctx.engine`, `ctx.level`, `ctx.squad` etc. are callable from the console; `ctx.squad.setCount(400, 'debug')`, `ctx.level.start(7)`, `ctx.boss.skipIntro()`.
- URL params (parsed in `main.js`): `?debug=1` (overlay + histograms + listener error reporting), `?level=N` (start that level at boot, unlocks it for the session only), `?seed=N` (overrides the run seed), `?quality=low|med|high` (manual tier), `?nomusic=1`, `?reset=1` (wipe save at boot), `?endless=1`.
- Debug overlay (`#debug`, 4 Hz): fps, refresh interval, JS ms (rolling 60), `renderer.info.render.calls` split main / shadow / post, triangles, tier (+ maxAutoTier; round 7: ' sw' on software GL), DPR, render scale ('rscale'), the sustained frame time and the dt clamp it gives, ' lite' after the state while a menu's scene is frozen, the newest diagnostics lines (round 7), counts, dropped-pool counters, time scale, and histograms from `ctx.debug.histograms`: `hitsPerGate`, `finalVsBase`, `lostPerWave`, `bossTtk`, `countAtBoss`; red class when main-pass calls > 50 (or total > 84) or JS > 7.5 ms.
- Seeded RNG: `util.Rng`; `ctx.rng` is created by `level.start()` from the run seed and is the only source of gameplay randomness (gate generation, wave layouts, zombie phases, spit timing, pickups). `ctx.rngFx` is for cosmetics. `Math.random` is banned in `js/` except inside `util.js` for the endless default seed. Prop placement uses `mulberry32(levelSeed·1000 + segmentIndex)` locally in `Environment`.
- `tools/validate-levels.js` runs `generateLevel` + `validateLevel` for L2–L30 and prints the E-trace; CI-style exit code 1 on errors. Endless (round 5d): chunks D1–30 (`--chunks N`) each on their own and concatenated as one run — mini-boss cadence, cross-chunk gate spacing, determinism, and the zombie pools (each type's live count, spawn → contact over waves and guards, within its pool); the endless table prints per chunk the wave count (the GDD formula's count in brackets when the pool-safe ramp added waves), the population per wave (the owed population in brackets), the horde delivered / owed, the peak pool use per type, the per-zombie HP cap and the largest capped / uncapped zombie.

---

## 14. Coding rules and definition of done

Rules (reviewers reject violations):

1. ES2022 modules, no bundler, no TypeScript, no decorators; one class or one cohesive set of exports per file; imports only from `three`, `three/addons/*`, `./util.js`, `./data.js` and (for factories) `./render.js`/`./models.js`.
2. Zero per-frame allocation in `step`/`render`/`update`: no `new`, no array literals, no closures, no spread, no `Array.prototype.map/filter/forEach`, no string concatenation except when a displayed value changes; use module-level scratch (`scratch.v3a` …) and typed-array pools with swap-remove.
3. Pools are allocated in `init()` at the data.js capacity and never grown; a full pool drops the newest request and increments `ctx.debug.dropped[name]`. Every tier-dependent pool is allocated ONCE at the HIGH-tier capacity (particles 1024 → 512 per InstancedMesh, tracers 256, dust 300, blob shadows 567, HP bars 260); the current tier only sets the *active* limit (`ctx.quality.params.particles`, `.tracers`, `.dust` …) that `emit`/`render` respect via `mesh.count`. A later raise (auto or `?quality=high` after a Low boot) therefore never needs a reallocation. Memory cost ≤ 300 KB total.
4. Every `localStorage` (the getter itself), `navigator.vibrate`, `AudioContext`, `document.fonts`, `requestFullscreen`, `document.fullscreenEnabled`, `getCoalescedEvents`, `setPointerCapture`, `deviceMemory`, `WEBGL_debug_renderer_info`, `navigator.getGamepads`, `screen.orientation`, `matchMedia`, `ResizeObserver`, `visualViewport` access is guarded (`try/catch` or feature test); the game must play with all of them missing.
5. No `console.log` in committed code; `console.warn` only through `util.warnOnce(key, msg)` — round 7: the one direct console call is `console.info` in `engine.diagText(true)`, the dump the user asks for with `window.__gateRushDiag()` (tools/check-imports.js sanctions exactly that one call, `CONSOLE_SANCTIONED`, and still warns on any other); errors surface through `ctx.ui.showError` (non-fatal: toast-style box that auto-hides after 6 s; fatal: box with RELOAD). `window.onerror`/`unhandledrejection` are installed at the top of `main.js`; in the artifact shell they also write into `#error-box` so a blank page never happens.
6. Naming: files lower-case, classes `PascalCase`, methods/fields `camelCase`, constants `UPPER_SNAKE` only in data.js, private fields prefixed `_`, event names `noun:verb`, data ids `category.key`.
7. Hot-path `InstancedMesh` rules: `count = active`, one `needsUpdate` per attribute per frame with `addUpdateRange`, `frustumCulled = false`, `DynamicDrawUsage`.
8. Every system exposes `init/reset/dispose`; `reset()` must be idempotent and allocation-free; `dispose()` must remove every listener it added.
9. Reduced motion, haptics and sound settings are honoured by the module that produces the effect (fx/audio), never by the emitter.
10. Numbers come from `data.js`; a literal number in a gameplay module needs a comment naming the data key it derives from or it is rejected.
11. (round-2 cross-group, V8 hygiene) A field of a long-lived object that will ever hold a fraction is CREATED holding a Double: seed it from a fraction and then set its start value (`this.f = 0.5; this.f = 0;`, or `-0` in a module-level event payload whose fields are always written before an emit). The first fractional store into a field created from a Smi (0, 1, −1) generalises the field, deprecates the object's map (shared with every object literal of the same shape, across modules) and deoptimises every optimised function that touched it, which then runs boxing baseline code for seconds. Never add properties to an object after construction in a hot path (declare every field in the constructor / literal), never write ad-hoc properties onto three.js objects (e.g. a stamp on a material), never make a scene object a prototype (`Object.create(mesh)`), and use integer `renderOrder`s. `docs/build/qa2/cross-tools/deoptab.mjs` + `mapgen.cjs` measure it (V8 `--log-deopt --log-maps`).

Definition of done (per module, checked by the integrator):

- [ ] Exports exactly the names in §6; signatures match; `init()` returns `this`.
- [ ] Runs with every other system stubbed (`ctx.<system>` missing → the module guards with `?.` only in non-hot paths, or the stub harness provides no-op systems).
- [ ] Zero allocations per frame: Chrome Performance "Allocation sampling" shows a flat heap over 30 s of play.
- [ ] `?debug=1` draw calls within budget for the module's meshes; `renderer.info` numbers documented in the PR.
- [ ] `reset()` twice in a row leaves no visible instance and no running timer.
- [ ] Every event in §5 the module emits carries exactly the documented fields; every event it listens to is listed in its section.
- [ ] Works in `artifact.html` (no SW, no `confirm`, storage throwing) and in `index.html` as a PWA.
- [ ] Touched by `tools/validate-levels.js` where applicable (level/levelgen/data).
- [ ] No `console.log`, no `Math.random` (except util), no literal tuning numbers.

Suggested engineer split (9): E1 main/engine/util/save + tools/serve + build-artifact; E2 data/levelgen/level + validate-levels; E3 render; E4 models; E5 squad/troops; E6 zombies/boss; E7 gates/pickups/weapons; E8 fx/audio; E9 ui/css/manifest/sw/make-icons. Integration order: E1 + E3 + E4 first (a scrolling road with an idle squad), then E5/E7, then E6, then E8/E9/E2 content.

---

## 15. Shells and tools

### 15.1 `index.html` (standalone PWA)

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, maximum-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover, user-scalable=no">
<meta name="theme-color" content="#0F1626">
<meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-capable" content="yes"><meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style" content="black-translucent">
<title>Gate Rush</title>
<link rel="manifest" href="manifest.webmanifest">
<link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="icons/apple-touch-icon-180.png">
<link rel="icon" href="icons/icon-192.png">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com"><link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Lilita+One&family=Nunito:wght@600;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="css/game.css">
<script type="importmap">{ "imports": { "three": "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.170.0/build/three.module.js", "three/addons/": "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.170.0/examples/jsm/" } }</script>
</head>
<body>
<!-- BEGIN APP --> <div id="app" data-shell="index" tabindex="-1"> … §10.1 … </div> <!-- END APP -->
<!-- BEGIN BOOTGUARD --> <script>
  window.addEventListener('error', function (e) { var b = document.getElementById('error-box'); if (b) { b.hidden = false; document.getElementById('error-text').textContent = (e.message || 'Load error'); } });
  if (!window.HTMLScriptElement || !HTMLScriptElement.supports || !HTMLScriptElement.supports('importmap')) { var b = document.getElementById('error-box'); if (b) { b.hidden = false; document.getElementById('error-text').textContent = 'This browser cannot load the game (no import maps).'; } }
</script> <!-- END BOOTGUARD -->
<script type="module" src="js/main.js"></script>
<script>if ('serviceWorker' in navigator && location.protocol !== 'file:') { try { navigator.serviceWorker.register('sw.js').catch(function(){}); } catch (e) {} }</script>
</body>
</html>
```

The inline classic BOOTGUARD script runs before the module script, so a bare-specifier failure (`import 'three'` with no honoured import map — hosts that already carry one, or browsers before import-map support) surfaces in `#error-box` instead of a blank page; `main.js` replaces this handler with its own `window.onerror` when it boots (§4.1 step 0).

**Update heal (1.2.0 release, index.html only).** A second inline script after `<!-- END BOOTGUARD -->` (so `tools/build-artifact.js` never copies it; the artifact shell has no service worker) defines `window.__gateRushHeal(message, show)`; the BOOTGUARD's two load-error listeners call it before showing `#error-box`. When a load error hits a page that a service worker controls and the registration has a newer worker installing or waiting, the heal writes "Finishing the update…" into `#boot-text`, waits for that worker to reach `installed` (or triggers `registration.update()` when none is pending), posts `{ type: 'SKIP_WAITING' }` (§15.4) and reloads on `controllerchange`, so the next document runs the new worker's one complete build. Once per session (`sessionStorage['gaterush.heal']`, else once per page); no controller (first visit), no newer worker after the update check, a redundant worker, or 60 s without a takeover → the plain error box with RELOAD, as before; the boot watchdog's stall message waits while a heal runs. Why: the 1.1.0 worker (sw.js v3) served code network-first with a 3 s budget per file, so the first launch after the 1.2.0 update on a slow network could link a 1.2.0 module against the cached 1.1.0 `data.js` (`SyntaxError: … does not provide an export named 'DISTRICTS'`; docs/build/r5-release.md §5). Any future `sw.js` must keep answering `SKIP_WAITING`.

### 15.2 `artifact.html`

No `<!doctype>`, `<html>`, `<head>`, `<body>`; no manifest, icons, theme-color or SW script. Contents, in order: the two font `<link rel="preconnect">` tags, the Google Fonts stylesheet link, `<link rel="stylesheet" href="css/game.css">`, the same import map `<script type="importmap">`, the same `<link rel="modulepreload">` tags as index.html (round-2 UI-29; copied AFTER the import map by `tools/build-artifact.js`, because Chrome rejects an import map once a module preload has started), the `#app` block with `data-shell="artifact"`, the same BOOTGUARD inline script, `<script type="module" src="js/main.js"></script>`. The host skeleton owns `<html>`; nothing reads `documentElement.dataset` — `main.js` reads `#app`'s `data-shell` into `ctx.config.isArtifact` and skips fullscreen offers and SW logic. Both shells reference the same `css/` and `js/` paths relative to the document.

### 15.3 Tools (node ≥ 18, zero dependencies)

- `tools/build-artifact.js`: reads `index.html`, extracts everything between `<!-- BEGIN APP -->` and `<!-- END APP -->` and between `<!-- BEGIN BOOTGUARD -->` and `<!-- END BOOTGUARD -->`, prepends the font links, the stylesheet link and the import map (copied verbatim from the `<head>` by matching the tag), appends the bootguard and the module script tag, replaces `data-shell="index"` with `data-shell="artifact"`, writes `artifact.html`. Fails loudly if a marker is missing, and fails if any file in `js/` is missing from `sw.js`'s `PRECACHE` list (§15.4).
- `tools/make-icons.js`: draws the icon programmatically into an RGBA buffer (rounded square #0F1626, orange gate frame #FFB02E, white "+" glyph with a dark outline, teal shadow), encodes PNG with `zlib.deflateSync` (filter 0 per scanline, CRC32 implemented inline) at 512, 192 and 180 px → `icons/icon-512.png`, `icons/icon-192.png`, `icons/apple-touch-icon-180.png`.
- `tools/serve.js`: `http.createServer` static server rooted at the repo (`--port 8080`, `--root .`), MIME map `{ '.html': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', '.js': 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8', '.mjs': 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8', '.css': 'text/css; charset=utf-8', '.json': 'application/json', '.webmanifest': 'application/manifest+json', '.png': 'image/png', '.svg': 'image/svg+xml', '.ico': 'image/x-icon', '.woff2': 'font/woff2' }`, `Cache-Control: no-store`, directory → `index.html`, path traversal rejected, 404 otherwise.
- `tools/validate-levels.js`: `import { generateLevel, validateLevel } from '../js/levelgen.js'`; prints a table per level (gates, waves, E-trace, warnings); exit 1 on any error.
- `tools/check-codex.js` (round 7, teaching:5): imports `../js/data.js`; checks that every `CODEX` id is unique and has its `STRINGS.codex.entries` copy (a card line for `CODEX.cards`), that every grantable item, zombie type and boss archetype has an entry, that every unlock level and legacy hint id is valid, that the copy and the number-quoting lessons (`HINTS.crawler`, the medic and boss-phase hints) quote the CURRENT table values (durations, bonuses, the slam radius, shield hits, intervals …), and that no text reads `undefined` / `NaN`; exit 1 on any error. Run it with check-imports after any data.js retune.
- `tools/package.js [--check]`: rebuilds `artifact.html`, then writes `dist/` (the two shells, manifest, `sw.js`, `css/`, `js/`, `icons/`) for a static HTTPS host; `--check` fails unless every file `sw.js` names exists in `dist/` and every `js/` file is in its `PRECACHE`, and prints the cache `VERSION` (§15.4). Round 5 (r5-ui-sw needsOtherGroup 4): `dist/sw.js` ends with a `// build <sha-256>` stamp of every shipped file (sw.js without its stamp included), and the build stops before touching `dist/` when the previous `dist/sw.js` has the same `VERSION` and another stamp — the §15.4 release rule (bump `VERSION`), enforced; delete `dist/` when the previous build never shipped.
- `tools/smoke.js` (puppeteer-core, headless Chrome on the real GPU; the usage is in its header): boot / ui / play / all scenarios in the index, artifact and sandboxed-iframe shells; a JSON report with page errors, console errors and warnings, failed requests, a state timeline, frame stats and screenshots; exit 1 on a boot failure, a page or console error, or a failed harness check. Round 4: `--scenario all` ends with a real tap (touch, or mouse with `--desktop`) on the results screen's HOME icon and expects the title (`report.home`). Round 5 (integration): `--level N` starts the level the way the menus do (`level.requestStart`, waiting for the run); every timeline sample carries `district`, `callsMain` / `callsShadow` / `callsPost` (render.callStats — the §9.1 budget split; `calls` stays the frame total) and `programs`; the report adds `callsMax` (per-frame maxima per district), `callsPeak` (the objects drawn in each district's peak main-pass frame), `programs` (min / max), `districts` (every `district:change` with the worst frame over its fade + 1 s), `startFrames` (the level-start frame and the first 2 s, the start frame included) and four screenshots across every endless rotation's cross-fade.

### 15.4 `manifest.webmanifest` and `sw.js`

Manifest: `{ "name": "Gate Rush", "short_name": "Gate Rush", "start_url": "./index.html", "display": "standalone", "orientation": "portrait", "background_color": "#0F1626", "theme_color": "#0F1626", "icons": [ { "src": "icons/icon-192.png", "sizes": "192x192", "type": "image/png" }, { "src": "icons/icon-512.png", "sizes": "512x512", "type": "image/png", "purpose": "any maskable" } ] }`. Service worker (`sw.js`, round 5, `VERSION = 'v4'`, `'v5'` since the round-5b build (docs/build/r5b-verify.md), `'v6'` for release 1.2.1 (docs/build/r5d-release.md), `'v7'` for release 1.2.2, the round-6 build (docs/build/r6-verify.md, docs/build/r6-release.md), `'v8'` for release 1.3.0, the round-7 build; caches `gaterush-<VERSION>` (app) and `gaterush-cdn-<VERSION>` (CDN)): **one consistent build per launch** (r4-release §7 item 4 — the 1.1.0 worker served code network-first, so a slow first launch after a release could mix files of two builds). `install` pre-caches the whole explicit `PRECACHE` list (service workers cannot glob; `["./", "./index.html", "./css/game.css", "./manifest.webmanifest", the six icons, every js/*.js]`) with `cache: 'reload'`; **every file is mandatory** (one failure deletes the half-filled cache and fails the install, so the old worker keeps serving its complete build and the browser retries later); the CDN cache copies the previous version's CDN entries forward (three r170 is pinned, font URLs are immutable) and fetches only the missing ones from `CDN_PRECACHE` (three.js module graph + the Google Fonts CSS; best effort). **No `skipWaiting()` at install**: the new worker waits while any page of the old build is open and takes over (a) at the next launch after every old page closed, or (b) when the page posts `{ type: 'SKIP_WAITING' }` (the title's "Update ready — tap to reload" pill, §10.3), after which `activate` claims the page and ui reloads it on `controllerchange`. `fetch` (GET only), same origin: a PRECACHE file (path match, no query) → the active version's cache (cache-first; a file the cache lost comes from the network and is not written back); a navigation to a precached path (`/`, `/index.html`, any query) → the cached shell (so `?level=` / `?debug=1` launch offline); any other navigation → the network, the cached shell offline; any other same-origin file → the network with the cache as offline fallback. `cdn.jsdelivr.net` / `fonts.googleapis.com` / `fonts.gstatic.com` → stale-while-revalidate into the CDN cache; any other origin → the network. `activate` deletes every other `gaterush-*` cache, disables navigation preload and claims open clients. The artifact shell never registers it. **Release rule:** every release must change `sw.js` (bump `VERSION`): with code served cache-first, a release whose `sw.js` is byte-identical never reaches an installed player. `tools/package.js` enforces it (round 5): `dist/sw.js` carries a build stamp of every shipped file, and a changed build under an unchanged `VERSION` stops the packaging (it would install into the cache the running build serves from). Verified with two marked builds on one origin (docs/build/r5-ui-sw.md): the first launch after a release still runs the old build in full, the new one installs and waits, the pill swaps it in with a reload, and a fresh launch with no old page open starts on the new build. **The 1.1.0 → 1.2.0 transition (v3 → v4)** is the one update that still runs its first launch through the old network-first worker (docs/build/r5-release.md §5, the shipped 1.1.0 dist against the 1.2.0 dist on one origin): on a fast network that launch is one consistent 1.2.0 build and v4 installs and waits (the pill shows, although the page already runs 1.2.0); on a slow network it can mix the two builds for that one launch — a mix that links boots and plays (six representative mixes ran boot, menus, L1 and HOME clean), and a 1.2.0 module against the cached 1.1.0 `data.js` fails to link and is recovered by index.html's update heal (§15.1). From v4 on, a launch never mixes.

---

## 16. Decisions (where two review findings conflicted, the simpler and more explicit resolution won)

| # | Topic | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| D1 | Coin accounting | `coin:pickup` is the only income path; `level._addCoins` is private and called solely from that listener. `zombie:die` and `boss:dead` carry NO coin field (one source of truth) rather than an "informational" copy. Sources: `kill, pile, crate, gate, max, boss, duplicate, refund, bonus`; a magnet re-pickup pays with source `duplicate` (not `crate`). `coinMult` applies to `kill` and `boss` only; replay/endless ×0.6 applies to every source; level/star bonus is credited as source `bonus` without `coinMult`. |
| D2 | Steering integration | Engine integrates `steerDx·k` + keyboard/gamepad ONCE per frame in `beginFrame` into the engine-owned `ctx.input.targetX`; squad only reads it and clamps locally while lane-locked. (The alternative — squad integrating in `step()` — would apply a frame's drag 0 or 5 times — up to 12 since round 7.) |
| D3 | Count label | fx owns the label and drives it from events; the badge/roll-up is keyed off `gate:pass` (which carries `op`), and `squad:count` with reason `'gate'` is ignored by the label. `gate:commit`-based badge timing was dropped; the x3 badge shows for `JUICE.label.multShow` (0.3 s) and the tween follows. Squad only calls `setWorldPos`. |
| D4 | Lane highlight decal | Single owner = gates, rendered through `fx.decal('lane')` + `fx.moveDecal`; squad's decal mesh is deleted. |
| D5 | Gate panel materials | Instanced panels (1 ShaderMaterial, per-instance `iPanel`/`iScale`) instead of 12 materials: per-gate flash/tint without shared uniforms AND 4 draws for all gates. `MaterialLibrary.gatePanel()` returns the single instance. |
| D6 | Zombie / gate / crate ids | Stable generation-tagged handles `(generation << 12) \| slot` with per-type slot bases (walker 0–119 … spitter 236–259) plus explicit `alive / dying / free` states; dying entries are invisible to every query. Plain slot indices were rejected because a slot can be reused while a caller still holds the id. |
| D7 | `waveHz` | A method `level.waveHz()` (hz of the most recently spawned wave; run-up hz during the boss); no mirror field is required for callers (`run.waveHz` is kept for stats only). |
| D8 | Boss minion HP | Level computes `minionHp` once (union of all `spawn` actions in the resolved archetype + the walker burst) with `solveWaveHp(script.eBoss, roadSpeedBase, unionUnits, bandMid)` and passes it in the spawn spec; the boss never imports levelgen or reads the script. Authored `minionHp` on the boss event overrides. |
| D9 | Boss HP multipliers | `bossHp = round50(20·eBoss·max(milestoneMult, archetype.hpMult ?? 1))` — Prime's ×1.25 and the milestone ×1.25 never stack, matching the GDD §10 table (L20/L30 = 15 000). |
| D10 | Endless end state | `finish(false)` in endless → `state.set('complete', { endless: true })` → screen `'endless'` and event `endless:end`; `gameover` is campaign-only. |
| D11 | State → screen | `StateMachine.set` emits `state:change` only; ui maps states to screens in its listener. Boss bar / letterbox / name card have no public ui methods (events only, hidden on `level:start`); `render.setLetterbox` deleted. |
| D12 | Paused loop | The rAF loop keeps running on every tier while paused (A, C with `fx.update(0, unscaledDt)`, F, G); the scene is redrawn at ≤ 30 fps on High and only on `render.dirty`/`engine.requestRedraw()` elsewhere; music runs from `setInterval`. |
| D13 | Audio unlock | Engine owns one set of capture listeners on `#app` for `pointerdown`, `pointerup`, `touchend`, `click`, `keydown` (iOS does not treat a touch `pointerdown` as an activation); `audio.init()` registers nothing. |
| D14 | Input listeners | On `#app` (not the canvas) with pointer capture; the UI layer is `pointer-events: none` except controls and modal screens. Round 7 fix: a steering drag starts (and captures the pointer) only while `playing` — a capture on a menu retargeted its click to `#app`, outside ui's listener, so a tap on the results panel never skipped the count-up. |
| D15 | Floaters / numbers | 24 floaters × 4 quads = 96; 32 damage numbers × 4 quads = 128 (not 128 numbers × 4 = 512); GDD §16 pool list updated to "60 gate + 96 floater + 128 damage". Round 7 (WP-C, the "9999" numbers): 5 quads each, 120 / 160. |
| D16 | Engine clock reset | One method name: `engine.resetLevelClock()` (not `resetLevelTime`). Game-over grade: `render.setDesaturate(t01)` (not `setGrade({desat})`). |
| D17 | `steer` hint | Carried by the L1 script row `{ at: 0, type:'hint', id:'steer' }`; level does NOT also show it on `level:start`. `bite` hint = wave `hintOnLoss` ("first `squad:loss` with cause `'bite'` after this wave spawned"), no per-wave zombie bookkeeping. |
| D18 | Soldier limb params | `SQUAD.limb = { uFreq: 2.2, uLegAmp: 0.55, uArmAmp: 0.3, uArmRest: 1.2, uBobAmp: 0.03, uRecoil: 0.06 }` (rifle-raised arms; the two findings differed only in arm values). |
| D19 | Lane caps | Mandatory from the script (`cap` for + / x / ÷, `flipCap` for −, `step` for x / ÷); `gateDefaults` runs in levelgen only; gates never reads `expectedSquad()`. |
| D20 | Camera | Portrait below aspect 0.9; vertical FOV derived from a constant horizontal FOV per preset (portrait 27.05° = 55° on 390×844, landscape 68.6° = 42° at 16:9), clamped. |
| D21 | Shadows | Medium uses `PCFShadowMap` (GDD §16 updated); `shadowMap.enabled` is never toggled, `sun.castShadow` is. |
| D22 | Draw budget | Measured as `shadow + main + post` with `renderer.info.autoReset = false`; the cap is 50 main + 20 shadow + 14 post. |
